# WP3: `@gmt/db`

Revision 4 (2026-10-14): reconciled with spec revision 12, the plan's "Cross-package contracts" (round 4) and main at commit 5a0b72f, and the coordinator's rulings on the first and second core-roster reviews (every finding addressed; section 11 lists what the plan has not yet absorbed).

## 1. Goal

Make `@gmt/db` the only place that touches SQLite: the complete spec §7 schema as one committed Drizzle migration, every repository and type in the plan's "Database" contract behind `createRepos`, the SQL-enforced `BudgetGuard`, the response cache, `app_lock`, the vendored airports and metro-code seed, the pre-migration backup, and the maintenance job, each pinned by the spec §9 item 3 tests.

## 2. Scope

**In**

- Drizzle schema, one file per spec §7 table (18 tables; `providers` is dropped, ruling 11), under `packages/db/src/schema/`, plus `schema/index.ts`.
- The first and only migration, `packages/db/drizzle/0000_initial.sql` with its `meta/`, generated by `drizzle-kit` and committed.
- Every class, type and function in the plan's "Database" block, with the changes in section 5.
- `SqliteBudgetGuard`: every cap predicate in SQL inside `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, the configured per-call rate as the only cost the ledger accepts, the billable rule at finalise, monthly and lifetime windows; per-execution caps from the execution's snapshot budget.
- Stored roll-ups and the within-provider merge computed by core's `rollUp` and `mergeWithinProvider` (WP1) inside each write transaction.
- The synthetic gate and the fixture ⇔ synthetic rule on every write that carries a provider or a source kind (ruling 1), in the repository and, where the row holds both, in SQL.
- `SqliteResponseCache`, `AppLock`, `seedAirports`, `sanityCheckAirports`, `loadAirportsDataset`, `runMaintenance`, `backupDatabase`.
- Vendored data under `packages/db/data/`: `airports.2026-10-01-a89e4e0.json` (mwgg/Airports, MIT, `airports.LICENSE`) and `metros.json` (Wikipedia, CC BY-SA 4.0, `metros.ATTRIBUTION`), and the script that builds both.

**Out**

- `openDatabase`, `Db.transaction`, `Db.immediateTransaction`, `pendingMigrations`, `applyMigrations`, the pragmas: WP0. WP3 adds the first migration and extends WP0's `test/client.test.ts`.
- `rollUp`, `mergeWithinProvider` and their reason rules: WP1. WP3 stores what they return and pins only that the stored value equals `rollUp(current outcomes)`.
- The backup CLI wrapper `scripts/backup.mjs` (WP12) and the boot order that calls `backupDatabase` (WP8, WP4b).
- Heartbeat timers, the 60 s / 90 s wait choices, SIGTERM release: WP8; WP13 tests "tool bootstrap starts no execution".
- Calling `reserve` before every HTTP attempt, cache-key construction and body scrubbing, non-URL credential scrubbing in error messages: WP4. The repositories strip URL query strings and userinfo with WP0's `stripUrlsInText` and refuse non-hex cache keys; a bare `api_key=…` or `Bearer …` outside a URL is WP4's obligation never to put into a message.
- Headroom for `skipped:budget`, pre-start checks, the error table, selection, cabin-not-offered decisions, parity and overrun flags, verdicts: WP7 and WP2.
- Alert evaluation and digests: WP2 and WP11.

## 3. Spec sections

- **§7 entire** (revision 12): every table, column, key, index and CHECK; the transaction units; SQLite settings; idempotent migrations with the pre-migration backup; retention; budget windows; `app_lock`; the airports seed with the 5,000-row sanity check and no network.
- **§5.3 "Cap predicates"**: billed vendors count `outcome IN ('pending','unknown') OR billable = 1`; free vendors count every non-cache row; `global-month` adds each enabled subscription provider's fixed fee once; a cap of 0 refuses when `current + this > 0`, zero-cost calls pass.
- **§5.3 "Billable rule"**: `searchapi` (and `fixture` under `syntheticAllowed`) billable iff HTTP 200; `travelpayouts` never; cache hits never. FX is never ledgered.
- **§5.3 "Crash between reserve and finalise"**: `pending` rows older than 10 minutes become `unknown` and stay counted; every ledger row carries `tile_index` (NULL for verification).
- **§5.5 repository rejections**: synthetic rows refused unless allowed; `fixture` ⇔ `synthetic` both directions, on prices, observations, offers and outcomes.
- **§6.6**: roll-up precedence and the within-provider monotone merge, computed by core and stored.
- **§9 item 3**: the db test list, mapped test by test in section 9.

## 4. Interfaces consumed

From the plan's "Cross-package contracts" (WP0, WP1). Where the plan text is still being updated to a ruling, the ruling's form is shown and marked.

```ts
// @gmt/core types/ids.ts — mint sites (ruling 15: row mappers and vendored data mint brands only through these)
export const iataSchema: z.ZodType<Iata, string>; export const isoDateSchema: z.ZodType<IsoDate, string>; export const isoDateTimeSchema: z.ZodType<IsoDateTime, string>;
export function asIata(s: string): Iata; export function asIsoDate(s: string): IsoDate; export function asIsoDateTime(s: string): IsoDateTime;   // throw on mismatch

// types/provenance.ts
export type ProviderId = 'travelpayouts' | 'searchapi' | 'fixture';
export type SourceKind = 'authorised-cached' | 'scraped-aggregate' | 'scraped-search' | 'synthetic';
export type Cabin = 'economy' | 'premium_economy' | 'business' | 'first';
export type PointOfSale = string | 'unknown';
export type FxInfo = { converted: false } | { converted: true; rateNano: number; rateDate: IsoDate; source: string };
export interface Provenance { providerId: ProviderId; sourceKind: SourceKind; fetchedAt: IsoDateTime; providerObservedAt?: IsoDateTime; maxAgeHours: number | 'unknown'; fx: FxInfo; pointOfSale: PointOfSale; cabin: Cabin }

// types/definition.ts
export interface ResolvedSpace { origins: Iata[]; destinations: Iata[]; departureFrom: IsoDate; departureTo: IsoDate; minNights: number; maxNights: number }
export interface CellKey { origin: Iata; destination: Iata; departureDate: IsoDate; returnDate: IsoDate }
export interface Cell extends CellKey { nights: number }
export function cellKeyString(c: CellKey): string;
export interface ExecutionBudget { maxCalls: number; maxFreeCalls: number; maxCostMicroGbp: number }
export interface SearchDefinition { id: string; name: string; origins: Iata[]; destinations: Iata[]; window: WindowSpec; minNights: number; maxNights: number; cabin: Cabin; constraints: Constraints; verification: VerificationPolicy; budget: ExecutionBudget; providers: ProvidersConfig; destinationNotes: Partial<Record<Iata, string>>; onwardCostPence: Partial<Record<Iata, number>>; scheduleCron: string | null; scheduleTz: string; confirmedCostPence: number | null; enabled: boolean; createdAt: IsoDateTime; updatedAt: IsoDateTime; deletedAt: IsoDateTime | null }
export type DefinitionInput = Omit<SearchDefinition, 'id' | 'confirmedCostPence' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt' | 'deletedAt'>;
export type DefinitionSnapshot = SearchDefinition & { resolved: ResolvedSpace; resolvedAt: IsoDateTime; windowClamped: boolean; droppedAlwaysInclude: CellKey[] };
// Child snapshots inherit the definition budget except maxCalls = CHILD_MAX_CALLS (spec §8.1, decision 14).

// types/execution.ts
export type ExecutionStatus = 'pending' | 'running' | 'paused_budget' | 'paused_error' | 'interrupted' | 'cancelled' | 'completed' | 'skipped_budget' | 'skipped_confirm' | 'skipped_window' | 'skipped_missed';
export const ACTIVE_STATUSES: readonly ExecutionStatus[]; export const RESUMABLE_STATUSES: readonly ExecutionStatus[];
export type Stage = 'discovery' | 'verification';
export type CellOutcomeStatus = 'not_run' | 'priced' | 'empty' | 'no_data' | 'error' | 'skipped';
export type SkipReason = 'not_selected' | 'budget' | 'provider_disabled' | 'departed';
export type NoDataReason = 'cabin-not-offered';
export type ErrorReason = ProviderErrorKind | NormalisationReason;   // the vendor message goes to lastError, never to reason
export interface CellOutcome extends CellKey { executionId: string; stage: Stage; providerId: ProviderId; status: CellOutcomeStatus; reason: string | null; attempts: number; lastError: string | null; fetchedAt: IsoDateTime | null; minPence: number | null; cabin: Cabin | null; pointOfSale: PointOfSale | null; sourceKind: SourceKind | null; reusedFromExecutionId: string | null }
export interface RollUp { status: CellOutcomeStatus | null; reason: string | null; minPence: number | null }
export type SelectionReason = 'always_include' | 'top_n' | 'ceiling' | 'stride';
export interface Execution { id: string; definitionId: string; isChild: boolean; cell: CellKey | null; snapshot: DefinitionSnapshot; status: ExecutionStatus; stage: Stage | null; estimate: Estimate | null; cancelRequested: boolean; resumeAfter: IsoDateTime | null; autoResumeCount: number; startedAt: IsoDateTime | null; finishedAt: IsoDateTime | null; errorText: string | null; createdAt: IsoDateTime; trigger: 'manual' | 'schedule' | 'tool' }
export interface Buckets { priced: number; empty: number; no_data: number; error: number; skipped: number; not_run: number; reused: number }

// types/itinerary.ts
export type SuppliedField = 'segments' | 'segmentTimes' | 'segmentCabins' | 'selfTransfer' | 'baggage' | 'fareConditions' | 'seller' | 'price';
export interface BaggageSnapshot { cabinBag?: 'included' | 'not-included'; checkedBags?: number; notes?: string[] }
export interface FareConditions { refundable?: boolean; changeable?: boolean; notes?: string[] }
export type Booking = { kind: 'get'; url: string; host: string } | { kind: 'post'; url: string; postData: string; host: string } | { kind: 'none' };
export interface Segment { marketingCarrier: string; operatingCarrier?: string; flightNumber: number; from: Iata; to: Iata; departLocal: string; arriveLocal: string; departUtc?: IsoDateTime; arriveUtc?: IsoDateTime; durationMinutes?: number; aircraft?: string }
export interface Layover { at: Iata; minutes: number; overnight: boolean; airportChange: boolean }
export interface NormalisedItinerary { hash: string; origin: Iata; destination: Iata; departureDate: IsoDate; returnDate: IsoDate; nights: number; outbound: Segment[]; inbound: Segment[]; outboundMinutes?: number; inboundMinutes?: number; stopsOut: number; stopsIn: number; layovers: Layover[]; airportChange?: boolean; marketingCarriers: string[] }
export interface Offer { itineraryHash: string; providerId: ProviderId; seller: string; cabin: Cabin }
export interface ObservationFields { pricePence: number; priceNativeMinor: number; currencyNative: string; fx: FxInfo; taxesIncluded: boolean | null; selfTransfer: boolean | null; segmentCabins: Cabin[] | null; baggage: BaggageSnapshot | null; fareConditions: FareConditions | null; booking: Booking; bookingExpiresAt: IsoDateTime | null }
export interface Observation extends ObservationFields { id: number; offerId: number; executionId: string; provenance: Provenance; suppliedFields: SuppliedField[]; partialExpansion: boolean }

// ports
export interface Reservation { id: string; finalise(r: { outcome: 'ok' | 'error' | 'unknown'; httpStatus?: number; durationMs: number }): void }
export interface BudgetGuard {
  reserve(p: { providerId: ProviderId; executionId: string | null; tileIndex: number | null; costMicroGbp: number; httpAttempt: number; endpointPath: string }): Reservation;
  recordCacheHit(p: { providerId: ProviderId; executionId: string | null; tileIndex: number | null; endpointPath: string }): void;
  usage(scope: BudgetScope): Usage;
  recentCalls(providerId: ProviderId, since: IsoDateTime): number;   // upstream attempts since `since`; http-kit seeds its hourly reservoir from it after a restart
}
export type BudgetScope = { kind: 'execution-calls' | 'execution-free-calls' | 'execution-cost'; executionId: string } | { kind: 'provider-month' | 'provider-lifetime'; providerId: ProviderId } | { kind: 'global-month' };
export interface Usage { calls: number; costMicroGbp: number; cap: number | null; windowStart: IsoDateTime | null; windowEnd: IsoDateTime | null }   // cap null for a window the plan does not use
export interface ResponseCache { get(key: string): { body: unknown; storedAt: IsoDateTime } | undefined; set(key: string, body: unknown, ttlSeconds: number, storedAt: IsoDateTime): void }
export interface Logger { debug(o: object, msg?: string): void; info(o: object, msg?: string): void; warn(o: object, msg?: string): void; error(o: object, msg?: string): void; child(bindings: object): Logger }
export interface Clock { now(): IsoDateTime; todayLondon(): IsoDate }
export const systemClock: Clock; export function fixedClock(at: IsoDateTime): Clock & { advance(ms: number): void; set(at: IsoDateTime): void };
export type OperatorMessageKind = 'paused_budget' | 'paused_error' | 'skipped_budget' | 'skipped_confirm' | 'skipped_window' | 'skipped_missed' | 'interrupted' | 'provider_disabled' | 'provider_capped' | 'parity_break' | 'alerts_calendar_only' | 'crash_loop';

// types/alerts.ts
export interface AlertRule { id: string; definitionId: string; type: AlertRuleType; thresholdPence: number | null; pct: number | null; basis: ResultBasis; requireAllVerified: boolean | null; cooldownHours: number; enabled: boolean }
export type AlertRuleInput = Omit<AlertRule, 'id'>;
export interface AlertEvent { id: number; ruleId: string; identityKey: string; observationId: number | null; calendarPriceId: number | null; dedupKey: string; firedAt: IsoDateTime; status: 'sent' | 'failed' | 'suppressed'; reason: string | null; payload: unknown }
export interface PriorFiring { identityKey: string; dedupKey: string; firedAt: IsoDateTime; pricePence: number }

// errors, config, api-contract
// new BudgetExceeded({ scope, cap, current, requested, providerId? }); stripUrlsInText(text): string (URL query and userinfo only)
export interface ProviderLimits { monthlyCallCap: number; window: 'month' | 'lifetime'; billingDay: number; ratePerMinute: number; ratePerHour: number; concurrency: number; cacheTtlHours: number; costPerCallMicroGbp: number; planFixedMicroGbp: number; excludedFromSchedule: boolean }
// Config.spend { monthlyCapMicroGbp; globalBillingDay; …; childMaxCalls }; Config.providers { travelpayouts: { token; limits } | null; searchapi: { key; plan; trialUsed; limits } | null; fixture: { costMicroGbp } | null }
export function parseConfig(env: Record<string, string | undefined>, opts: ParseConfigOptions): ConfigResult;
export function syntheticAllowed(c: Pick<Config, 'nodeEnv' | 'databasePath' | 'allowFixtureProvider' | 'inContainer'>): boolean;
export * as apiContract from './api-contract/index.js';   // schemas live only under this namespace (main 5a0b72f)
// apiContract.definitionShapeSchema   the unrefined definition field shape (no confirmedCostPence): the read-side schema for stored rows and snapshots
// apiContract.definitionCreateRequestSchema   the refined request schema: API request path only (WP8), never used by db reads
// apiContract.estimateSchema, apiContract.bookingSchema   Estimate, Booking
export type { HistoryPoint, RouteSeriesPoint } from './api-contract/index.js';   // top-level inferred types
// HistoryPoint = { at: IsoDateTime; pricePence; provenance }; RouteSeriesPoint = { date: IsoDate; pricePence; fetchedAt; providerId; sourceKind }

// WP1 (coverage/rollUp.ts)
export function resolveWindow(w: WindowSpec, todayLondon: IsoDate): { departureFrom: IsoDate; departureTo: IsoDate; clamped: boolean };   // history's definition scope (ruling 8)
export function rollUp(outcomes: CellOutcome[]): RollUp;   // [] → { status: null, reason: null, minPence: null }
export function mergeWithinProvider(a: CellOutcome, b: CellOutcome): CellOutcome;   // (existing, incoming); priced|empty|no_data|error of one cell/stage/provider/execution; winner's fields, min priced minPence

// @gmt/db client.ts, migrate.ts (WP0; ruling 14)
export interface Db {
  raw: BetterSqlite3.Database; drizzle: BetterSQLite3Database;
  transaction<T>(fn: () => T extends Promise<unknown> ? never : T): T;            // Task 0.10's conditional type (the contract text is being aligned)
  immediateTransaction<T>(fn: () => T extends Promise<unknown> ? never : T): T;   // BEGIN IMMEDIATE, same Promise guard (ruling 14)
  close(): void;
}
export function openDatabase(path: string): Db;
export function pendingMigrations(db: Db, migrationsDir: string): string[];
export function applyMigrations(db: Db, migrationsDir: string): { applied: string[] };
```

## 5. Interfaces produced

The plan's "Database" block with this revision's changes applied (each marked `// ruling N` is a contract change the plan is being updated to carry):

```ts
export interface RepoOptions { allowSynthetic: boolean }
export interface Repos { airports: AirportsRepo; fx: FxRepo; definitions: DefinitionsRepo; executions: ExecutionsRepo; cells: CellsRepo; calendarPrices: CalendarPricesRepo; itineraries: ItinerariesRepo; ledger: LedgerRepo; alerts: AlertsRepo; notifications: NotificationsRepo; routeHistory: RoutePriceHistoryRepo }
export function createRepos(db: Db, clock: Clock, opts: RepoOptions): Repos;
// Constructors take only what they use (arch-005): AirportsRepo(db), FxRepo(db), DefinitionsRepo(db, clock), ExecutionsRepo(db, clock), CellsRepo(db, clock, opts),
// CalendarPricesRepo(db, clock, opts), ItinerariesRepo(db, clock, opts), LedgerRepo(db), AlertsRepo(db, clock), NotificationsRepo(db, clock), RoutePriceHistoryRepo(db, opts).
export interface Airport { iata: Iata; icao: string | null; name: string; city: string; country: string; tz: string; lat: number; lon: number; metro: string | null; source: string; datasetVersion: string }
export interface AirportsDataset { version: string; airports: Array<Omit<Airport, 'source' | 'datasetVersion'>>; metros: Record<string, string[]> }
export class AirportsRepo { exists(iata): boolean; get(iata): Airport | null; search(q: string, limit: number): Airport[]; metroOf(iata): string | null; airportsOfMetro(code: string): Iata[]; sharedMetro(iatas: Iata[]): string | null; count(): number; upsertAll(dataset: AirportsDataset): void; datasetVersion(): string | null; tzOf(iata): string | null }
export class FxRepo { rateNano(date: IsoDate, currency: string): number | null; latest(currency: string, maxAgeDays: number, today: IsoDate): { rateNano: number; date: IsoDate; source: string } | null; upsert(rows: Array<{ date: IsoDate; currency: string; rateNano: number; source: string }>): void }
export class DefinitionsRepo { create(input: DefinitionInput & { confirmedCostPence?: number }): SearchDefinition; update(id, patch: Partial<DefinitionInput>): SearchDefinition; get(id): SearchDefinition | null; list(opts?: { includeDeleted?: boolean }): SearchDefinition[]; softDelete(id): void; setConfirmedCost(id, pence: number | null): void }
export type ExecutionCreateInput = { definitionId: string; isChild: boolean; cell: CellKey | null; snapshot: DefinitionSnapshot; estimate: Estimate | null; trigger: Execution['trigger']; status: 'pending' | 'running' | (ExecutionStatus & `skipped_${string}`) }
export type ExecutionPatch = Partial<Pick<Execution, 'stage' | 'resumeAfter' | 'errorText' | 'startedAt' | 'finishedAt' | 'cancelRequested' | 'estimate' | 'snapshot'>>
export class ExecutionsRepo { create(input): Execution; get(id): Execution | null; listForDefinition(definitionId, opts: { limit: number; includeChildren: boolean }): Execution[]; listByStatus(statuses: ExecutionStatus[]): Execution[]; activeParent(definitionId): Execution | null; activeChild(definitionId, cell: CellKey): Execution | null; latestCompleted(definitionId): Execution | null; claimResume(id): boolean /* also clears cancel_requested and finished_at (dm-004) */; setStatus(id, status, patch?): void; patch(id, patch): void; requestCancel(id): void; bootSweep(bootAt: IsoDateTime): { interrupted: string[] }; incrementAutoResume(id): number; resetAutoResume(id): void; lastScheduledFiring(definitionId): IsoDateTime | null }
export interface TileWrite { tileIndex: number; cells: CellKey[]; prices: CalendarPriceInsert[]; outcomes: Array<Pick<CellOutcome, 'origin' | 'destination' | 'departureDate' | 'returnDate' | 'status' | 'reason' | 'lastError' | 'fetchedAt' | 'minPence' | 'cabin' | 'pointOfSale' | 'sourceKind'>>; discards: number }
export interface CalendarPriceInsert { providerId: ProviderId; sourceKind: SourceKind; cabin: Cabin; cell: CellKey; pricePence: number; priceNativeMinor: number; currencyNative: string; fx: FxInfo; pointOfSale: PointOfSale; fetchedAt: IsoDateTime; providerObservedAt: IsoDateTime | null; maxAgeHours: number | null; vendorLink: string | null; reusedFromExecutionId: string | null }
export interface CalendarPriceRow extends CalendarPriceInsert { id: number; executionId: string; provenance: Provenance }
export interface VerifiedCellWrite { cell: CellKey; outcome: TileWrite['outcomes'][number]; itineraries: Array<{ itinerary: NormalisedItinerary; offers: Array<{ offer: Offer; observation: ObservationFields & { provenance: Provenance; suppliedFields: SuppliedField[]; partialExpansion: boolean } }> }> }   // ruling 13: bestObservationSelector removed; best_observation_id = cheapest observation written
export interface ReusedCopy { cell: CellKey; outcome: CellOutcome; price: CalendarPriceRow | null }
export class CellsRepo { seed(executionId, cells: Cell[], discoveryProviders: ProviderId[]): void; seedVerification(executionId, selected: Array<{ cell: CellKey; reason: SelectionReason }>, allCells: CellKey[], verificationProviders: ProviderId[]): void; outcomes(executionId, filter?: { stage?: Stage; providerId?: ProviderId; cells?: CellKey[] }): CellOutcome[]; resumable(executionId, stage, providerId, manual: boolean): CellKey[]; markDeparted(executionId, today: IsoDate): number; markNoData(executionId, stage, providerId, reason: NoDataReason): number; writeTile(executionId, providerId, w: TileWrite): void; writeTileError(executionId, providerId, cells: CellKey[], reason: string, lastError: string | null): void; writeVerifiedCell(executionId, providerId, w: VerifiedCellWrite): void; setOutcome(executionId, cell, stage, providerId, patch: Partial<CellOutcome> & { incrementAttempts?: boolean }): void /* ruling 4: never sets or rewrites priced|empty|no_data */; setOutcomesForProvider(executionId, stage, providerId, from: CellOutcomeStatus[], to: { status: CellOutcomeStatus; reason: string | null }): number /* ruling 4 */; copyReused(executionId, providerId, copies: ReusedCopy[]): void; rollUps(executionId): Map<string, { discovery: RollUp; verification: RollUp }>; buckets(executionId, stage, providerId): Buckets; bucketsPerDay(executionId): Map<IsoDate, Array<{ stage: Stage; providerId: ProviderId; buckets: Buckets; total: number }>>; selected(executionId): Array<{ cell: CellKey; reason: SelectionReason }>; hasVerificationOutcomes(executionId): boolean }
export class CalendarPricesRepo { insert(executionId, rows: CalendarPriceInsert[]): number[]; latestPerCell(definitionId, cabin, space: ResolvedSpace | null): CalendarPriceRow[]; fetchedBy(executionId): CalendarPriceRow[]; previousFor(definitionId, providerId, cabin, cell, before: IsoDateTime, notOlderThan: IsoDateTime): CalendarPriceRow | null; reuseCandidates(definitionId, providerId, cabin, pointOfSale, sourceKind, cells: CellKey[], newerThan: IsoDateTime): ReusedCopy[]; supersedeReused(executionId, providerId, cell): void; history(origin, destination, cabin, opts?: { definitionId?: string }): Array<{ providerId: ProviderId; points: Array<HistoryPoint & { cell: Cell; executionId: string }> }> /* ruling 8 */; belowVerified(definitionId, cabin, space, thresholdPence): CalendarPriceRow[] }
export interface ObservationRow extends Observation { offer: Offer & { id: number }; itinerary: NormalisedItinerary; cell: Cell; seller: string }
export class ItinerariesRepo { upsertItinerary(it: NormalisedItinerary): void; upsertOffer(offer: Offer): number; insertObservation(obs: Omit<Observation, 'id'>): number; get(hash): (NormalisedItinerary & { firstSeenAt: IsoDateTime }) | null; observationsForDefinition(definitionId, opts: { executionIds: string[] | 'all'; space: ResolvedSpace | null; cabin: Cabin; includeChildren: boolean }): ObservationRow[]; writtenBy(executionId): ObservationRow[]; previousObservation(offerId: number, before: IsoDateTime): Observation | null; offerHistory(hash): Array<{ providerId: ProviderId; seller: string; cabin: Cabin; points: HistoryPoint[] }>; latestVerificationOutcomeForCell(definitionId, cell, cabin: Cabin): CellOutcome | null /* ruling 9 */; bestObservationForCell(executionId, cell): number | null }
export class LedgerRepo { countsFor(executionId): { calls: number; costMicroGbp: number; tiles: number; cacheHits: number; perProvider: Array<{ providerId: ProviderId; stage: Stage | null; calls: number; costMicroGbp: number; tiles: number; cacheHits: number }> } /* ruling 7: tiles = first-attempt upstream rows; parity = tiles + cacheHits */; firstAttemptTiles(executionId, providerId): number; sweepPending(olderThan: IsoDateTime): number; pruneCacheHits(olderThan: IsoDateTime, providerIds: ProviderId[]): number }
export class AlertsRepo { rules(definitionId?: string): AlertRule[]; get(id): AlertRule | null; create(input: AlertRuleInput): AlertRule; update(id, patch: Partial<AlertRuleInput>): AlertRule; softDelete(id): void /* ruling 12 */; events(filter: { ruleId?: string; definitionId?: string; limit: number }): AlertEvent[]; priorFirings(ruleId, identityKeys: string[], since: IsoDateTime): PriorFiring[]; insertEvent(e: Omit<AlertEvent, 'id'>): number }
export class NotificationsRepo { wasSent(kind: OperatorMessageKind, executionId: string | null, definitionId: string): boolean; record(kind, executionId, definitionId, status: 'sent' | 'failed'): void }
export class RoutePriceHistoryRepo { upsert(rows: Array<{ origin; destination; cabin: Cabin | 'unknown'; providerId; date: IsoDate; lowestPricePence: number; fetchedAt: IsoDateTime }>): void; series(origin, destination, providerId, cabin: Cabin | 'unknown'): RouteSeriesPoint[] /* ruling 9 */ }
export interface BudgetConfig { providers: Partial<Record<ProviderId, ProviderLimits & { billable: boolean; trialUsed: number }>>; spend: Config['spend'] }   // ruling 6: fixtureCostMicroGbp removed; the fixture's rate is providers.fixture.costPerCallMicroGbp
export function budgetConfigFromConfig(c: Config): BudgetConfig;
export class SqliteBudgetGuard implements BudgetGuard { constructor(db: Db, config: BudgetConfig, clock: Clock) }   // reserve refuses a costMicroGbp other than the provider's costPerCallMicroGbp (ruling 6)
export class SqliteResponseCache implements ResponseCache { constructor(db: Db, clock: Clock) }   // keys must be 64 lower-case hex (sec-001)
export class AppLock { constructor(db: Db, holder: 'app' | 'tool', clock: Clock, log: Logger, opts?: { sleep?: (ms: number) => void; pollMs?: number }); acquire(opts: { waitMs: number }): 'acquired' | 'refused'; heartbeat(): void; release(): void; static inspect(db: Db): { holder: 'app' | 'tool'; heartbeatAt: IsoDateTime; acquiredAt: IsoDateTime } | null }
export function seedAirports(db: Db, dataset: AirportsDataset): { inserted: number; datasetVersion: string };
export function sanityCheckAirports(db: Db, referenced: Iata[]): { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: string };
export function runMaintenance(db: Db, clock: Clock, log: Logger, opts: { lifetimeProviders: ProviderId[] }): { cacheRowsDeleted: number; ledgerRowsPruned: number; dbBytes: number };
export function loadAirportsDataset(dir: string): AirportsDataset;
export function backupDatabase(dbPath: string, backupsDir: string, opts: { keep: number; clock: Clock; verify?: (file: string) => string }): { file: string };   // verify is a test seam (default: PRAGMA integrity_check)
```

Additional exports (additive): `RowRejected` (every repository refusal, `.rule`), `RowCorrupt` (a stored value that fails to parse, `.column`), `MIGRATIONS_DIR`, `AIRPORTS_DATA_DIR`, `AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS`, `HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS`, `LOCK_STALE_AFTER_MS`, `effectiveCallCap`. `monthWindow`, `CLAIM_RESUME_SQL` and the Drizzle tables stay internal (arch-012, arch-r2-007): no named consumer, and exporting tables would invite queries outside the repositories.

## 6. Library notes

Verified on 2026-10-14. Versions are the plan's Task 0.1 pins. "Scratch run" means a throwaway install under the session scratchpad with those exact versions on Node 26.10.

- **drizzle-kit 0.31.11 + drizzle-orm 0.45.4** (context7 `/drizzle-team/drizzle-orm-docs`, `sqlite/indexes-constraints.mdx`, `drizzle-kit-generate.mdx`; scratch run). A schema split over files that import each other as `./parent.js` (NodeNext style) generated cleanly with plain `drizzle-kit generate --name=initial`; `check(name, sql.raw('status IN (…)'))` was emitted as `CONSTRAINT "…" CHECK(status IN (…))`; `uniqueIndex(…).on(…).where(sql.raw('is_child = 0 AND status IN (…)'))` was emitted as `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX … WHERE is_child = 0 AND status IN (…)`; re-running with no schema change printed "No schema changes, nothing to migrate" and wrote nothing. So the script is `db:generate` = `drizzle-kit generate`; WP3 passes `--name=initial` when it regenerates (`pnpm --filter @gmt/db db:generate --name=initial`), and later work packages name their own migrations (arch-r2-010); no tsx fallback and no hand-appended SQL are needed (ruling 16). Layout: `drizzle/0000_initial.sql`, `drizzle/meta/_journal.json`, `drizzle/meta/0000_snapshot.json`. API used: `sqliteTable(name, columns, (t) => [ … ])`, `integer`, `text`, `real`, `primaryKey({ columns })`, `foreignKey({ columns, foreignColumns })`, `.references(() => t.id)`, `index`, `uniqueIndex`, `.where(sql)`, `check`. Repositories use `db.raw.prepare` with explicit SQL; `// why:` the queries are window functions, CTEs, `json_each` and compare-and-set statements reviewers must read; Drizzle owns schema and migration.
- **better-sqlite3 13.0.3** (context7 `/wiselibs/better-sqlite3` `docs/api.md`; scratch run). `transaction(fn)` and its `.immediate()` variant (wrapped by WP0's `Db.transaction` and `Db.immediateTransaction`); nested transaction functions become savepoints; async functions are not supported. `pragma(name, { simple: true })`. `Database.SqliteError` with `.code` (`SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE`, `SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK`, `SQLITE_BUSY`, `SQLITE_NOTADB`). Named parameters `@name`; extra object keys are ignored (verified), a missing key throws. `RETURNING` with `.all()`/`.get()`. `new Database(path, { readonly: true, fileMustExist: true })`. Scratch-verified for `backupDatabase`: a read-only connection runs `VACUUM INTO ?` against a WAL database whose writer is still open with uncheckpointed pages, and the copy holds them; `VACUUM INTO` accepts an existing **empty** target (created with `openSync(file, 'wx', 0o600)`, mode 600 kept) and fails with `SQLITE_ERROR "output file already exists"` on a non-empty one; a non-database source fails with `SQLITE_NOTADB`; `openSync(path, 'wx')` fails with `EEXIST` on a dangling symlink. Scratch-verified for the ledger path CHECK: `GLOB '/*' AND NOT GLOB '//*' AND NOT GLOB '*[^A-Za-z0-9._~/-]*'` accepts `/api/v1/search` and `/aviasales/v3/prices_for_dates` and rejects `//h/x`, `/x#t`, `/x@y`, `/x?y`, `x`, `/a b`. Type affinity: inserting the text `'412'` into an INTEGER column stores the integer 412 (passes `typeof = 'integer'`); `'abc'` and `'412.5'` fail the CHECK.
- **vitest 5.0.4**: `describe`, `it(name, fn, timeout)`, `it.each`, `afterEach`, `expect(...).toThrow(Class | RegExp | asymmetric)`, `vi.fn`. Per-task runs use `pnpm --filter @gmt/db exec vitest run <file>` (decision 8); thresholds apply to the full `pnpm --filter @gmt/db test`. Hooks registered at module scope of an imported helper apply to the importing test file.
- **node:worker_threads** (Node ≥ 22): `Worker`, `SharedArrayBuffer`, `Atomics` (claim race; the busy-but-absorbed contention test).
- **mwgg/Airports** (MIT) at commit `a89e4e0dda9d9878337e2da190ebe1ef1d6af705` (2026-10-01): 29,308 entries, 7,917 with a three-letter IATA code and a `tz`, no duplicate IATA codes; no metro field.
- **Wikipedia "IATA airport code", section "Cities with multiple commercial airports"** (CC BY-SA 4.0), revision `1378312633` (raw wikitext `https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=IATA_airport_code&oldid=1378312633&action=raw&section=8`). Parsed with the Task 3.5 rules (former codes removed, members checked against the airports file and the metro's majority country) it yields **53** metro codes, dropping `NYC:IDL`, `BER:SXF`, `LUH:HWR`, `MIA:PBI` (absent from the airports file), with `LON: LCY LGW LHR LTN SEN STN` and `NYC: EWR JFK LGA`. The list is illustrative: an airport absent from it has `metro = NULL`, and Travelpayouts queries it per airport (spec §5.2).

**Modify**: `packages/db/test/client.test.ts` (WP0's; Task 3.1 adds the cases that need a migration, arch-011), `packages/db/src/schema/index.ts`, `packages/db/src/index.ts`, `packages/db/package.json` (dependency `@gmt/core: workspace:*` already present; script `"db:generate": "drizzle-kit generate"`). **Delete**: `packages/db/drizzle/.gitkeep`.

`// why:` the migration is regenerated from scratch whenever a task adds a table (`rm -rf packages/db/drizzle && pnpm --filter @gmt/db db:generate --name=initial`). No database outside a test exists before WP3 merges, so the merged branch carries exactly one migration. After WP3 merges, schema changes are new migrations, never edits.

**Code rules this brief keeps (ruling 15)**: no `as` in `src/` or `test/` except `as const` and `satisfies`. Brands are minted only through `asIata`/`asIsoDate`/`asIsoDateTime` (row mappers via `internal/read.ts`, which turns a failure into `RowCorrupt` naming the column); enum columns narrow through `oneOf(values, v)`; JSON columns parse through core schemas under `apiContract` (`definitionShapeSchema` for stored definitions and snapshots, never the refined `definitionCreateRequestSchema`, whose save-time refinements belong to the request path; `estimateSchema`, `bookingSchema`) or the small guards in `internal/read.ts` (`readVerificationPolicy` reads the one nested object whose core schema carries a save-time refinement); row interfaces use `string`/`number` for every column; errors narrow with `instanceof`; key lists come from typed constant arrays, never `Object.keys(...) as`.

## 8. Tasks

Conventions: run commands from the repository root; tests import from `../src/...js`; when a step says "Add to" a file, its `import` lines go to the top and merge with existing ones; commit messages end with `MrReasonable <4990954+MrReasonable@users.noreply.github.com>`. "Regenerate the migration" = `rm -rf packages/db/drizzle && pnpm --filter @gmt/db db:generate --name=initial`.

### Task 3.1: Ledger tables, the global-month cap in SQL, and the first migration (first failing test)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/paths.ts`, `src/types.ts`, `src/internal/enums.ts`, `src/internal/validate.ts`, `src/internal/read.ts`, `src/schema/checks.ts`, `src/schema/searchDefinitions.ts`, `src/schema/searchExecutions.ts`, `src/schema/providerRequests.ts`, `src/budget/window.ts`, `src/budgetConfig.ts` (type and `effectiveCallCap`), `src/budgetGuard.ts`, `test/helpers/db.ts`, `test/helpers/factories.ts`, `test/helpers/hold-worker.mjs`, `test/budgetGuard.cap.test.ts`
- Modify: `test/client.test.ts` (WP0's), `src/schema/index.ts`, `package.json`
- Generate: `drizzle/0000_initial.sql`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Db` (incl. `immediateTransaction`), `openDatabase`, `pendingMigrations`, `applyMigrations`, `BudgetGuard`, `BudgetExceeded`, `ProviderLimits`, `fixedClock`, `asIsoDateTime`.
- Produces: `SqliteBudgetGuard` (provider and global-month predicates, the cost rule), `BudgetConfig`, `effectiveCallCap`, `RowRejected`, `RowCorrupt`, `MIGRATIONS_DIR`, `AIRPORTS_DATA_DIR`, every type in `src/types.ts`, the `provider_requests`, `search_definitions`, `search_executions` tables.

**Arithmetic pinned by this task.** The cap is 300p = 300 × 10 000 = **3,000,000 µGBP**; each call costs **3,200 µGBP** (0.32p, spec §6.3), and the provider's configured rate is 3,200 so `reserve` accepts it. After *k* billed calls the global usage is 3,200·*k*; a reserve is refused when 3,200·*k* + 3,200 > 3,000,000, i.e. *k* + 1 > 937.5. Reserves 1 to **937** succeed (937 × 3,200 = **2,998,400 µGBP**) and reserve 938 throws `BudgetExceeded` `global-month` with `current` 2,998,400, `requested` 3,200, `cap` 3,000,000 (3,001,600 > 3,000,000). Spec §9 item 3's literal form: 1,000 rows × 3,200 µGBP = **3,200,000 µGBP = 320p**, already over 300p, so the next reserve throws with `current` 3,200,000.

```ts
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { type Logger, asIsoDateTime } from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { type Db, openDatabase } from '../../src/client.js';
import { applyMigrations } from '../../src/migrate.js';
import { MIGRATIONS_DIR } from '../../src/paths.js';

/** The instant every test starts from unless it says otherwise. Minted, never cast (ruling 15). */
export const T0 = asIsoDateTime('2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z');
/** The one timestamp mint for tests. */
export const at = asIsoDateTime;

const handles: Db[] = [];
const dirs: string[] = [];
// why: test-008. Every handle opened through these helpers is closed, and every temp directory removed,
// after each test, including when the test failed half way. Registered at module scope, so it applies
// to every test file that imports this helper.
afterEach(() => {
  for (const db of handles.splice(0)) if (db.raw.open) db.close();
  for (const dir of dirs.splice(0)) rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

export function openTracked(path: string): Db {
  const db = openDatabase(path);
  handles.push(db);
  return db;
}

/** A migrated database. `:memory:` by default; pass a path for multi-handle tests. */
export function freshDb(path = ':memory:'): Db {
  const db = openTracked(path);
  applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR);
  return db;
}

/** A fresh temp directory removed after the test. */
export function tmpDir(): string {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'gmt-db-'));
  dirs.push(dir);
  return dir;
}

/** A unique file path ending in `.test.db` (the synthetic gate's test suffix, spec §5.5). */
export function tmpDbPath(name = 'wp3.test.db'): string {
  return join(tmpDir(), name);
}

export const silentLog: Logger = { debug: () => undefined, info: () => undefined, warn: () => undefined, error: () => undefined, child: () => silentLog };

export function count(db: Db, table: string): number {
  return db.raw.prepare<[], { n: number }>(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM ${table}`).get()?.n ?? 0;
}

/** The SqliteError code a call throws, or null; anything else is rethrown so a wrong failure never passes. */
export function sqliteCode(fn: () => unknown): string | null {
  try {
    fn();
    return null;
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof Database.SqliteError) return e.code;
    throw e;
  }
}
```

```ts
import type { ProviderLimits } from '@gmt/core';
import type { BudgetConfig } from '../../src/budgetConfig.js';

/** One provider's guard entry. Defaults: a 40usd-shaped subscription (rate 0, no fee). */
export function limits(over: Partial<ProviderLimits & { billable: boolean; trialUsed: number }> = {}): ProviderLimits & { billable: boolean; trialUsed: number } {
  return {
    monthlyCallCap: 10_000, window: 'month', billingDay: 1, ratePerMinute: 10, ratePerHour: 2000, concurrency: 2, cacheTtlHours: 1,
    costPerCallMicroGbp: 0, planFixedMicroGbp: 0, excludedFromSchedule: false, billable: true, trialUsed: 0, ...over,
  };
}

/** A guard configuration; `spend` overrides the spec §12 defaults. */
export function budgetConfig(providers: BudgetConfig['providers'], spend: Partial<BudgetConfig['spend']> = {}): BudgetConfig {
  return {
    providers,
    spend: { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 0, globalBillingDay: 1, softConfirmMicroGbp: 1_000_000, executionMaxCalls: 2000, executionMaxFreeCalls: 2000, executionMaxCostMicroGbp: 0, childMaxCalls: 6, ...spend },
  };
}
```

`packages/db/test/helpers/hold-worker.mjs`:

```js
// Holds the write lock on a file database for `holdMs`, then commits (plan Review Focus 5: contention that
// busy_timeout=5000 must absorb). Raw better-sqlite3, so the worker needs no workspace TypeScript.
import { parentPort, workerData } from 'node:worker_threads';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';

const db = new Database(workerData.path);
db.prepare('BEGIN IMMEDIATE').run();
db.prepare(`INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, endpoint_path, started_at, outcome, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt) VALUES ('travelpayouts', '/held', ?, 'ok', 0, 1)`).run(workerData.at);
parentPort.postMessage('holding');
Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, workerData.holdMs);
db.prepare('COMMIT').run();
db.close();
parentPort.postMessage('released');
```

`packages/db/test/budgetGuard.cap.test.ts`:

```ts
import { BudgetExceeded, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { SqliteBudgetGuard } from '../src/budgetGuard.js';
import { T0, at, count, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { budgetConfig, limits } from './helpers/factories.js';

const COST = 3_200; // µGBP per call: 0.32p (spec §6.3)
const CAP = 300 * 10_000; // 300p in µGBP = 3,000,000
// A per-call SearchApi plan (no fixed fee): its configured rate is COST, the only cost reserve accepts.
const perCall = budgetConfig({ searchapi: limits({ costPerCallMicroGbp: COST }) }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: CAP });
const call = { providerId: 'searchapi', executionId: null, tileIndex: null, costMicroGbp: COST, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/api/v1/search' } as const;

describe('SqliteBudgetGuard global-month cap in SQL (spec §5.3, §9 item 3)', () => {
  it('937 calls of 3,200 µGBP pass a 3,000,000 µGBP cap and the 938th throws global-month without writing a row', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, perCall, fixedClock(T0));
    for (let k = 1; k <= 937; k++) guard.reserve(call).finalise({ outcome: 'ok', httpStatus: 200, durationMs: 10 });
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'global-month' }).costMicroGbp).toBe(2_998_400);
    let thrown: unknown;
    try {
      guard.reserve(call);
    } catch (e) {
      thrown = e;
    }
    expect(thrown).toBeInstanceOf(BudgetExceeded);
    expect(thrown).toMatchObject({ scope: 'global-month', cap: 3_000_000, current: 2_998_400, requested: 3_200 });
    expect(count(db, 'provider_requests')).toBe(937);
  });

  it('1,000 ledger rows at 3,200 µGBP (320p) trip a 300p cap in SQL', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const insert = db.raw.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, execution_id, endpoint_path, tile_index, started_at, finished_at, duration_ms, http_status, outcome, billable, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt)
       VALUES ('searchapi', NULL, '/api/v1/search', NULL, @at, @at, 5, 200, 'ok', 1, 3200, 1)`,
    );
    db.transaction(() => {
      for (let i = 0; i < 1000; i++) insert.run({ at: T0 });
    });
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, perCall, fixedClock(T0));
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'global-month' })).toMatchObject({ calls: 1000, costMicroGbp: 3_200_000, cap: 3_000_000 });
    expect(() => guard.reserve(call)).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ scope: 'global-month', current: 3_200_000, requested: 3_200 }));
    expect(count(db, 'provider_requests')).toBe(1000);
  });
});

describe('recentCalls seeds the hourly limiter (core BudgetGuard port, cost-r2-001)', () => {
  it('counts every upstream attempt since `since` (inclusive) for one provider; cache hits, other providers and earlier rows excluded', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const insert = db.raw.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, execution_id, endpoint_path, tile_index, started_at, outcome, billable, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt) VALUES (@p, NULL, '/x', NULL, @at, @outcome, 0, 0, 1)`,
    );
    const since = '2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z';
    for (const outcome of ['pending', 'unknown', 'error', 'ok', 'cache_hit']) insert.run({ p: 'searchapi', at: since, outcome });
    insert.run({ p: 'searchapi', at: '2026-10-14T10:59:59.999Z', outcome: 'ok' }); // 1 ms before: excluded
    insert.run({ p: 'travelpayouts', at: T0, outcome: 'ok' });
    insert.run({ p: 'travelpayouts', at: T0, outcome: 'error' });
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: limits({ costPerCallMicroGbp: COST }), travelpayouts: limits({ billable: false, costPerCallMicroGbp: 0 }) }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: CAP }), fixedClock(T0));
    expect(guard.recentCalls('searchapi', at(since))).toBe(4); // pending, unknown, error, ok
    expect(guard.recentCalls('travelpayouts', at(since))).toBe(2); // a free vendor is metered the same way
    expect(() => guard.recentCalls('fixture', at(since))).toThrow(/no budget configuration/);
  });
});
```

Add to WP0's `packages/db/test/client.test.ts` (arch-011: one file for the client and migration subject; WP0's own cases stay as they are):

```ts
import { Worker } from 'node:worker_threads';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { applyMigrations, pendingMigrations } from '../src/migrate.js';
import { MIGRATIONS_DIR } from '../src/paths.js';
import { T0, count, openTracked, tmpDbPath } from './helpers/db.js';

const schemaOf = (db: ReturnType<typeof openTracked>) => db.raw.prepare(`SELECT type, name, sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' ORDER BY type, name`).all();
const insertLedgerRow = (db: ReturnType<typeof openTracked>) =>
  db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, endpoint_path, started_at, outcome, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt) VALUES ('travelpayouts', '/v3', ?, 'pending', 0, 1)`).run(T0);

describe('migrations against the first real migration (spec §7 "Migrations", §9 item 3)', () => {
  it('apply twice: the second run applies nothing and changes nothing', () => {
    const db = openTracked(tmpDbPath());
    expect(pendingMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR)).toEqual(['0000_initial']);
    expect(applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR)).toEqual({ applied: ['0000_initial'] });
    const before = schemaOf(db);
    expect(applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR)).toEqual({ applied: [] });
    expect(schemaOf(db)).toEqual(before);
    expect(pendingMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR)).toEqual([]);
    expect(count(db, '__drizzle_migrations')).toBe(1);
  });

  it('a missing migrations directory fails naming the directory', () => {
    expect(() => applyMigrations(openTracked(':memory:'), '/nonexistent/drizzle')).toThrow(/\/nonexistent\/drizzle/);
  });

  it('an async transaction callback is rejected and its synchronous writes are rolled back', () => {
    const db = openTracked(':memory:');
    applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR);
    // @ts-expect-error repository transaction callbacks are typed to return non-Promise values (Task 0.10's conditional type)
    expect(() => db.transaction(async () => insertLedgerRow(db))).toThrow(/synchronous/);
    // why: better-sqlite3 runs the callback up to its first await, so the insert happened; the rollback must undo it.
    expect(count(db, 'provider_requests')).toBe(0);
    // @ts-expect-error the same guard on the BEGIN IMMEDIATE variant (ruling 14)
    expect(() => db.immediateTransaction(async () => insertLedgerRow(db))).toThrow(/synchronous/);
    expect(count(db, 'provider_requests')).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('SQLite settings on a file database (spec §7, plan Review Focus 5)', () => {
  it('a second handle on the same file gets busy_timeout=5000 too (the tool bootstrap opens its own)', () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    openTracked(path);
    expect(openTracked(path).raw.pragma('busy_timeout', { simple: true })).toBe(5000);
  });

  it('contention shorter than busy_timeout is absorbed: a write waits for another connection and succeeds', async () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const db = openTracked(path);
    applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR);
    const worker = new Worker(new URL('./helpers/hold-worker.mjs', import.meta.url), { workerData: { path, at: T0, holdMs: 200 } });
    const released = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      worker.on('message', (m) => m === 'released' && resolve(m));
      worker.once('error', reject);
    });
    await new Promise((resolve) => worker.once('message', resolve)); // 'holding'
    const started = Date.now();
    insertLedgerRow(db); // blocks inside SQLite until the worker commits, well under 5,000 ms
    expect(Date.now() - started).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(100);
    await released;
    expect(count(db, 'provider_requests')).toBe(2);
  });

  it('contention longer than busy_timeout surfaces SqliteError SQLITE_BUSY, not a hang or a crash', () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const db = openTracked(path);
    applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR);
    const other = openTracked(path);
    other.raw.pragma('busy_timeout = 50'); // why: keep the test fast; production uses 5000
    db.raw.prepare('BEGIN IMMEDIATE').run();
    let thrown: unknown;
    try {
      insertLedgerRow(other);
    } catch (e) {
      thrown = e;
    }
    db.raw.prepare('ROLLBACK').run();
    expect(thrown).toBeInstanceOf(Database.SqliteError);
    if (!(thrown instanceof Database.SqliteError)) return;
    expect(thrown.code).toBe('SQLITE_BUSY');
  });
});
```

```ts
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

/** Absolute paths of the package's committed assets, valid from `src/` (Vitest) and `dist/` (image). Spec §7. */
export const MIGRATIONS_DIR = fileURLToPath(new URL('../drizzle', import.meta.url));
export const AIRPORTS_DATA_DIR = fileURLToPath(new URL('../data', import.meta.url));
```

`packages/db/src/internal/enums.ts`:

```ts
import type { Cabin, CellOutcomeStatus, ErrorReason, ExecutionStatus, ProviderId, SelectionReason, SkipReason, SourceKind, Stage, SuppliedField } from '@gmt/core';

// Typed constant lists: the only way the package enumerates a union (ruling 15: no `Object.keys(...) as`).
// `satisfies` proves every member belongs to the core union.
export const PROVIDER_IDS = ['travelpayouts', 'searchapi', 'fixture'] as const satisfies readonly ProviderId[];
export const SOURCE_KINDS = ['authorised-cached', 'scraped-aggregate', 'scraped-search', 'synthetic'] as const satisfies readonly SourceKind[];
export const CABINS = ['economy', 'premium_economy', 'business', 'first'] as const satisfies readonly Cabin[];
export const STAGES = ['discovery', 'verification'] as const satisfies readonly Stage[];
export const OUTCOME_STATUSES = ['not_run', 'priced', 'empty', 'no_data', 'error', 'skipped'] as const satisfies readonly CellOutcomeStatus[];
export const SKIP_REASONS = ['not_selected', 'budget', 'provider_disabled', 'departed'] as const satisfies readonly SkipReason[];
export const SELECTION_REASONS = ['always_include', 'top_n', 'ceiling', 'stride'] as const satisfies readonly SelectionReason[];
export const SUPPLIED_FIELDS = ['segments', 'segmentTimes', 'segmentCabins', 'selfTransfer', 'baggage', 'fareConditions', 'seller', 'price'] as const satisfies readonly SuppliedField[];
export const EXECUTION_STATUSES = ['pending', 'running', 'paused_budget', 'paused_error', 'interrupted', 'cancelled', 'completed', 'skipped_budget', 'skipped_confirm', 'skipped_window', 'skipped_missed'] as const satisfies readonly ExecutionStatus[];
/** Spec §3: an error outcome's reason is a `ProviderError.kind` or a normalisation reason, never a vendor message (prov-009). */
export const ERROR_REASONS = [
  'auth', 'rate-limit', 'quota', 'upstream', 'parse', 'network', 'limiter-timeout',
  'fx-unavailable', 'currency-unsupported', 'unknown-airport-tz', 'no-return-legs', 'unmappable-carrier', 'bad-local-time',
] as const satisfies readonly ErrorReason[];
/** The outcome statuses only a write that carries data may set (ruling 4). */
export const TERMINAL_STATUSES = ['priced', 'empty', 'no_data'] as const satisfies readonly CellOutcomeStatus[];
```

`packages/db/src/internal/validate.ts`:

```ts
import { type FxInfo, type ProviderId, type SourceKind, isoDateSchema, isoDateTimeSchema, stripUrlsInText } from '@gmt/core';
import { ERROR_REASONS } from './enums.js';

/** A row a repository refuses to write (spec §5.5, §6.3, §7). Always an error, never a silent fix. */
export class RowRejected extends Error {
  readonly rule: string;
  constructor(rule: string, message: string) {
    // why: messages can quote values; spec §5.3 forbids any sink holding a URL query.
    super(stripUrlsInText(`${rule}: ${message}`));
    this.name = 'RowRejected';
    this.rule = rule;
  }
}

/** Integers only for money, counts and rates (spec §6.3). */
export function assertInt(field: string, v: number, min = 0): void {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(v) || v < min) throw new RowRejected('integer', `${field} must be an integer ≥ ${min}, got ${String(v)}`);
}
export function assertNullableInt(field: string, v: number | null, min = 0): void {
  if (v !== null) assertInt(field, v, min);
}
/** `Date.prototype.toISOString()` form only (spec §6.3). */
export function assertIsoDateTime(field: string, v: string): void {
  if (!isoDateTimeSchema.safeParse(v).success) throw new RowRejected('timestamp', `${field} must be in toISOString() form, got ${v}`);
}
export function assertNullableIsoDateTime(field: string, v: string | null): void {
  if (v !== null) assertIsoDateTime(field, v);
}
export function assertIsoDate(field: string, v: string): void {
  if (!isoDateSchema.safeParse(v).success) throw new RowRejected('date', `${field} must be YYYY-MM-DD, got ${v}`);
}

/** Spec §5.5 synthetic gate on a provider alone (rows that carry a provider but no source kind yet). */
export function assertProviderAllowed(providerId: ProviderId, allowSynthetic: boolean): void {
  if (providerId === 'fixture' && !allowSynthetic) throw new RowRejected('synthetic-gate', 'fixture rows are refused outside syntheticAllowed');
}

/** Spec §5.5: `provider_id = 'fixture'` ⇔ `source_kind = 'synthetic'`, and the synthetic gate. */
export function assertProviderKind(providerId: ProviderId, sourceKind: SourceKind, allowSynthetic: boolean): void {
  if ((providerId === 'fixture') !== (sourceKind === 'synthetic')) {
    throw new RowRejected('provider-kind', `provider ${providerId} with source kind ${sourceKind}: fixture ⇔ synthetic must hold`);
  }
  assertProviderAllowed(providerId, allowSynthetic);
}

/** Spec §7 price-table money rules: integers; ISO 4217; converted ⇔ currency ≠ GBP; converted rows carry rate, date, source; unconverted pence = native minor. */
export function assertPriceMoney(p: { pricePence: number; priceNativeMinor: number; currencyNative: string; fx: FxInfo }): void {
  assertInt('price_pence', p.pricePence);
  assertInt('price_native_minor', p.priceNativeMinor);
  if (!/^[A-Z]{3}$/.test(p.currencyNative)) throw new RowRejected('currency', `currency_native must be ISO 4217, got ${p.currencyNative}`);
  if (!p.fx.converted) {
    if (p.currencyNative !== 'GBP') throw new RowRejected('fx', `unconverted price in ${p.currencyNative}`);
    if (p.pricePence !== p.priceNativeMinor) throw new RowRejected('fx', `price_pence ${p.pricePence} must equal price_native_minor ${p.priceNativeMinor} when unconverted`);
    return;
  }
  if (p.currencyNative === 'GBP') throw new RowRejected('fx', 'a GBP price cannot be FX-converted');
  assertInt('fx_rate_nano', p.fx.rateNano, 1);
  assertIsoDate('fx_rate_date', p.fx.rateDate);
  if (p.fx.source.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('fx', 'fx_source must be named');
}

/** ISO alpha-2 or 'unknown' (spec §3). */
export function assertPointOfSale(v: string): void {
  if (v !== 'unknown' && !/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(v)) throw new RowRejected('point-of-sale', `point_of_sale must be ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 or 'unknown', got ${v}`);
}

/** Spec §3: error reasons come from the closed list; the vendor message belongs in last_error (prov-009). */
export function assertErrorReason(reason: string | null): void {
  if (reason === null || !ERROR_REASONS.some((r) => r === reason)) throw new RowRejected('error-reason', `error reason must be a ProviderError kind or normalisation reason, got ${String(reason)}`);
}

/**
 * Spec §5.3 "endpoint_path is the path only": a leading single '/', then only unreserved path characters,
 * at most 200 long (sec-002). No query, fragment, userinfo, scheme or whitespace can pass.
 */
export function assertEndpointPath(path: string): void {
  if (!/^\/(?!\/)[A-Za-z0-9._~/-]{0,199}$/.test(path)) throw new RowRejected('endpoint-path', 'endpoint_path is a path only: /segment/segment, unreserved characters, ≤ 200');
}
```

`packages/db/src/internal/read.ts` (the row-mapping toolkit every repository uses; ruling 15):

```ts
import {
  type BaggageSnapshot, type Booking, type Cabin, type CellKey, type Estimate, type FareConditions, type Iata, type IsoDate, type IsoDateTime, type Layover,
  type SuppliedField, type VerificationPolicy, apiContract, asIata, asIsoDate, asIsoDateTime,
} from '@gmt/core';
import { CABINS, SUPPLIED_FIELDS } from './enums.js';

/** A stored value that does not parse as its type: corruption is reported, never passed through (dm-006). */
export class RowCorrupt extends Error {
  readonly column: string;
  constructor(column: string, detail: string) {
    super(`stored ${column} is not valid: ${detail}`);
    this.name = 'RowCorrupt';
    this.column = column;
  }
}

function mint<T>(column: string, v: string, f: (s: string) => T): T {
  try {
    return f(v);
  } catch (err) {
    throw new RowCorrupt(column, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
  }
}
export const readIata = (column: string, v: string): Iata => mint(column, v, asIata);
export const readDate = (column: string, v: string): IsoDate => mint(column, v, asIsoDate);
export const readTime = (column: string, v: string): IsoDateTime => mint(column, v, asIsoDateTime);
export const readNullableTime = (column: string, v: string | null): IsoDateTime | null => (v === null ? null : readTime(column, v));

/** Narrows a stored string to a member of a typed constant list. */
export function oneOf<T extends string>(column: string, values: readonly T[], v: string): T {
  const hit = values.find((x) => x === v);
  if (hit === undefined) throw new RowCorrupt(column, `unexpected value ${v}`);
  return hit;
}
export function nullableOneOf<T extends string>(column: string, values: readonly T[], v: string | null): T | null {
  return v === null ? null : oneOf(column, values, v);
}

export function readJson(column: string, text: string): unknown {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(text);
  } catch {
    throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'not JSON');
  }
}
export function isRecord(v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof v === 'object' && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v);
}
/** Parses through a core zod schema (anything with `parse`), reporting the column on failure. */
export function parseWith<T>(column: string, schema: { parse(v: unknown): T }, v: unknown): T {
  try {
    return schema.parse(v);
  } catch (err) {
    throw new RowCorrupt(column, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
  }
}

export function readStringArray(column: string, v: unknown): string[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(v)) throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'not an array');
  return v.map((x) => {
    if (typeof x !== 'string') throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'array member is not a string');
    return x;
  });
}
export const readIataArray = (column: string, v: unknown): Iata[] => readStringArray(column, v).map((s) => readIata(column, s));
export const readCabins = (column: string, v: unknown): Cabin[] => readStringArray(column, v).map((s) => oneOf(column, CABINS, s));
export const readSuppliedFields = (column: string, v: unknown): SuppliedField[] => readStringArray(column, v).map((s) => oneOf(column, SUPPLIED_FIELDS, s));

export function readInt(column: string, v: unknown): number {
  if (typeof v !== 'number' || !Number.isSafeInteger(v)) throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'expected an integer');
  return v;
}
export function readBool(column: string, v: unknown): boolean {
  if (typeof v !== 'boolean') throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'expected a boolean');
  return v;
}
export function readString(column: string, v: unknown): string {
  if (typeof v !== 'string') throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'expected a string');
  return v;
}

export function readLayovers(column: string, v: unknown): Layover[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(v)) throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'not an array');
  return v.map((x) => {
    if (!isRecord(x) || typeof x.at !== 'string') throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'bad layover');
    return { at: readIata(column, x.at), minutes: readInt(column, x.minutes), overnight: readBool(column, x.overnight), airportChange: readBool(column, x.airportChange) };
  });
}

export function readCellKey(column: string, v: unknown): CellKey {
  if (!isRecord(v) || typeof v.origin !== 'string' || typeof v.destination !== 'string' || typeof v.departureDate !== 'string' || typeof v.returnDate !== 'string') {
    throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'bad cell key');
  }
  return { origin: readIata(column, v.origin), destination: readIata(column, v.destination), departureDate: readDate(column, v.departureDate), returnDate: readDate(column, v.returnDate) };
}

function notes(column: string, v: unknown): { notes?: string[] } {
  return v === undefined ? {} : { notes: readStringArray(column, v) };
}
export function readBaggage(column: string, v: unknown): BaggageSnapshot {
  if (!isRecord(v)) throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'bad baggage');
  const cabinBag = v.cabinBag === undefined ? {} : { cabinBag: oneOf(column, ['included', 'not-included'], typeof v.cabinBag === 'string' ? v.cabinBag : '') };
  const checkedBags = v.checkedBags === undefined ? {} : { checkedBags: readInt(column, v.checkedBags) };
  return { ...cabinBag, ...checkedBags, ...notes(column, v.notes) };
}
export function readFareConditions(column: string, v: unknown): FareConditions {
  if (!isRecord(v)) throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'bad fare conditions');
  const refundable = v.refundable === undefined ? {} : { refundable: readBool(column, v.refundable) };
  const changeable = v.changeable === undefined ? {} : { changeable: readBool(column, v.changeable) };
  return { ...refundable, ...changeable, ...notes(column, v.notes) };
}
export const readBooking = (column: string, text: string): Booking => parseWith(column, apiContract.bookingSchema, readJson(column, text));
export const readEstimate = (column: string, text: string): Estimate => parseWith(column, apiContract.estimateSchema, readJson(column, text));

/**
 * A stored verification policy, read field by field. // why: core's policy schema refines topN ≤ maxCells and
 * alwaysInclude ≤ maxCells, which are save-time policy; a stored row (a child snapshot with maxCells 1 and an
 * inherited topN, spec §8.1) must read back as stored (arch-r2-003, dm-r2-001).
 */
export function readVerificationPolicy(column: string, v: unknown): VerificationPolicy {
  if (!isRecord(v) || !Array.isArray(v.alwaysInclude)) throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'not a verification policy');
  return {
    maxCells: readInt(column, v.maxCells), topN: readInt(column, v.topN), expandTop: readInt(column, v.expandTop), bookingLinks: readBool(column, v.bookingLinks),
    alwaysInclude: v.alwaysInclude.map((x: unknown) => readCellKey(column, x)),
    ...(v.priceCeilingPence === undefined ? {} : { priceCeilingPence: readInt(column, v.priceCeilingPence) }),
    ...(v.perDepartureDateBest === undefined ? {} : { perDepartureDateBest: readInt(column, v.perDepartureDateBest) }),
    ...(v.maxStops === undefined ? {} : { maxStops: readInt(column, v.maxStops) }),
  };
}
```

`packages/db/src/schema/checks.ts`:

```ts
import { type SQL, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { CABINS, SOURCE_KINDS } from '../internal/enums.js';

// why: CHECK and partial-index bodies use unqualified column names via sql.raw, emitted verbatim (section 6, scratch run).
// Values are schema constants, never input.
export function isInteger(col: string): SQL {
  return sql.raw(`typeof(${col}) = 'integer' AND ${col} >= 0`);
}
export function isNullOrInteger(col: string): SQL {
  return sql.raw(`${col} IS NULL OR (typeof(${col}) = 'integer' AND ${col} >= 0)`);
}
const ISO_GLOB = "'[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]T[0-9][0-9]:[0-9][0-9]:[0-9][0-9].[0-9][0-9][0-9]Z'";
/** `toISOString()` form in SQL behind the repositories (spec §6.3). */
export function isIsoTimestamp(col: string, nullable = false): SQL {
  return sql.raw(nullable ? `${col} IS NULL OR ${col} GLOB ${ISO_GLOB}` : `${col} GLOB ${ISO_GLOB}`);
}
export function isOneOf(col: string, values: readonly string[], nullable = false): SQL {
  const list = values.map((v) => `'${v}'`).join(', ');
  return sql.raw(nullable ? `${col} IS NULL OR ${col} IN (${list})` : `${col} IN (${list})`);
}
export function isBool(col: string, nullable = false): SQL {
  return sql.raw(nullable ? `${col} IS NULL OR ${col} IN (0, 1)` : `${col} IN (0, 1)`);
}
export function isPointOfSale(col: string, nullable = false): SQL {
  return sql.raw(`${nullable ? `${col} IS NULL OR ` : ''}${col} = 'unknown' OR ${col} GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z]'`);
}
/** Spec §5.5 in SQL: `provider_id = 'fixture'` ⇔ `source_kind = 'synthetic'` wherever a row holds both. */
export function isProviderKind(nullableKind = false): SQL {
  return sql.raw(`${nullableKind ? 'source_kind IS NULL OR ' : ''}(provider_id = 'fixture') = (source_kind = 'synthetic')`);
}

export const ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL = "'pending', 'running', 'paused_budget', 'paused_error', 'interrupted'";

/** The CHECKs both price tables carry (spec §7). */
export function priceTableChecks(table: string) {
  return [
    check(`${table}_price_ck`, isInteger('price_pence')),
    check(`${table}_native_ck`, isInteger('price_native_minor')),
    check(`${table}_fx_converted_ck`, sql.raw("(fx_converted = 1) = (currency_native <> 'GBP')")),
    check(`${table}_fx_complete_ck`, sql.raw('fx_converted = 0 OR (fx_rate_nano IS NOT NULL AND fx_rate_date IS NOT NULL AND fx_source IS NOT NULL)')),
    check(`${table}_fx_rate_ck`, isNullOrInteger('fx_rate_nano')),
    check(`${table}_fx_bool_ck`, isBool('fx_converted')),
    check(`${table}_currency_ck`, sql.raw("currency_native GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]'")),
    check(`${table}_fetched_ck`, isIsoTimestamp('fetched_at')),
    check(`${table}_observed_ck`, isIsoTimestamp('provider_observed_at', true)),
    check(`${table}_max_age_ck`, isNullOrInteger('max_age_hours')),
    check(`${table}_cabin_ck`, isOneOf('cabin', CABINS)),
    check(`${table}_pos_ck`, isPointOfSale('point_of_sale')),
    check(`${table}_kind_ck`, isOneOf('source_kind', SOURCE_KINDS)),
  ];
}
```

`packages/db/src/schema/searchDefinitions.ts`:

```ts
import { check, integer, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { CABINS } from '../internal/enums.js';
import { isBool, isIsoTimestamp, isInteger, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';

/** Spec §7 `search_definitions`, soft-deleted. JSON columns hold the core sub-objects; reads parse them back through core's schema. */
export const searchDefinitions = sqliteTable(
  'search_definitions',
  {
    id: text('id').primaryKey(),
    name: text('name').notNull(),
    originsJson: text('origins_json').notNull(),
    destinationsJson: text('destinations_json').notNull(),
    windowJson: text('window_json').notNull(),
    minNights: integer('min_nights').notNull(),
    maxNights: integer('max_nights').notNull(),
    cabin: text('cabin').notNull(),
    constraintsJson: text('constraints_json').notNull(),
    verificationPolicyJson: text('verification_policy_json').notNull(),
    budgetJson: text('budget_json').notNull(),
    providersJson: text('providers_json').notNull(),
    destinationNotesJson: text('destination_notes_json'),
    onwardCostJson: text('onward_cost_json'),
    scheduleCron: text('schedule_cron'),
    scheduleTz: text('schedule_tz').notNull(),
    confirmedCostPence: integer('confirmed_cost_pence'),
    enabled: integer('enabled').notNull(),
    deletedAt: text('deleted_at'),
    createdAt: text('created_at').notNull(),
    updatedAt: text('updated_at').notNull(),
  },
  () => [
    check('search_definitions_cabin_ck', isOneOf('cabin', CABINS)),
    check('search_definitions_min_nights_ck', isInteger('min_nights')),
    check('search_definitions_max_nights_ck', isInteger('max_nights')),
    check('search_definitions_confirmed_ck', isNullOrInteger('confirmed_cost_pence')),
    check('search_definitions_enabled_ck', isBool('enabled')),
    check('search_definitions_created_ck', isIsoTimestamp('created_at')),
    check('search_definitions_updated_ck', isIsoTimestamp('updated_at')),
    check('search_definitions_deleted_ck', isIsoTimestamp('deleted_at', true)),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/schema/searchExecutions.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text, uniqueIndex } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { EXECUTION_STATUSES, STAGES } from '../internal/enums.js';
import { ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL, isBool, isIsoTimestamp, isInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { searchDefinitions } from './searchDefinitions.js';

/**
 * Spec §7 `search_executions` (`trigger`, `created_at`; `started_at` NULL until the run starts).
 * One active parent per definition and one active child per (definition, cell): partial unique indexes.
 */
export const searchExecutions = sqliteTable(
  'search_executions',
  {
    id: text('id').primaryKey(),
    definitionId: text('definition_id').notNull().references(() => searchDefinitions.id),
    isChild: integer('is_child').notNull().default(0),
    childOrigin: text('child_origin'),
    childDestination: text('child_destination'),
    childDepartureDate: text('child_departure_date'),
    childReturnDate: text('child_return_date'),
    definitionSnapshotJson: text('definition_snapshot_json').notNull(),
    trigger: text('trigger').notNull(),
    status: text('status').notNull(),
    stage: text('stage'),
    estimateJson: text('estimate_json'),
    cancelRequested: integer('cancel_requested').notNull().default(0),
    resumeAfter: text('resume_after'),
    autoResumeCount: integer('auto_resume_count').notNull().default(0),
    createdAt: text('created_at').notNull(),
    startedAt: text('started_at'),
    finishedAt: text('finished_at'),
    errorText: text('error_text'),
  },
  (t) => [
    check('search_executions_status_ck', isOneOf('status', EXECUTION_STATUSES)),
    check('search_executions_trigger_ck', isOneOf('trigger', ['manual', 'schedule', 'tool'])),
    check('search_executions_stage_ck', isOneOf('stage', STAGES, true)),
    check('search_executions_is_child_ck', isBool('is_child')),
    check('search_executions_cancel_ck', isBool('cancel_requested')),
    check('search_executions_resume_count_ck', isInteger('auto_resume_count')),
    check('search_executions_created_ck', isIsoTimestamp('created_at')),
    check('search_executions_started_ck', isIsoTimestamp('started_at', true)),
    check('search_executions_finished_ck', isIsoTimestamp('finished_at', true)),
    check('search_executions_resume_after_ck', isIsoTimestamp('resume_after', true)),
    check(
      'search_executions_child_cell_ck',
      sql.raw(
        '(is_child = 0 AND child_origin IS NULL AND child_destination IS NULL AND child_departure_date IS NULL AND child_return_date IS NULL) OR ' +
          '(is_child = 1 AND child_origin IS NOT NULL AND child_destination IS NOT NULL AND child_departure_date IS NOT NULL AND child_return_date IS NOT NULL)',
      ),
    ),
    uniqueIndex('search_executions_active_parent_uq').on(t.definitionId).where(sql.raw(`is_child = 0 AND status IN (${ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL})`)),
    uniqueIndex('search_executions_active_child_uq')
      .on(t.definitionId, t.childOrigin, t.childDestination, t.childDepartureDate, t.childReturnDate)
      .where(sql.raw(`is_child = 1 AND status IN (${ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL})`)),
    index('search_executions_definition_started_idx').on(t.definitionId, t.startedAt),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/schema/providerRequests.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { PROVIDER_IDS } from '../internal/enums.js';
import { isBool, isIsoTimestamp, isInteger, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/** Spec §7 `provider_requests`: one row per HTTP attempt (`pending` at reserve) and per cache hit. FX is never ledgered. */
export const providerRequests = sqliteTable(
  'provider_requests',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    providerId: text('provider_id').notNull(),
    executionId: text('execution_id').references(() => searchExecutions.id),
    endpointPath: text('endpoint_path').notNull(),
    tileIndex: integer('tile_index'),
    startedAt: text('started_at').notNull(),
    finishedAt: text('finished_at'),
    durationMs: integer('duration_ms'),
    httpStatus: integer('http_status'),
    outcome: text('outcome').notNull(),
    billable: integer('billable'),
    costMicroGbp: integer('cost_micro_gbp').notNull(),
    httpAttempt: integer('http_attempt').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    check('provider_requests_provider_ck', isOneOf('provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS)),
    check('provider_requests_outcome_ck', isOneOf('outcome', ['pending', 'ok', 'error', 'unknown', 'cache_hit'])),
    check('provider_requests_billable_ck', isBool('billable', true)),
    check('provider_requests_cost_ck', isInteger('cost_micro_gbp')),
    check('provider_requests_attempt_ck', isInteger('http_attempt')),
    check('provider_requests_tile_ck', isNullOrInteger('tile_index')),
    check('provider_requests_started_ck', isIsoTimestamp('started_at')),
    check('provider_requests_finished_ck', isIsoTimestamp('finished_at', true)),
    // why: sec-002, the SQL twin of assertEndpointPath (GLOB has no length quantifier, so length() caps it).
    check('provider_requests_path_ck', sql.raw("endpoint_path GLOB '/*' AND endpoint_path NOT GLOB '//*' AND endpoint_path NOT GLOB '*[^A-Za-z0-9._~/-]*' AND length(endpoint_path) <= 200")),
    index('provider_requests_provider_idx').on(t.providerId, t.startedAt, t.outcome, t.billable),
    index('provider_requests_execution_idx').on(t.executionId),
  ],
);
```
