`packages/db/src/budget/window.ts` (internal; arch-012):

```ts
import type { IsoDateTime } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { assertInt } from '../internal/validate.js';
import { readTime } from '../internal/read.js';

/** Spec §7 "Budget windows": 00:00 UTC on the billing day (1–28) on or before `now`, for one month. One SQL expression for predicates and `/budget`. */
export const MONTH_WINDOW_SQL = `
WITH w AS (
  SELECT CASE WHEN CAST(strftime('%d', @now) AS INTEGER) >= @billingDay
              THEN strftime('%Y-%m-', @now)
              ELSE strftime('%Y-%m-', @now, 'start of month', '-1 month') END
         || printf('%02d', @billingDay) || 'T00:00:00.000Z' AS ws
)
SELECT ws AS windowStart, strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ', ws, '+1 month') AS windowEnd FROM w`;

export function monthWindow(db: Db, now: IsoDateTime, billingDay: number): { windowStart: IsoDateTime; windowEnd: IsoDateTime } {
  assertInt('billingDay', billingDay, 1);
  if (billingDay > 28) throw new RangeError(`billingDay must be 1–28, got ${billingDay}`);
  const row = db.raw.prepare<{ now: string; billingDay: number }, { windowStart: string; windowEnd: string }>(MONTH_WINDOW_SQL).get({ now, billingDay });
  if (row === undefined) throw new Error('month window query returned no row');
  return { windowStart: readTime('window_start', row.windowStart), windowEnd: readTime('window_end', row.windowEnd) };
}
```

`packages/db/src/budgetConfig.ts` (Task 3.1 part; Task 3.3 adds `budgetConfigFromConfig`):

```ts
import type { Config, ProviderId, ProviderLimits } from '@gmt/core';

/**
 * The guard's view of the config (plan "Database" contract, ruling 6). One entry per configured provider:
 * its limits (including `costPerCallMicroGbp`, the only cost `reserve` accepts), `trialUsed` (decision 14)
 * and whether it is billable. A provider with no entry cannot be reserved for (fail closed).
 */
export interface BudgetConfig { providers: Partial<Record<ProviderId, ProviderBudget>>; spend: Config['spend'] }
/** One provider's entry in `BudgetConfig`. */
export type ProviderBudget = ProviderLimits & { billable: boolean; trialUsed: number };

/** Spec §12 `SEARCHAPI_TRIAL_USED` (decision 14): credits spent before this install count against a lifetime window; monthly windows ignore it. */
export function effectiveCallCap(b: ProviderLimits & { trialUsed: number }): number {
  return b.window === 'lifetime' ? Math.max(0, b.monthlyCallCap - b.trialUsed) : b.monthlyCallCap;
}
```

`packages/db/src/budgetGuard.ts` (Task 3.1 version; Task 3.2 replaces `checkCaps` and `executionUsage`):

```ts
import { type BudgetGuard, BudgetExceeded, type BudgetScope, type Clock, type IsoDateTime, type ProviderId, type Reservation, type Usage } from '@gmt/core';
import { monthWindow } from './budget/window.js';
import { type BudgetConfig, type ProviderBudget, effectiveCallCap } from './budgetConfig.js';
import type { Db } from './client.js';
import { PROVIDER_IDS } from './internal/enums.js';
import { RowRejected, assertEndpointPath, assertInt, assertIsoDateTime } from './internal/validate.js';

type ReserveParams = Parameters<BudgetGuard['reserve']>[0];
type CacheHitParams = Parameters<BudgetGuard['recordCacheHit']>[0];
interface CallsAndCost { calls: number; cost: number }
interface Window { windowStart: IsoDateTime | null; windowEnd: IsoDateTime | null }

/** Spec §5.3 cap predicate for one provider: billed vendors count pending/unknown or billable rows; free vendors every non-cache row. */
export const PROVIDER_USAGE_SQL = `
SELECT COUNT(*) AS calls, COALESCE(SUM(cost_micro_gbp), 0) AS cost
FROM provider_requests
WHERE provider_id = @providerId AND outcome <> 'cache_hit'
  AND (@billed = 0 OR outcome IN ('pending', 'unknown') OR billable = 1)
  AND (@windowStart IS NULL OR (started_at >= @windowStart AND started_at < @windowEnd))`;

/** Spec §5.3 global-month: Σ cost over billed rows of billed providers in the global window (fixed fees added in TS). */
export const GLOBAL_USAGE_SQL = `
SELECT COUNT(*) AS calls, COALESCE(SUM(cost_micro_gbp), 0) AS cost
FROM provider_requests
WHERE provider_id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@billedProviders)) AND outcome <> 'cache_hit'
  AND (outcome IN ('pending', 'unknown') OR billable = 1)
  AND started_at >= @windowStart AND started_at < @windowEnd`;

const INSERT_PENDING_SQL = `
INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, execution_id, endpoint_path, tile_index, started_at, outcome, billable, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt)
VALUES (@providerId, @executionId, @endpointPath, @tileIndex, @startedAt, 'pending', NULL, @cost, @httpAttempt)`;
const FINALISE_SQL = `
UPDATE provider_requests SET outcome = @outcome, http_status = @httpStatus, duration_ms = @durationMs, finished_at = @finishedAt, billable = @billable,
  cost_micro_gbp = CASE WHEN @zeroCost = 1 THEN 0 ELSE cost_micro_gbp END
WHERE id = @id AND outcome = 'pending'`;
const INSERT_CACHE_HIT_SQL = `
INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, execution_id, endpoint_path, tile_index, started_at, finished_at, duration_ms, http_status, outcome, billable, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt)
VALUES (@providerId, @executionId, @endpointPath, @tileIndex, @at, @at, 0, NULL, 'cache_hit', 0, 0, 0)`;

/**
 * The budget guard, enforced in SQL (spec §5.3). `reserve` runs every cap predicate and inserts the `pending`
 * ledger row inside one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction (`Db.immediateTransaction`), so two writers can never
 * both pass the same headroom. The billable decision happens at `finalise`.
 */
export class SqliteBudgetGuard implements BudgetGuard {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    private readonly config: BudgetConfig,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
  ) {}

  reserve(p: ReserveParams): Reservation {
    const budget = this.budgetFor(p.providerId);
    assertInt('httpAttempt', p.httpAttempt, 1);
    if (p.tileIndex !== null) assertInt('tileIndex', p.tileIndex);
    assertEndpointPath(p.endpointPath);
    // why: ruling 6 (cost-002). The configured rate is the only cost the ledger can hold, so a stale or mis-wired
    // caller cannot record 0 for a per-call plan and slip past global-month and execution-cost.
    if (p.costMicroGbp !== budget.costPerCallMicroGbp) throw new RowRejected('cost', `${p.providerId} costs ${budget.costPerCallMicroGbp} µGBP per call; got ${String(p.costMicroGbp)}`);
    const now = this.clock.now();
    const id = this.db.immediateTransaction(() => {
      this.checkCaps(p, budget, now);
      return Number(
        this.db.raw
          .prepare(INSERT_PENDING_SQL)
          .run({ providerId: p.providerId, executionId: p.executionId, endpointPath: p.endpointPath, tileIndex: p.tileIndex, startedAt: now, cost: p.costMicroGbp, httpAttempt: p.httpAttempt })
          .lastInsertRowid,
      );
    });
    return this.reservation(id, budget);
  }

  recordCacheHit(p: CacheHitParams): void {
    this.budgetFor(p.providerId);
    if (p.tileIndex !== null) assertInt('tileIndex', p.tileIndex);
    assertEndpointPath(p.endpointPath);
    this.db.raw.prepare(INSERT_CACHE_HIT_SQL).run({ providerId: p.providerId, executionId: p.executionId, endpointPath: p.endpointPath, tileIndex: p.tileIndex, at: this.clock.now() });
  }

  usage(scope: BudgetScope): Usage {
    const now = this.clock.now();
    if (scope.kind === 'global-month') {
      const g = this.globalUsage(now);
      return { calls: g.calls, costMicroGbp: g.cost, cap: this.config.spend.monthlyCapMicroGbp, windowStart: g.windowStart, windowEnd: g.windowEnd };
    }
    if (scope.kind === 'provider-month' || scope.kind === 'provider-lifetime') {
      const budget = this.budgetFor(scope.providerId);
      const asked = scope.kind === 'provider-lifetime' ? 'lifetime' : 'month';
      const u = this.providerUsage(scope.providerId, budget, now, asked);
      // why: plan Budget port "cap is null for a window the plan does not use".
      return { calls: u.calls, costMicroGbp: u.cost, cap: asked === budget.window ? effectiveCallCap(budget) : null, windowStart: u.windowStart, windowEnd: u.windowEnd };
    }
    return this.executionUsage(scope.kind, scope.executionId);
  }

  /**
   * Core port (cost-r2-001): upstream attempts for one provider since `since` (inclusive), whatever their outcome or
   * billable flag, because vendor rate limits meter requests, not billed calls. Cache hits are not requests.
   */
  recentCalls(providerId: ProviderId, since: IsoDateTime): number {
    assertIsoDateTime('since', since);
    this.budgetFor(providerId); // fail closed on an unconfigured provider
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string, string], { n: number }>(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM provider_requests WHERE provider_id = ? AND outcome <> 'cache_hit' AND started_at >= ?`)
      .get(providerId, since)?.n ?? 0;
  }

  protected checkCaps(p: ReserveParams, budget: ProviderBudget, now: IsoDateTime): void {
    const prov = this.providerUsage(p.providerId, budget, now, budget.window);
    const callCap = effectiveCallCap(budget);
    if (prov.calls + 1 > callCap) throw this.exceeded(budget.window === 'lifetime' ? 'provider-lifetime' : 'provider-month', callCap, prov.calls, 1, p.providerId);
    if (budget.billable) {
      const g = this.globalUsage(now);
      const cap = this.config.spend.monthlyCapMicroGbp;
      // why: spec §5.3 "A cap of 0 refuses when current + this > 0; zero-cost calls pass."
      if (p.costMicroGbp > 0 && g.cost + p.costMicroGbp > cap) throw this.exceeded('global-month', cap, g.cost, p.costMicroGbp, p.providerId);
    }
  }

  protected executionUsage(kind: 'execution-calls' | 'execution-free-calls' | 'execution-cost', executionId: string): Usage {
    // why: Task 3.1 enforces provider and global scopes only; Task 3.2 replaces this method.
    throw new RangeError(`scope ${kind} is not supported (execution ${executionId})`);
  }

  protected providerUsage(providerId: ProviderId, budget: ProviderBudget, now: IsoDateTime, window: 'month' | 'lifetime'): CallsAndCost & Window {
    const w: Window = window === 'lifetime' ? { windowStart: null, windowEnd: null } : monthWindow(this.db, now, budget.billingDay);
    const row = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CallsAndCost>(PROVIDER_USAGE_SQL).get({ providerId, billed: budget.billable ? 1 : 0, ...w });
    return { calls: row?.calls ?? 0, cost: row?.cost ?? 0, ...w };
  }

  protected globalUsage(now: IsoDateTime): CallsAndCost & { windowStart: IsoDateTime; windowEnd: IsoDateTime } {
    const w = monthWindow(this.db, now, this.config.spend.globalBillingDay);
    const billed = this.providersWhere(true);
    const row = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CallsAndCost>(GLOBAL_USAGE_SQL).get({ billedProviders: JSON.stringify(billed), ...w });
    // why: spec §5.3 the subscription fee counts once per enabled subscription provider, not per row.
    const fixed = billed.reduce((sum, id) => sum + (this.config.providers[id]?.planFixedMicroGbp ?? 0), 0);
    return { calls: row?.calls ?? 0, cost: (row?.cost ?? 0) + fixed, ...w };
  }

  protected providersWhere(billable: boolean): ProviderId[] {
    return PROVIDER_IDS.filter((id) => this.config.providers[id]?.billable === billable);
  }

  protected budgetFor(providerId: ProviderId): ProviderBudget {
    const b = this.config.providers[providerId];
    if (b === undefined) throw new Error(`provider ${providerId} has no budget configuration`); // fail closed
    return b;
  }

  protected exceeded(scope: BudgetExceeded['scope'], cap: number, current: number, requested: number, providerId: ProviderId): BudgetExceeded {
    return new BudgetExceeded({ scope, cap, current, requested, providerId });
  }

  private reservation(id: number, budget: ProviderBudget): Reservation {
    let done = false;
    return {
      id: String(id),
      finalise: (r) => {
        if (done) throw new Error(`reservation ${id} already finalised`);
        assertInt('durationMs', r.durationMs);
        // spec §5.3 Billable rule: billable providers iff upstream HTTP 200; free providers never.
        const billable = budget.billable && r.outcome === 'ok' && r.httpStatus === 200;
        // why: an `unknown` attempt may have been billed by the vendor, so it keeps its cost and stays counted.
        const zeroCost = !billable && r.outcome !== 'unknown';
        const changes = this.db.raw
          .prepare(FINALISE_SQL)
          .run({ id, outcome: r.outcome, httpStatus: r.httpStatus ?? null, durationMs: r.durationMs, finishedAt: this.clock.now(), billable: billable ? 1 : 0, zeroCost: zeroCost ? 1 : 0 }).changes;
        if (changes !== 1) throw new Error(`reservation ${id} is no longer pending`);
        done = true;
      },
    };
  }
}
```

```ts
import type { Cell, CellKey, CellOutcome, DefinitionSnapshot, Estimate, Execution, ExecutionStatus, FxInfo, Iata, IsoDateTime, NormalisedItinerary, Observation, ObservationFields, Offer, PointOfSale, ProviderId, Provenance, Cabin, SourceKind, SuppliedField } from '@gmt/core';

/** Spec §7 `airports` row. */
export interface Airport { iata: Iata; icao: string | null; name: string; city: string; country: string; tz: string; lat: number; lon: number; metro: string | null; source: string; datasetVersion: string }
/** The vendored airports dataset plus the attributed metro-code list (decision 11). */
export interface AirportsDataset { version: string; airports: Array<Omit<Airport, 'source' | 'datasetVersion'>>; metros: Record<string, string[]> }
/** Repositories refuse synthetic rows unless this is true (`syntheticAllowed(config)`, spec §5.5). */
export interface RepoOptions { allowSynthetic: boolean }
export type ExecutionCreateInput = { definitionId: string; isChild: boolean; cell: CellKey | null; snapshot: DefinitionSnapshot; estimate: Estimate | null; trigger: Execution['trigger']; status: 'pending' | 'running' | (ExecutionStatus & `skipped_${string}`) };
export type ExecutionPatch = Partial<Pick<Execution, 'stage' | 'resumeAfter' | 'errorText' | 'startedAt' | 'finishedAt' | 'cancelRequested' | 'estimate' | 'snapshot'>>;
/** One discovery tile's write (spec §7 atomic unit). `discards` is validated and reported by the engine, not stored. */
export interface TileWrite {
  tileIndex: number; cells: CellKey[]; prices: CalendarPriceInsert[];
  outcomes: Array<Pick<CellOutcome, 'origin' | 'destination' | 'departureDate' | 'returnDate' | 'status' | 'reason' | 'lastError' | 'fetchedAt' | 'minPence' | 'cabin' | 'pointOfSale' | 'sourceKind'>>;
  discards: number;
}
/** A calendar price to write; `maxAgeHours: null` means unknown and is written explicitly (spec §7). */
export interface CalendarPriceInsert {
  providerId: ProviderId; sourceKind: SourceKind; cabin: Cabin; cell: CellKey; pricePence: number; priceNativeMinor: number; currencyNative: string; fx: FxInfo;
  pointOfSale: PointOfSale; fetchedAt: IsoDateTime; providerObservedAt: IsoDateTime | null; maxAgeHours: number | null; vendorLink: string | null; reusedFromExecutionId: string | null;
}
export interface CalendarPriceRow extends CalendarPriceInsert { id: number; executionId: string; provenance: Provenance }
/** One verified cell's write (spec §7 atomic unit). best_observation_id becomes the cheapest observation written (ruling 13). */
export interface VerifiedCellWrite {
  cell: CellKey; outcome: TileWrite['outcomes'][number];
  itineraries: Array<{ itinerary: NormalisedItinerary; offers: Array<{ offer: Offer; observation: ObservationFields & { provenance: Provenance; suppliedFields: SuppliedField[]; partialExpansion: boolean } }> }>;
}
/** A reuse source for one cell (spec §8.1 reuse). `price` is null for empty and no_data outcomes. */
export interface ReusedCopy { cell: CellKey; outcome: CellOutcome; price: CalendarPriceRow | null }
export interface ObservationRow extends Observation { offer: Offer & { id: number }; itinerary: NormalisedItinerary; cell: Cell; seller: string }
```

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { type CellKey, type ExecutionBudget, asIata, asIsoDate } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../../src/client.js';
import { T0 } from './db.js';

// The worked example only (brief rules): London → New York.
export const LHR = asIata('LHR');
export const LGW = asIata('LGW');
export const JFK = asIata('JFK');
export const EWR = asIata('EWR');

export function cell(origin = LHR, destination = JFK, departureDate = '2027-03-14', returnDate = '2027-03-28'): CellKey {
  return { origin, destination, departureDate: asIsoDate(departureDate), returnDate: asIsoDate(returnDate) };
}

/**
 * A definition and an execution inserted with raw SQL; the snapshot holds only the budget the guard reads
 * (plan Budget port: per-execution caps come from the snapshot).
 */
export function rawExecution(db: Db, budget: ExecutionBudget, opts: { isChild?: boolean } = {}): { definitionId: string; executionId: string } {
  const definitionId = randomUUID();
  const executionId = randomUUID();
  db.raw
    .prepare(
      `INSERT INTO search_definitions (id, name, origins_json, destinations_json, window_json, min_nights, max_nights, cabin, constraints_json,
        verification_policy_json, budget_json, providers_json, schedule_tz, enabled, created_at, updated_at)
       VALUES (@id, 'London to New York', '["LHR","LGW"]', '["JFK","EWR"]', '{"kind":"relative","startOffsetDays":7,"lengthDays":182}', 10, 21, 'economy', '{}',
        '{"maxCells":10,"topN":10,"alwaysInclude":[],"expandTop":2,"bookingLinks":true}', @budget, '{"discovery":[],"verification":[],"discoveryRefreshHours":{}}', 'Europe/London', 1, @at, @at)`,
    )
    .run({ id: definitionId, budget: JSON.stringify(budget), at: T0 });
  const c = cell();
  const child = opts.isChild === true;
  db.raw
    .prepare(
      `INSERT INTO search_executions (id, definition_id, is_child, child_origin, child_destination, child_departure_date, child_return_date, definition_snapshot_json, trigger, status, created_at, started_at)
       VALUES (@id, @definitionId, @isChild, @o, @d, @dep, @ret, @snapshot, 'manual', 'running', @at, @at)`,
    )
    .run({
      id: executionId, definitionId, isChild: child ? 1 : 0, o: child ? c.origin : null, d: child ? c.destination : null, dep: child ? c.departureDate : null, ret: child ? c.returnDate : null,
      snapshot: JSON.stringify({ budget }), at: T0,
    });
  return { definitionId, executionId };
}
```

`packages/db/test/budgetGuard.predicates.test.ts`:

```ts
import { type BudgetGuard, BudgetExceeded, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { BudgetConfig } from '../src/budgetConfig.js';
import { SqliteBudgetGuard } from '../src/budgetGuard.js';
import { applyMigrations } from '../src/migrate.js';
import { MIGRATIONS_DIR } from '../src/paths.js';
import { T0, count, freshDb, openTracked, sqliteCode, tmpDbPath } from './helpers/db.js';
import { budgetConfig, limits, rawExecution } from './helpers/factories.js';

type Req = Parameters<BudgetGuard['reserve']>[0];
const sub40 = limits({ planFixedMicroGbp: 32_000_000 });
const perCall = limits({ costPerCallMicroGbp: 3200 });
const tp = limits({ billable: false, monthlyCallCap: 50_000 });
const fixture = limits({ costPerCallMicroGbp: 3200 });
const trial = limits({ window: 'lifetime', monthlyCallCap: 100, trialUsed: 9 }); // effective lifetime cap 91
const req = (providerId: Req['providerId'], executionId: string | null, costMicroGbp = 0): Req => ({ providerId, executionId, tileIndex: null, costMicroGbp, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/api/v1/search' });
const ok = { outcome: 'ok', httpStatus: 200, durationMs: 1 } as const;

/** test-007: a refused reserve throws the expected breach and changes neither the ledger nor any usage figure. */
function expectRefused(guard: SqliteBudgetGuard, db: ReturnType<typeof freshDb>, r: Req, match: object): void {
  const rows = count(db, 'provider_requests');
  const before = guard.usage({ kind: 'global-month' });
  expect(() => guard.reserve(r)).toThrow(expect.objectContaining(match));
  expect(count(db, 'provider_requests')).toBe(rows);
  expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'global-month' })).toEqual(before);
}

describe('cost rule (ruling 6): the configured rate is the only cost the ledger accepts', () => {
  it('refuses a cost that differs from the provider rate, in both directions, writing nothing', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: perCall, travelpayouts: tp }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 1_000_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 0))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'cost' })); // per-call plan, cost 0
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', null, 1))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'cost' })); // free vendor, cost 1
    expect(count(db, 'provider_requests')).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('billable rule at finalise (spec §5.3, cost-004)', () => {
  it.each([
    // provider, outcome, status, → stored billable, stored cost_micro_gbp, counted in provider scope, counted in global
    ['travelpayouts', 'ok', 200, 0, 0, 1, 0],
    ['fixture', 'ok', 200, 1, 3200, 1, 1],
    ['fixture', 'error', 500, 0, 0, 0, 0],
    ['searchapi', 'ok', 204, 0, 0, 0, 0],
    ['searchapi', 'ok', 200, 1, 3200, 1, 1],
    ['searchapi', 'unknown', undefined, 0, 3200, 1, 1],
  ] as const)('%s %s %s → billable %i, stored cost %i', (providerId, outcome, httpStatus, billable, cost, inProvider, inGlobal) => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ travelpayouts: tp, fixture, searchapi: perCall }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 1_000_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    const rate = providerId === 'travelpayouts' ? 0 : 3200;
    guard.reserve(req(providerId, null, rate)).finalise(httpStatus === undefined ? { outcome, durationMs: 1 } : { outcome, httpStatus, durationMs: 1 });
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT billable, cost_micro_gbp AS cost FROM provider_requests').get()).toEqual({ billable, cost });
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'provider-month', providerId }).calls).toBe(inProvider);
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'global-month' }).calls).toBe(inGlobal);
  });
});

describe('cap predicates (spec §5.3)', () => {
  it('provider scope: pending and unknown count against a billed provider; error rows do not', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: limits({ monthlyCallCap: 3, costPerCallMicroGbp: 3200 }) }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 1_000_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)); // left pending
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)).finalise({ outcome: 'unknown', durationMs: 60_000 });
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)).finalise({ outcome: 'error', httpStatus: 500, durationMs: 5 });
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'provider-month', providerId: 'searchapi' }).calls).toBe(2);
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)).finalise(ok);
    expectRefused(guard, db, req('searchapi', null, 3200), { scope: 'provider-month', cap: 3, current: 3 });
  });

  it('global scope: pending and unknown count at full cost; error, free-vendor and cache-hit rows add nothing', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: perCall, travelpayouts: tp }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 12_800 }), fixedClock(T0));
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)); // pending: 3,200
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)).finalise({ outcome: 'unknown', durationMs: 1 }); // 6,400
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)).finalise({ outcome: 'error', httpStatus: 503, durationMs: 1 }); // 0
    guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', null)).finalise(ok);
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId: null, tileIndex: null, endpointPath: '/api/v1/search' });
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'global-month' })).toMatchObject({ calls: 2, costMicroGbp: 6400 });
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)).finalise(ok); // 9,600
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)).finalise(ok); // 12,800: at the cap exactly
    expectRefused(guard, db, req('searchapi', null, 3200), { scope: 'global-month', cap: 12_800, current: 12_800, requested: 3200 });
  });

  it('global fee counts once per enabled subscription provider; with no subscription provider configured the fee is 0', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const withFee = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: sub40, travelpayouts: tp }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 3_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    expect(withFee.usage({ kind: 'global-month' })).toMatchObject({ calls: 0, costMicroGbp: 32_000_000, cap: 3_000_000 });
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) withFee.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 0)).finalise(ok); // zero-cost calls pass although the fee alone exceeds the cap
    expect(withFee.usage({ kind: 'global-month' })).toMatchObject({ calls: 5, costMicroGbp: 32_000_000 });
    const feePlusRate = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: limits({ planFixedMicroGbp: 32_000_000, costPerCallMicroGbp: 1 }) }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 3_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    expectRefused(feePlusRate, db, req('searchapi', null, 1), { scope: 'global-month', current: 32_000_000, requested: 1 });
    expect(new SqliteBudgetGuard(freshDb(), budgetConfig({ travelpayouts: tp }), fixedClock(T0)).usage({ kind: 'global-month' }).costMicroGbp).toBe(0);
  });

  it('execution scopes: billed and free counted separately; pending and unknown count; error and cache hits do not', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const { executionId } = rawExecution(db, { maxCalls: 3, maxFreeCalls: 3, maxCostMicroGbp: 0 });
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: sub40, travelpayouts: tp }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 35_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', executionId)).finalise(ok);
    expectRefused(guard, db, req('travelpayouts', executionId), { scope: 'execution-free-calls', cap: 3, current: 3 });
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', executionId)); // pending
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', executionId)).finalise({ outcome: 'error', httpStatus: 500, durationMs: 1 }); // not counted
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId, tileIndex: null, endpointPath: '/api/v1/search' }); // not counted
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', executionId)).finalise({ outcome: 'unknown', durationMs: 1 });
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', executionId)).finalise(ok);
    expectRefused(guard, db, req('searchapi', executionId), { scope: 'execution-calls', cap: 3, current: 3 });
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'execution-calls', executionId })).toMatchObject({ calls: 3, cap: 3, windowStart: null });
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'execution-free-calls', executionId })).toMatchObject({ calls: 3, cap: 3 });
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'execution-cost', executionId })).toMatchObject({ costMicroGbp: 0, cap: 0 });
  });

  it('execution-cost: zero-cost calls pass a 0 cap on a 0-rate plan; a 3,200 rate is refused by a 0 cap; 6,400 allows two', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const zero = rawExecution(db, { maxCalls: 100, maxFreeCalls: 100, maxCostMicroGbp: 0 }).executionId;
    const some = rawExecution(db, { maxCalls: 100, maxFreeCalls: 100, maxCostMicroGbp: 6_400 }).executionId;
    const subGuard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: limits() }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 1_000_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    subGuard.reserve(req('searchapi', zero, 0)).finalise(ok);
    const perCallGuard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: perCall }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 1_000_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    expectRefused(perCallGuard, db, req('searchapi', zero, 3200), { scope: 'execution-cost', cap: 0, current: 0, requested: 3200 });
    perCallGuard.reserve(req('searchapi', some, 3200)).finalise(ok);
    perCallGuard.reserve(req('searchapi', some, 3200)).finalise(ok);
    expectRefused(perCallGuard, db, req('searchapi', some, 3200), { scope: 'execution-cost', current: 6_400 });
  });

  it('a child execution is capped by its own snapshot: CHILD_MAX_CALLS for calls, the inherited free and cost caps for the rest', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const { executionId } = rawExecution(db, { maxCalls: 6, maxFreeCalls: 2000, maxCostMicroGbp: 0 }, { isChild: true });
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: sub40, travelpayouts: tp }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 35_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) guard.reserve(req('searchapi', executionId)).finalise(ok);
    expectRefused(guard, db, req('searchapi', executionId), { scope: 'execution-calls', cap: 6 });
    guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', executionId)).finalise(ok); // inherited free allowance
  });

  it('a call cap of 0 refuses the first call', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    expectRefused(new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: limits({ monthlyCallCap: 0 }) }), fixedClock(T0)), db, req('searchapi', null), { scope: 'provider-month' });
  });

  it('lifetime window: every row ever counts against the effective cap; the month scope reports cap null', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const insert = db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, endpoint_path, started_at, finished_at, outcome, billable, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt, http_status, duration_ms) VALUES ('searchapi', '/api/v1/search', @at, @at, 'ok', 1, 0, 1, 200, 1)`);
    for (const month of ['2026-08', '2026-09', '2026-10']) for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) insert.run({ at: `${month}-02T00:00:00.000Z` });
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: trial }), fixedClock(T0));
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'provider-lifetime', providerId: 'searchapi' })).toEqual({ calls: 90, costMicroGbp: 0, cap: 91, windowStart: null, windowEnd: null });
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null)).finalise(ok);
    expectRefused(guard, db, req('searchapi', null), { scope: 'provider-lifetime', cap: 91, current: 91 });
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'provider-month', providerId: 'searchapi' })).toMatchObject({ calls: 31, cap: null, windowStart: '2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z' });
    expect(new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: sub40 }), fixedClock(T0)).usage({ kind: 'provider-lifetime', providerId: 'searchapi' })).toMatchObject({ cap: null, windowStart: null });
  });

  it('fails closed: unconfigured provider, unknown execution, double finalise', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: sub40 }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 35_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', null))).toThrow(/no budget configuration/);
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('searchapi', 'no-such-execution'))).toThrow(/no-such-execution/);
    const r = guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null));
    r.finalise(ok);
    expect(() => r.finalise(ok)).toThrow(/already finalised/);
    expect(count(db, 'provider_requests')).toBe(1);
  });

  it('endpoint_path is a path only, on both writers and in SQL (sec-002, test-006)', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: sub40 }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 35_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    for (const bad of ['/x?api_key=SECRET', '//h/x', '/x#t', '/x@y', 'x', '/a b']) {
      expect(() => guard.reserve({ ...req('searchapi', null), endpointPath: bad })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'endpoint-path' }));
      expect(() => guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId: null, tileIndex: null, endpointPath: bad })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'endpoint-path' }));
    }
    const raw = (path: string) => () =>
      db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, endpoint_path, started_at, outcome, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt) VALUES ('searchapi', ?, ?, 'ok', 0, 1)`).run(path, T0);
    expect(sqliteCode(raw('/x?api_key=SECRET'))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(raw('no-leading-slash'))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    for (const bad of ['//h/x', '/x#t', '/x@y', `/${'a'.repeat(200)}`]) expect(sqliteCode(raw(bad)), bad).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    raw(`/${'a'.repeat(199)}`)(); // 200 characters: the longest path allowed
    expect(count(db, 'provider_requests')).toBe(1);
  });

  it('every ledger row carries tile_index as given, NULL for verification (spec §5.3)', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ travelpayouts: tp }), fixedClock(T0));
    guard.reserve({ ...req('travelpayouts', null), tileIndex: 4 }).finalise(ok);
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId: null, tileIndex: 5, endpointPath: '/aviasales/v3/prices_for_dates' });
    guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', null)).finalise(ok);
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT tile_index AS t FROM provider_requests ORDER BY id').all()).toEqual([{ t: 4 }, { t: 5 }, { t: null }]);
  });
});

describe('BEGIN IMMEDIATE: the predicates run under the write lock (spec §7, cost-001, test-004)', () => {
  /** Runs `probe` after the predicates and before the insert, inside reserve's transaction. */
  class ProbeGuard extends SqliteBudgetGuard {
    probe: () => void = () => undefined;
    protected override checkCaps(...args: Parameters<SqliteBudgetGuard['checkCaps']>): void {
      super.checkCaps(...args);
      this.probe();
    }
  }
  const fileDbs = () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const a = openTracked(path);
    applyMigrations(a, MIGRATIONS_DIR);
    const b = openTracked(path);
    b.raw.pragma('busy_timeout = 50');
    return { a, b };
  };
  const cfg: BudgetConfig = budgetConfig({ searchapi: limits({ monthlyCallCap: 1, costPerCallMicroGbp: 3200 }) }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 1_000_000_000 });

  it('while reserve is deciding, another connection cannot write: the lock is already held', () => {
    const { a, b } = fileDbs();
    const guard = new ProbeGuard(a, cfg, fixedClock(T0));
    let code: string | null = 'not probed';
    guard.probe = () => {
      code = sqliteCode(() => b.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, endpoint_path, started_at, outcome, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt) VALUES ('searchapi', '/x', ?, 'pending', 3200, 1)`).run(T0));
    };
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200));
    expect(code).toBe('SQLITE_BUSY');
    expect(count(a, 'provider_requests')).toBe(1);
  });

  it('a reserve on a second connection while the first holds the lock fails busy and inserts nothing', () => {
    const { a, b } = fileDbs();
    a.raw.prepare('BEGIN IMMEDIATE').run();
    expect(sqliteCode(() => new SqliteBudgetGuard(b, cfg, fixedClock(T0)).reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)))).toBe('SQLITE_BUSY');
    a.raw.prepare('ROLLBACK').run();
    expect(count(a, 'provider_requests')).toBe(0);
  });

  it('two connections at a cap of one call: the second sees the first and is refused', () => {
    const { a, b } = fileDbs();
    new SqliteBudgetGuard(a, cfg, fixedClock(T0)).reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200));
    expect(() => new SqliteBudgetGuard(b, cfg, fixedClock(T0)).reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200))).toThrow(BudgetExceeded);
    expect(count(a, 'provider_requests')).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

`// why:` the race is proven deterministically with the probe rather than two worker threads: the guard imports workspace TypeScript (`@gmt/core`), which a raw worker cannot load, and the probe distinguishes `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` from a deferred transaction exactly (under a deferred transaction the probe's insert succeeds). `Database` is imported for the `instanceof` narrowing in `sqliteCode`.

In `src/budgetGuard.ts`, add (import `type ExecutionBudget` from `@gmt/core`):

```ts
/** Spec §5.3 execution scopes: rows of this execution for the given providers, billed or free rule. */
export const EXECUTION_USAGE_SQL = `
SELECT COUNT(*) AS calls, COALESCE(SUM(cost_micro_gbp), 0) AS cost
FROM provider_requests
WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND provider_id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@providers)) AND outcome <> 'cache_hit'
  AND (@billed = 0 OR outcome IN ('pending', 'unknown') OR billable = 1)`;

/** Plan Budget port: per-execution caps come from the snapshot budget, never from config at reserve time. */
const EXECUTION_CAPS_SQL = `
SELECT json_extract(definition_snapshot_json, '$.budget.maxCalls') AS maxCalls,
       json_extract(definition_snapshot_json, '$.budget.maxFreeCalls') AS maxFreeCalls,
       json_extract(definition_snapshot_json, '$.budget.maxCostMicroGbp') AS maxCostMicroGbp
FROM search_executions WHERE id = ?`;
```

and replace `checkCaps` and `executionUsage`:

```ts
  // why: check order is provider (the vendor's hard stop), then global (total spend), then the execution's own caps;
  // one breach is reported and the engine's error table (spec §8.1) acts on its scope.
  protected checkCaps(p: ReserveParams, budget: ProviderBudget, now: IsoDateTime): void {
    const prov = this.providerUsage(p.providerId, budget, now, budget.window);
    const callCap = effectiveCallCap(budget);
    if (prov.calls + 1 > callCap) throw this.exceeded(budget.window === 'lifetime' ? 'provider-lifetime' : 'provider-month', callCap, prov.calls, 1, p.providerId);
    if (budget.billable) {
      const g = this.globalUsage(now);
      const cap = this.config.spend.monthlyCapMicroGbp;
      if (p.costMicroGbp > 0 && g.cost + p.costMicroGbp > cap) throw this.exceeded('global-month', cap, g.cost, p.costMicroGbp, p.providerId);
    }
    if (p.executionId === null) return;
    const caps = this.executionCaps(p.executionId);
    if (budget.billable) {
      const u = this.executionRows(p.executionId, this.providersWhere(true), true);
      if (u.calls + 1 > caps.maxCalls) throw this.exceeded('execution-calls', caps.maxCalls, u.calls, 1, p.providerId);
      if (p.costMicroGbp > 0 && u.cost + p.costMicroGbp > caps.maxCostMicroGbp) throw this.exceeded('execution-cost', caps.maxCostMicroGbp, u.cost, p.costMicroGbp, p.providerId);
    } else {
      const u = this.executionRows(p.executionId, this.providersWhere(false), false);
      if (u.calls + 1 > caps.maxFreeCalls) throw this.exceeded('execution-free-calls', caps.maxFreeCalls, u.calls, 1, p.providerId);
    }
  }

  protected executionUsage(kind: 'execution-calls' | 'execution-free-calls' | 'execution-cost', executionId: string): Usage {
    const caps = this.executionCaps(executionId);
    const billed = kind !== 'execution-free-calls';
    const u = this.executionRows(executionId, this.providersWhere(billed), billed);
    const cap = kind === 'execution-calls' ? caps.maxCalls : kind === 'execution-free-calls' ? caps.maxFreeCalls : caps.maxCostMicroGbp;
    return { calls: u.calls, costMicroGbp: u.cost, cap, windowStart: null, windowEnd: null };
  }

  private executionRows(executionId: string, providers: ProviderId[], billed: boolean): CallsAndCost {
    const row = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CallsAndCost>(EXECUTION_USAGE_SQL).get({ executionId, providers: JSON.stringify(providers), billed: billed ? 1 : 0 });
    return { calls: row?.calls ?? 0, cost: row?.cost ?? 0 };
  }

  private executionCaps(executionId: string): ExecutionBudget {
    const row = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { maxCalls: unknown; maxFreeCalls: unknown; maxCostMicroGbp: unknown }>(EXECUTION_CAPS_SQL).get(executionId);
    if (row === undefined) throw new Error(`execution ${executionId} does not exist; refusing to reserve`);
    const { maxCalls, maxFreeCalls, maxCostMicroGbp } = row;
    if (typeof maxCalls !== 'number' || typeof maxFreeCalls !== 'number' || typeof maxCostMicroGbp !== 'number') throw new Error(`execution ${executionId} snapshot has no budget; refusing to reserve`);
    return { maxCalls, maxFreeCalls, maxCostMicroGbp };
  }
```

Append to `packages/db/src/budgetConfig.ts` (import `syntheticAllowed` as a value from `@gmt/core`):

```ts
/** The fixture provider's guard entry: billable at the configured test rate, a finite test cap (no config field exists for it). */
const FIXTURE_LIMITS: ProviderLimits = {
  monthlyCallCap: 100_000, window: 'month', billingDay: 1, ratePerMinute: 6000, ratePerHour: 100_000, concurrency: 4, cacheTtlHours: 1,
  costPerCallMicroGbp: 0, planFixedMicroGbp: 0, excludedFromSchedule: false,
};

/**
 * Builds the guard's configuration from the parsed config (spec §12). Only configured providers appear.
 * `trialUsed` carries `SEARCHAPI_TRIAL_USED` for a lifetime plan (decision 14). The fixture's rate is
 * `FIXTURE_COST_MICRO_GBP`, read only from `providers.fixture.costPerCallMicroGbp` (ruling 6).
 */
export function budgetConfigFromConfig(c: Config): BudgetConfig {
  const providers: BudgetConfig['providers'] = {};
  const tp = c.providers.travelpayouts;
  if (tp !== null) providers.travelpayouts = { ...tp.limits, billable: false, trialUsed: 0 };
  const sa = c.providers.searchapi;
  if (sa !== null) providers.searchapi = { ...sa.limits, billable: true, trialUsed: sa.limits.window === 'lifetime' ? sa.trialUsed : 0 };
  const fixture = c.providers.fixture;
  if (fixture !== null && syntheticAllowed(c)) providers.fixture = { ...FIXTURE_LIMITS, costPerCallMicroGbp: fixture.costMicroGbp, billable: true, trialUsed: 0 };
  return { providers, spend: c.spend };
}
```

### Task 3.4: `LedgerRepo` and the pending sweep

**Files:**
- Create: `src/repositories/ledger.ts`, `test/ledger.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `LedgerRepo(db)` with `countsFor`, `firstAttemptTiles`, `sweepPending`, `pruneCacheHits`.

Meanings (ruling 7, cost-005): `calls` = upstream attempts (non-cache rows), not cap usage, which comes from `BudgetGuard.usage`; `tiles` = distinct (provider, tile_index) over first-attempt **upstream** rows; `cacheHits` = cache-hit rows; spec §8.1 parity = `tiles + cacheHits`. A row's stage is derived from `tile_index` (non-NULL discovery, NULL verification; spec §5.3); `stage: null` never occurs for an execution's rows.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```ts
import { fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { SqliteBudgetGuard } from '../src/budgetGuard.js';
import { LedgerRepo } from '../src/repositories/ledger.js';
import { T0, at, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { budgetConfig, limits, rawExecution } from './helpers/factories.js';

const cfg = budgetConfig({ searchapi: limits({ costPerCallMicroGbp: 3200 }), travelpayouts: limits({ billable: false, monthlyCallCap: 50_000 }) }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 1_000_000_000 });
const ok = { outcome: 'ok', httpStatus: 200, durationMs: 1 } as const;

describe('LedgerRepo (spec §5.3, §7, §8.1 parity)', () => {
  it('sweepPending: pending rows older than the cutoff become unknown and stay counted', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, cfg, clock);
    guard.reserve({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId: null, tileIndex: null, costMicroGbp: 3200, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/s' }); // 12:00, left pending
    clock.advance(9 * 60_000);
    guard.reserve({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId: null, tileIndex: null, costMicroGbp: 3200, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/s' }); // 12:09
    expect(new LedgerRepo(db).sweepPending(at('2026-10-14T12:01:00.000Z'))).toBe(1); // boot 12:11 − 10 min
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT outcome FROM provider_requests ORDER BY id').all()).toEqual([{ outcome: 'unknown' }, { outcome: 'pending' }]);
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'provider-month', providerId: 'searchapi' })).toMatchObject({ calls: 2, costMicroGbp: 6400 });
  });

  it('countsFor: upstream calls, cost, first-attempt upstream tiles and cache hits, total and per (provider, stage)', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const { executionId } = rawExecution(db, { maxCalls: 100, maxFreeCalls: 100, maxCostMicroGbp: 100_000 });
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, cfg, fixedClock(T0));
    const tp = (tileIndex: number, httpAttempt: number) => guard.reserve({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId, tileIndex, costMicroGbp: 0, httpAttempt, endpointPath: '/p' }).finalise(ok);
    tp(0, 1); tp(0, 1); // tile 0, two pages
    tp(1, 1); tp(1, 2); // tile 1, one retry
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId, tileIndex: 2, endpointPath: '/p' }); // tile 2 answered by the cache
    guard.reserve({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId, tileIndex: null, costMicroGbp: 3200, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/s' }).finalise(ok);
    const ledger = new LedgerRepo(db);
    const c = ledger.countsFor(executionId);
    expect([c.calls, c.costMicroGbp, c.tiles, c.cacheHits]).toEqual([5, 3200, 2, 1]); // parity = tiles + cacheHits = 3
    expect(c.perProvider).toEqual([
      { providerId: 'searchapi', stage: 'verification', calls: 1, costMicroGbp: 3200, tiles: 0, cacheHits: 0 },
      { providerId: 'travelpayouts', stage: 'discovery', calls: 4, costMicroGbp: 0, tiles: 2, cacheHits: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(ledger.firstAttemptTiles(executionId, 'travelpayouts')).toBe(2);
    expect(ledger.firstAttemptTiles(executionId, 'searchapi')).toBe(0);
    expect(ledger.countsFor('none')).toEqual({ calls: 0, costMicroGbp: 0, tiles: 0, cacheHits: 0, perProvider: [] });
  });

  it('pruneCacheHits removes only cache_hit rows older than the cutoff for the named providers', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(at('2026-06-01T00:00:00.000Z'));
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, cfg, clock);
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId: null, tileIndex: 0, endpointPath: '/p' });
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId: null, tileIndex: null, endpointPath: '/s' });
    guard.reserve({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId: null, tileIndex: 0, costMicroGbp: 0, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/p' }).finalise(ok);
    expect(new LedgerRepo(db).pruneCacheHits(T0, ['travelpayouts'])).toBe(1);
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT provider_id AS p, outcome AS o FROM provider_requests ORDER BY id').all()).toEqual([{ p: 'searchapi', o: 'cache_hit' }, { p: 'travelpayouts', o: 'ok' }]);
    expect(new LedgerRepo(db).pruneCacheHits(T0, [])).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```ts
import type { IsoDateTime, ProviderId, Stage } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { PROVIDER_IDS, STAGES } from '../internal/enums.js';
import { oneOf } from '../internal/read.js';
import { assertIsoDateTime } from '../internal/validate.js';

/** Distinct tiles fetched upstream on a first attempt (ruling 7); cache hits are counted separately so parity = tiles + cacheHits. */
const TILE_EXPR = `COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN tile_index IS NOT NULL AND http_attempt = 1 AND outcome <> 'cache_hit' THEN provider_id || ':' || tile_index END)`;
const COUNTS = `COALESCE(SUM(outcome <> 'cache_hit'), 0) AS calls, COALESCE(SUM(cost_micro_gbp), 0) AS cost, ${TILE_EXPR} AS tiles, COALESCE(SUM(outcome = 'cache_hit'), 0) AS cacheHits`;
interface CountsRow { calls: number; cost: number; tiles: number; cacheHits: number }

/** Reads and housekeeping over `provider_requests` (spec §7). Inserts and finalisation belong to `SqliteBudgetGuard`. */
export class LedgerRepo {
  constructor(private readonly db: Db) {}

  /**
   * Actuals for one execution, total and per (provider, stage). `calls` are upstream attempts, not cap usage
   * (cap usage comes from `BudgetGuard.usage`); spec §8.1 parity is `tiles + cacheHits`.
   */
  countsFor(executionId: string): { calls: number; costMicroGbp: number; tiles: number; cacheHits: number; perProvider: Array<{ providerId: ProviderId; stage: Stage | null; calls: number; costMicroGbp: number; tiles: number; cacheHits: number }> } {
    const total = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], CountsRow>(`SELECT ${COUNTS} FROM provider_requests WHERE execution_id = ?`).get(executionId);
    const rows = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], CountsRow & { providerId: string; stage: string }>(
        `SELECT provider_id AS providerId, CASE WHEN tile_index IS NULL THEN 'verification' ELSE 'discovery' END AS stage, ${COUNTS}
         FROM provider_requests WHERE execution_id = ? GROUP BY providerId, stage ORDER BY providerId, stage`,
      )
      .all(executionId);
    return {
      calls: total?.calls ?? 0, costMicroGbp: total?.cost ?? 0, tiles: total?.tiles ?? 0, cacheHits: total?.cacheHits ?? 0,
      perProvider: rows.map((r) => ({ providerId: oneOf('provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS, r.providerId), stage: oneOf('stage', STAGES, r.stage), calls: r.calls, costMicroGbp: r.cost, tiles: r.tiles, cacheHits: r.cacheHits })),
    };
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 parity numerator for one provider (add its cache hits for the full count). */
  firstAttemptTiles(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId): number {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string, string], { n: number }>(`SELECT ${TILE_EXPR} AS n FROM provider_requests WHERE execution_id = ? AND provider_id = ?`).get(executionId, providerId)?.n ?? 0;
  }

  /** Spec §5.3 "boot sweep marks pending older than 10 minutes unknown (still counted)". The caller passes boot − 10 min. */
  sweepPending(olderThan: IsoDateTime): number {
    assertIsoDateTime('olderThan', olderThan);
    return this.db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE provider_requests SET outcome = 'unknown' WHERE outcome = 'pending' AND started_at < ?`).run(olderThan).changes;
  }

  /** Spec §7 retention: prune `cache_hit` rows older than the cutoff for the named (monthly-window) providers. */
  pruneCacheHits(olderThan: IsoDateTime, providerIds: ProviderId[]): number {
    assertIsoDateTime('olderThan', olderThan);
    if (providerIds.length === 0) return 0;
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare(`DELETE FROM provider_requests WHERE outcome = 'cache_hit' AND started_at < @olderThan AND provider_id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@ids))`)
      .run({ olderThan, ids: JSON.stringify(providerIds) }).changes;
  }
}
```

```ts
    expect(seedAirports(db, dataset(rows, 'v1'))).toEqual({ inserted: 3, datasetVersion: 'v1' });
    expect(seedAirports(db, dataset(rows, 'v1'))).toEqual({ inserted: 0, datasetVersion: 'v1' });

describe('AirportsRepo', () => {
  const seeded = (rows: Row[], metros: Record<string, string[]> = {}) => {
    const repo = new AirportsRepo(freshDb());
    repo.upsertAll(dataset(rows, 'test', metros));
    return repo;
  };

    const repo = seeded([row('AAA'), row('AAB'), row('BBB'), row('CCC', { tz: 'Europe/London' })], { AAX: ['AAA', 'AAB'], BBX: ['BBB'] });
    expect([repo.exists('AAA'), repo.exists('ZZZ')]).toEqual([true, false]);
    expect([repo.metroOf('AAA'), repo.metroOf('CCC')]).toEqual(['AAX', null]);
    expect(repo.airportsOfMetro('AAX')).toEqual(['AAA', 'AAB']);
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([asIata('AAA'), asIata('AAB')])).toBe('AAX');
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([asIata('AAA'), asIata('BBB')])).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([asIata('AAA'), asIata('CCC')])).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([asIata('AAA'), asIata('ZZZ')])).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([])).toBeNull();
    expect([repo.tzOf('CCC'), repo.tzOf('ZZZ')]).toEqual(['Europe/London', null]);
  });

  it('search ranks an exact code, then the members of a metro code, then a code prefix, then name or city; wildcards are literal', () => {
    const repo = seeded([row('AAB', { name: 'Alpha Field' }), row('AAA', { name: 'Beta Field' }), row('BAA', { name: 'Field AA' }), row('CCC', { name: '100% Synthetic' }), row('QQQ'), row('QQR')], { QQX: ['QQQ', 'QQR'] });
    expect(repo.search('aaa', 10).map((a) => a.iata)).toEqual(['AAA']);
    expect(repo.search('QQX', 10).map((a) => a.iata)).toEqual(['QQQ', 'QQR']); // po-003: a metro code lists its airports
    expect(repo.search('AA', 10).map((a) => a.iata)).toEqual(['AAA', 'AAB', 'BAA']);
    expect(repo.search('alpha', 10).map((a) => a.iata)).toEqual(['AAB']);
    expect(repo.search('%', 10).map((a) => a.iata)).toEqual(['CCC']);
    expect(repo.search('  ', 10)).toEqual([]);
    expect(repo.search('field', 1)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('rejects a malformed country, an unknown time zone and a malformed metro code, writing nothing', () => {
    // why: a malformed IATA code cannot be built without a cast (asIata throws), so the typed path proves the other rules.
    const repo = new AirportsRepo(freshDb());
    expect(() => repo.upsertAll(dataset([row('AAA'), { ...row('AAC'), country: 'zz' }]))).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(() => repo.upsertAll(dataset([row('AAA', { tz: 'Mars/Olympus_Mons' })]))).toThrow(/tz/);
    expect(() => repo.upsertAll(dataset([row('AAA')], 'v', { x1: ['AAA'] }))).toThrow(/metro/);
    expect(repo.count()).toBe(0);
    expect(repo.datasetVersion()).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`packages/db/src/schema/airports.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, index, real, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';

/** Spec §7 `airports`: mwgg/Airports (MIT) with `metro` from the vendored, attributed metro list. */
export const airports = sqliteTable(
  'airports',
  {
    iata: text('iata').primaryKey(),
    icao: text('icao'),
    name: text('name').notNull(),
    city: text('city').notNull(),
    country: text('country').notNull(),
    tz: text('tz').notNull(),
    lat: real('lat').notNull(),
    lon: real('lon').notNull(),
    metro: text('metro'),
    source: text('source').notNull(),
    datasetVersion: text('dataset_version').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    check('airports_iata_ck', sql.raw("iata GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]'")),
    check('airports_metro_ck', sql.raw("metro IS NULL OR metro GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]'")),
    check('airports_country_ck', sql.raw("country GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z]'")),
    check('airports_tz_ck', sql.raw('length(tz) > 0')),
    index('airports_metro_idx').on(t.metro),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/airports.ts`:

```ts
import type { Iata } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { readIata } from '../internal/read.js';
import { RowRejected } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { Airport, AirportsDataset } from '../types.js';

interface AirportDbRow { iata: string; icao: string | null; name: string; city: string; country: string; tz: string; lat: number; lon: number; metro: string | null; source: string; dataset_version: string }
const toAirport = (r: AirportDbRow): Airport => ({
  iata: readIata('airports.iata', r.iata), icao: r.icao, name: r.name, city: r.city, country: r.country, tz: r.tz, lat: r.lat, lon: r.lon, metro: r.metro, source: r.source, datasetVersion: r.dataset_version,
});
const IATA = /^[A-Z]{3}$/;

function isKnownTimeZone(tz: string): boolean {
  try {
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en', { timeZone: tz });
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/** Metro code per airport; an airport in two metros is a data error, never a guess. Used at build, load and seed. */
export function metroIndex(metros: Record<string, string[]>): Map<string, string> {
  const out = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const [code, members] of Object.entries(metros)) {
    if (!IATA.test(code)) throw new RowRejected('metro', `metro code must match ^[A-Z]{3}$, got ${code}`);
    for (const m of members) {
      const prior = out.get(m);
      if (prior !== undefined && prior !== code) throw new RowRejected('metro', `${m} is listed under both ${prior} and ${code}`);
      out.set(m, code);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Airport lookups for validation, tiling and normalisation (spec §5.2, §5.6, §6.1). No airport is named in code. */
export class AirportsRepo {
  constructor(private readonly db: Db) {}

  exists(iata: string): boolean {
    return this.db.raw.prepare('SELECT 1 AS present FROM airports WHERE iata = ?').get(iata) !== undefined;
  }

  get(iata: string): Airport | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], AirportDbRow>('SELECT * FROM airports WHERE iata = ?').get(iata);
    return r === undefined ? null : toAirport(r);
  }

  tzOf(iata: string): string | null {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { tz: string }>('SELECT tz FROM airports WHERE iata = ?').get(iata)?.tz ?? null;
  }

  /** Autocomplete (spec §11, `GET /api/airports`): exact code, then a metro code's members (po-003), then code prefix, then name/city substring. */
  search(q: string, limit: number): Airport[] {
    const needle = q.trim();
    if (needle.length === 0) return [];
    // why: the operator's text is matched literally; % and _ are escaped for LIKE.
    const e = needle.replace(/[\\%_]/g, (c) => `\\${c}`);
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, AirportDbRow>(
        `SELECT * FROM airports
         WHERE iata = upper(@q) OR metro = upper(@q) OR iata LIKE upper(@e) || '%' ESCAPE '\\'
            OR name LIKE '%' || @e || '%' ESCAPE '\\' OR city LIKE '%' || @e || '%' ESCAPE '\\'
         ORDER BY CASE WHEN iata = upper(@q) THEN 0 WHEN metro = upper(@q) THEN 1 WHEN iata LIKE upper(@e) || '%' ESCAPE '\\' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END, iata
         LIMIT @limit`,
      )
      .all({ q: needle, e, limit: Math.max(1, Math.trunc(limit)) })
      .map(toAirport);
  }

  metroOf(iata: string): string | null {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { metro: string | null }>('SELECT metro FROM airports WHERE iata = ?').get(iata)?.metro ?? null;
  }

  /** Every airport the table maps to a metro code (WP1 names them, plan Review Focus 1). */
  airportsOfMetro(code: string): Iata[] {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { iata: string }>('SELECT iata FROM airports WHERE metro = ? ORDER BY iata').all(code).map((r) => readIata('airports.iata', r.iata));
  }

  /** The one metro code every given airport shares, else null (spec §5.2). */
  sharedMetro(iatas: Iata[]): string | null {
    const unique = [...new Set(iatas)];
    if (unique.length === 0) return null;
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], { n: number; nulls: number; distinctMetros: number; metro: string | null }>(
        `SELECT COUNT(*) AS n, COALESCE(SUM(metro IS NULL), 0) AS nulls, COUNT(DISTINCT metro) AS distinctMetros, MIN(metro) AS metro FROM airports WHERE iata IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?))`,
      )
      .get(JSON.stringify(unique));
    if (r === undefined || r.n !== unique.length || r.nulls > 0 || r.distinctMetros !== 1) return null;
    return r.metro;
  }

  count(): number {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[], { n: number }>('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM airports').get()?.n ?? 0;
  }

  /** The version most rows carry, or null for an empty table. */
  datasetVersion(): string | null {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[], { v: string }>('SELECT dataset_version AS v FROM airports GROUP BY dataset_version ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC, v DESC LIMIT 1').get()?.v ?? null;
  }

  /** Upsert by IATA in one transaction; every row is validated first, so a bad dataset writes nothing. `metro` comes from `dataset.metros`. */
  upsertAll(dataset: AirportsDataset): void {
    const metros = metroIndex(dataset.metros);
    for (const a of dataset.airports) {
      if (!IATA.test(a.iata)) throw new RowRejected('iata', `airport code must match ^[A-Z]{3}$, got ${a.iata}`);
      if (!/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(a.country)) throw new RowRejected('country', `country must be ISO alpha-2, got ${a.country} for ${a.iata}`);
      if (!isKnownTimeZone(a.tz)) throw new RowRejected('tz', `unknown time zone ${a.tz} for ${a.iata}`);
    }
    const source = `mwgg/Airports ${dataset.version}`;
    const stmt = this.db.raw.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO airports (iata, icao, name, city, country, tz, lat, lon, metro, source, dataset_version)
       VALUES (@iata, @icao, @name, @city, @country, @tz, @lat, @lon, @metro, @source, @datasetVersion)
       ON CONFLICT(iata) DO UPDATE SET icao = excluded.icao, name = excluded.name, city = excluded.city, country = excluded.country, tz = excluded.tz,
         lat = excluded.lat, lon = excluded.lon, metro = excluded.metro, source = excluded.source, dataset_version = excluded.dataset_version`,
    );
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const a of dataset.airports) stmt.run({ ...a, metro: metros.get(a.iata) ?? null, source, datasetVersion: dataset.version });
    });
  }
}
```

`packages/db/src/seed/airports.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { type Iata, asIata } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { isRecord } from '../internal/read.js';
import { AirportsRepo, metroIndex } from '../repositories/airports.js';
import type { AirportsDataset } from '../types.js';

/** Spec §7: boot fails unless at least this many airports carry an IATA code and a tz. */
export const AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS = 5000;

function fail(file: string, field: string, detail: string): never {
  throw new Error(`${file}: ${field} ${detail}`);
}
function str(file: string, field: string, v: unknown): string {
  return typeof v === 'string' ? v : fail(file, field, 'must be a string');
}
function num(file: string, field: string, v: unknown): number {
  return typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? v : fail(file, field, 'must be a finite number');
}

/** Reads and validates the vendored files (po-007). No network is involved anywhere in seeding (spec §7). */
export function loadAirportsDataset(dir: string): AirportsDataset {
  const files = readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => /^airports\..+\.json$/.test(f));
  const file = files[0];
  if (files.length !== 1 || file === undefined) throw new Error(`expected exactly one airports.<version>.json in ${dir}, found ${files.length}`);
  const a: unknown = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dir, file), 'utf8'));
  if (!isRecord(a) || !Array.isArray(a.airports)) fail(file, 'airports', 'must be an array');
  const version = str(file, 'version', a.version);
  const m: unknown = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dir, 'metros.json'), 'utf8'));
  if (!isRecord(m) || !isRecord(m.metros)) fail('metros.json', 'metros', 'must be an object');
  const revision = str('metros.json', 'revision', m.revision);
  const metros: Record<string, string[]> = {};
  for (const [code, members] of Object.entries(m.metros)) {
    if (!Array.isArray(members)) fail('metros.json', `metros.${code}`, 'must be an array');
    metros[code] = members.map((x, i) => str('metros.json', `metros.${code}[${i}]`, x));
  }
  const index = metroIndex(metros);
  const airports = a.airports.map((x: unknown, i: number) => {
    const f = `airports[${i}]`;
    if (!isRecord(x)) return fail(file, f, 'must be an object');
    const iataText = str(file, `${f}.iata`, x.iata);
    let iata: Iata;
    try {
      iata = asIata(iataText);
    } catch {
      return fail(file, `${f}.iata`, `is not an IATA code: ${iataText}`);
    }
    return {
      iata, icao: x.icao === null ? null : str(file, `${f}.icao`, x.icao), name: str(file, `${f}.name`, x.name), city: str(file, `${f}.city`, x.city),
      country: str(file, `${f}.country`, x.country), tz: str(file, `${f}.tz`, x.tz), lat: num(file, `${f}.lat`, x.lat), lon: num(file, `${f}.lon`, x.lon),
      metro: index.get(iata) ?? null,
    };
  });
  return { version: `${version}+metros-${revision}`, airports, metros };
}

/** Seeds or re-seeds `airports` by upsert when the dataset version changes (spec §7); skips when every row is present at this version. */
export function seedAirports(db: Db, dataset: AirportsDataset): { inserted: number; datasetVersion: string } {
  const atVersion = db.raw.prepare<[string], { n: number }>('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM airports WHERE dataset_version = ?').get(dataset.version)?.n ?? 0;
  if (atVersion > 0 && atVersion >= dataset.airports.length) return { inserted: 0, datasetVersion: dataset.version };
  new AirportsRepo(db).upsertAll(dataset);
  return { inserted: dataset.airports.length, datasetVersion: dataset.version };
}

/** Spec §7 sanity check, run at boot after seeding; a failure fails boot (WP8). */
export function sanityCheckAirports(db: Db, referenced: Iata[]): { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: string } {
  const n = db.raw.prepare<[], { n: number }>(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM airports WHERE iata GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]' AND length(tz) > 0`).get()?.n ?? 0;
  if (n < AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS) return { ok: false, reason: `airports table holds ${n} rows with an IATA code and tz; at least ${AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS} are required` };
  const exists = db.raw.prepare('SELECT 1 AS present FROM airports WHERE iata = ?');
  const missing = [...new Set(referenced)].filter((iata) => exists.get(iata) === undefined).sort();
  if (missing.length > 0) return { ok: false, reason: `airports referenced by enabled definitions are missing: ${missing.join(', ')}` };
  return { ok: true };
}
```

`packages/db/scripts/build-airports.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Builds the vendored airports data (spec §7, decision 11). Run by a person when updating the data, never by the app or CI.
//
//   node packages/db/scripts/build-airports.mjs --input <mwgg airports.json> --commit <40-hex sha> --date <YYYY-MM-DD>
//   node packages/db/scripts/build-airports.mjs --wikitext <section wikitext> --revision <Wikipedia revision id>
// The second form needs the first's output: metro members are checked against the built airports file (po-002).
import { readFileSync, readdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { parseArgs } from 'node:util';

const MIN_ROWS = 5000; // spec §7 sanity threshold
const IATA = /^[A-Z]{3}$/;
const dataDir = fileURLToPath(new URL('../data/', import.meta.url));
const { values } = parseArgs({ options: { input: { type: 'string' }, commit: { type: 'string' }, date: { type: 'string' }, wikitext: { type: 'string' }, revision: { type: 'string' } } });

if (values.wikitext) {
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(values.revision ?? '')) throw new Error('--revision <numeric revision id> is required with --wikitext');
  const builtFile = readdirSync(dataDir).find((f) => /^airports\..+\.json$/.test(f));
  if (!builtFile) throw new Error('build the airports file first');
  const byIata = new Map(JSON.parse(readFileSync(dataDir + builtFile, 'utf8')).airports.map((a) => [a.iata, a]));
  const metros = {};
  const drops = [];
  const owner = new Map();
  for (const line of readFileSync(values.wikitext, 'utf8').split('\n')) {
    if (!line.startsWith('*')) continue;
    const head = line.match(/\(([A-Z]{3})\)&nbsp;–/);
    if (!head) continue;
    // why: a code introduced by "formerly" (inline or in ''…'' italics) is a historical code, not a member.
    const rest = line.slice(line.indexOf(head[0]) + head[0].length).replace(/''[^']*''/g, '').replace(/formerly [A-Z]{3}/g, '');
    const codes = [...new Set([...rest.matchAll(/\(([A-Z]{3})[,)]/g)].map((m) => m[1]))].sort();
    const present = codes.filter((c) => byIata.has(c) || (drops.push(`${head[1]}:${c} absent from the airports file`), false));
    const countries = {};
    for (const c of present) countries[byIata.get(c).country] = (countries[byIata.get(c).country] ?? 0) + 1;
    const majority = Object.entries(countries).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]?.[0];
    const kept = present.filter((c) => byIata.get(c).country === majority || (drops.push(`${head[1]}:${c} in ${byIata.get(c).country}, not ${majority}`), false));
    for (const c of kept) {
      if (owner.has(c) && owner.get(c) !== head[1]) throw new Error(`${c} is listed under both ${owner.get(c)} and ${head[1]}`);
      owner.set(c, head[1]);
    }
    if (kept.length > 0) metros[head[1]] = kept;
  }
  const source = `https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=IATA_airport_code&oldid=${values.revision}`;
  writeFileSync(`${dataDir}metros.json`, `${JSON.stringify({ source, revision: values.revision, licence: 'CC BY-SA 4.0', retrieved: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), metros }, null, 1)}\n`);
  console.log(`wrote ${Object.keys(metros).length} metro codes from revision ${values.revision}; dropped: ${drops.join('; ') || 'none'}`);
  process.exit(0);
}

if (!values.input || !/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(values.commit ?? '') || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(values.date ?? '')) {
  console.error('usage: build-airports.mjs --input <airports.json> --commit <40-hex sha> --date <YYYY-MM-DD>');
  process.exit(2);
}
const knownTz = (tz) => {
  try {
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en', { timeZone: tz });
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
};
const seen = new Set();
const airports = [];
let skipped = 0;
for (const a of Object.values(JSON.parse(readFileSync(values.input, 'utf8')))) {
  const iata = typeof a.iata === 'string' ? a.iata.trim() : '';
  if (!IATA.test(iata) || typeof a.tz !== 'string' || a.tz.length === 0) continue;
  if (!knownTz(a.tz) || typeof a.lat !== 'number' || typeof a.lon !== 'number' || !/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(a.country ?? '')) {
    skipped++;
    continue;
  }
  if (seen.has(iata)) throw new Error(`duplicate IATA code ${iata}; refusing to guess which row is right`);
  seen.add(iata);
  airports.push({ iata, icao: a.icao || null, name: String(a.name), city: typeof a.city === 'string' ? a.city : '', country: a.country, tz: a.tz, lat: a.lat, lon: a.lon });
}
airports.sort((x, y) => (x.iata < y.iata ? -1 : 1));
if (airports.length < MIN_ROWS) throw new Error(`only ${airports.length} airports with IATA and tz; at least ${MIN_ROWS} required`);
const version = `${values.date}-${values.commit.slice(0, 7)}`;
writeFileSync(`${dataDir}airports.${version}.json`, `${JSON.stringify({ version, source: `mwgg/Airports@${values.commit}`, licence: 'MIT; see airports.LICENSE', airports })}\n`);
console.log(`wrote ${airports.length} airports (${skipped} skipped for an unknown tz, missing coordinates or country), version ${version}`);
```

```ts
import { asIsoDate } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { FxRepo } from '../src/repositories/fx.js';
import { freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';

const d = asIsoDate;
const row = { date: d('2026-10-12'), currency: 'USD', rateNano: 750_000_000, source: 'frankfurter' };

describe('FxRepo (spec §5.6, §6.3, §7 fx_rates)', () => {
  it('stores integer nano rates with their source and answers the newest rate within maxAgeDays', () => {
    const repo = new FxRepo(freshDb());
    repo.upsert([{ ...row, date: d('2026-10-09'), rateNano: 760_000_000 }, row, { ...row, currency: 'JPY', rateNano: 5_100_000 }]);
    expect(repo.rateNano(d('2026-10-12'), 'USD')).toBe(750_000_000);
    expect(repo.rateNano(d('2026-10-13'), 'USD')).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.latest('USD', 3, d('2026-10-14'))).toEqual({ rateNano: 750_000_000, date: '2026-10-12', source: 'frankfurter' });
    expect(repo.latest('USD', 3, d('2026-10-15'))?.date).toBe('2026-10-12'); // 15 − 3 = 12: in
    expect(repo.latest('USD', 3, d('2026-10-16'))).toBeNull(); // 16 − 3 = 13: too old
    expect(repo.latest('CNY', 3, d('2026-10-14'))).toBeNull();
  });

  it('upsert replaces the rate and source for the same date and currency', () => {
    const repo = new FxRepo(freshDb());
    repo.upsert([{ ...row, source: 'a' }]);
    repo.upsert([{ ...row, rateNano: 751_000_000, source: 'b' }]);
    expect(repo.latest('USD', 3, d('2026-10-12'))).toEqual({ rateNano: 751_000_000, date: '2026-10-12', source: 'b' });
  });

  it('rejects non-integer rates, lower-case currencies, GBP itself and an empty source, writing nothing', () => {
    const repo = new FxRepo(freshDb());
    expect(() => repo.upsert([row, { ...row, currency: 'EUR', rateNano: 0.75 }])).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(() => repo.upsert([{ ...row, currency: 'usd' }])).toThrow(/currency/);
    expect(() => repo.upsert([{ ...row, currency: 'GBP' }])).toThrow(/GBP/);
    expect(() => repo.upsert([{ ...row, source: '' }])).toThrow(/source/);
    expect(repo.rateNano(d('2026-10-12'), 'USD')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`packages/db/src/schema/fxRates.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, integer, primaryKey, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';

/** Spec §7 `fx_rates`: GBP per 1 native unit × 1e9 (spec §6.3), with the source stamped on converted prices. */
export const fxRates = sqliteTable(
  'fx_rates',
  { date: text('date').notNull(), currency: text('currency').notNull(), rateNano: integer('rate_nano').notNull(), source: text('source').notNull() },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.date, t.currency] }),
    check('fx_rates_rate_ck', sql.raw("typeof(rate_nano) = 'integer' AND rate_nano > 0")),
    check('fx_rates_currency_ck', sql.raw("currency GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]' AND currency <> 'GBP'")),
    check('fx_rates_date_ck', sql.raw("date GLOB '[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]'")),
    check('fx_rates_source_ck', sql.raw('length(source) > 0')),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/fx.ts`:

```ts
import type { IsoDate } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { readDate } from '../internal/read.js';
import { RowRejected, assertInt, assertIsoDate } from '../internal/validate.js';

/** FX rates for normalisation (spec §5.6: `FX_MAX_RATE_AGE_DAYS`, ECB-covered currencies only). */
export class FxRepo {
  constructor(private readonly db: Db) {}

  rateNano(date: IsoDate, currency: string): number | null {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string, string], { r: number }>('SELECT rate_nano AS r FROM fx_rates WHERE date = ? AND currency = ?').get(date, currency)?.r ?? null;
  }

  /** The newest rate dated within [today − maxAgeDays, today]; null means `fx-unavailable` (spec §5.6). */
  latest(currency: string, maxAgeDays: number, today: IsoDate): { rateNano: number; date: IsoDate; source: string } | null {
    assertInt('maxAgeDays', maxAgeDays);
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, { rateNano: number; date: string; source: string }>(
        `SELECT rate_nano AS rateNano, date, source FROM fx_rates WHERE currency = @currency AND date <= @today AND date >= date(@today, '-' || @maxAgeDays || ' days') ORDER BY date DESC LIMIT 1`,
      )
      .get({ currency, today, maxAgeDays });
    return r === undefined ? null : { rateNano: r.rateNano, date: readDate('fx_rates.date', r.date), source: r.source };
  }

  upsert(rows: Array<{ date: IsoDate; currency: string; rateNano: number; source: string }>): void {
    for (const r of rows) {
      assertIsoDate('date', r.date);
      if (!/^[A-Z]{3}$/.test(r.currency)) throw new RowRejected('currency', `currency must be ISO 4217 upper-case, got ${r.currency}`);
      if (r.currency === 'GBP') throw new RowRejected('currency', 'GBP is the base currency and has no rate row');
      assertInt('rate_nano', r.rateNano, 1);
      if (r.source.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('source', 'an FX rate must name its source');
    }
    const stmt = this.db.raw.prepare('INSERT INTO fx_rates (date, currency, rate_nano, source) VALUES (@date, @currency, @rateNano, @source) ON CONFLICT(date, currency) DO UPDATE SET rate_nano = excluded.rate_nano, source = excluded.source');
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const r of rows) stmt.run(r);
    });
  }
}
```

```ts
import type { DefinitionInput } from '@gmt/core';

/** The worked example (spec §1, §11 defaults): LHR, LGW → JFK, EWR, 10–21 nights, 182-day relative window. */
export function exampleDefinitionInput(over: Partial<DefinitionInput> = {}): DefinitionInput {
  return {
    name: 'London to New York', origins: [LHR, LGW], destinations: [JFK, EWR], window: { kind: 'relative', startOffsetDays: 7, lengthDays: 182 },
    minNights: 10, maxNights: 21, cabin: 'economy', constraints: { maxJourneyMinutes: 1500, arrivalWindowLocal: { from: '06:00', to: '23:00' } },
```
