```ts
import { fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RowCorrupt } from '../src/internal/read.js';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { DefinitionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/definitions.js';
import { T0, count, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { JFK, exampleDefinitionInput } from './helpers/factories.js';

describe('DefinitionsRepo (spec §7 search_definitions)', () => {
  it('round-trips every field; confirmed cost starts null', () => {
    const repo = new DefinitionsRepo(freshDb(), fixedClock(T0));
    const input = exampleDefinitionInput({ destinationNotes: { [JFK]: 'onward leg not included' }, onwardCostPence: { [JFK]: 4500 } });
    const created = repo.create(input);
    expect(created).toEqual({ ...input, id: created.id, confirmedCostPence: null, createdAt: T0, updatedAt: T0, deletedAt: null });
    expect(repo.get(created.id)).toEqual(created);
    expect(repo.get('missing')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('create stores the create click\'s confirmation in the same INSERT; a non-integer confirmation writes nothing (arch-r2-005, dm-r2-002)', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const repo = new DefinitionsRepo(db, fixedClock(T0));
    const created = repo.create({ ...exampleDefinitionInput(), confirmedCostPence: 1_250 });
    expect(created.confirmedCostPence).toBe(1_250);
    expect(repo.get(created.id)?.confirmedCostPence).toBe(1_250);
    expect(() => repo.create({ ...exampleDefinitionInput(), confirmedCostPence: 12.5 })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'integer' }));
    expect(repo.list()).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('update merges a patch and moves updated_at', () => {
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new DefinitionsRepo(freshDb(), clock);
    const d = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput());
    clock.advance(60_000);
    expect(repo.update(d.id, { maxNights: 14, enabled: false })).toMatchObject({ maxNights: 14, enabled: false, minNights: 10, createdAt: T0, updatedAt: '2026-10-14T12:01:00.000Z' });
  });

  it('soft delete: hidden from list unless asked, kept for history, refuses updates, keeps executions', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new DefinitionsRepo(db, clock);
    const keep = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ name: 'kept' }));
    const gone = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ name: 'gone' }));
    db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO search_executions (id, definition_id, definition_snapshot_json, trigger, status, created_at) VALUES ('e1', ?, '{}', 'manual', 'completed', ?)`).run(gone.id, T0);
    clock.advance(1000);
    repo.softDelete(gone.id);
    expect(repo.list().map((d) => d.name)).toEqual(['kept']);
    expect(repo.list({ includeDeleted: true }).map((d) => d.name)).toEqual(['kept', 'gone']);
    expect(repo.get(gone.id)).toMatchObject({ deletedAt: '2026-10-14T12:00:01.000Z', enabled: false });
    expect(() => repo.update(gone.id, { name: 'x' })).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(() => repo.softDelete('missing')).toThrow(/missing/);
    expect(count(db, 'search_executions')).toBe(1);
    expect(repo.get(keep.id)?.deletedAt).toBeNull();
  });

  it('setConfirmedCost stores integer pence only, and update never touches it', () => {
    const repo = new DefinitionsRepo(freshDb(), fixedClock(T0));
    const d = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput());
    repo.setConfirmedCost(d.id, 412);
    repo.update(d.id, { name: 'renamed' });
    expect(repo.get(d.id)?.confirmedCostPence).toBe(412);
    repo.setConfirmedCost(d.id, null);
    expect(repo.get(d.id)?.confirmedCostPence).toBeNull();
    expect(() => repo.setConfirmedCost(d.id, 4.5)).toThrow(RowRejected);
  });

  it('rejects non-integer nights, budgets and onward costs', () => {
    const repo = new DefinitionsRepo(freshDb(), fixedClock(T0));
    expect(() => repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ minNights: 10.5 }))).toThrow(/min_nights/);
    expect(() => repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ budget: { maxCalls: 1, maxFreeCalls: 1, maxCostMicroGbp: 0.5 } }))).toThrow(/maxCostMicroGbp/);
    expect(() => repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ onwardCostPence: { [JFK]: 12.3 } }))).toThrow(/onward/);
  });

  it('a corrupt stored JSON column is reported as RowCorrupt, never returned (dm-006)', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const repo = new DefinitionsRepo(db, fixedClock(T0));
    const d = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput());
    db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE search_definitions SET window_json = '{"kind":"weekly"}' WHERE id = ?`).run(d.id);
    expect(() => repo.get(d.id)).toThrow(RowCorrupt);
  });
});
```

`packages/db/src/internal/definitionRow.ts`:

```ts
import { type DefinitionInput, type DefinitionSnapshot, type SearchDefinition, apiContract } from '@gmt/core';
import { RowCorrupt, isRecord, parseWith, readBool, readCellKey, readDate, readInt, readIataArray, readJson, readNullableTime, readString, readTime, readVerificationPolicy } from './read.js';

/**
 * The definition fields without the verification policy. // why: the unrefined shape (no save-time cross-field rules);
 * omitting `verification` is allowed because the refinement sits inside that field, not on the object.
 */
const SHAPE_WITHOUT_POLICY = apiContract.definitionShapeSchema.omit({ verification: true });

/**
 * The DefinitionInput fields of a stored row or snapshot. Unknown keys (id, timestamps, resolved space, the confirmed
 * cost) are stripped by the shape schema; callers read those separately.
 */
export function readDefinitionInput(column: string, raw: Record<string, unknown>): DefinitionInput {
  return { ...parseWith(column, SHAPE_WITHOUT_POLICY, raw), verification: readVerificationPolicy(`${column}.verification`, raw.verification) };
}

export interface DefinitionDbRow {
  id: string; name: string; origins_json: string; destinations_json: string; window_json: string; min_nights: number; max_nights: number; cabin: string;
  constraints_json: string; verification_policy_json: string; budget_json: string; providers_json: string; destination_notes_json: string | null;
  onward_cost_json: string | null; schedule_cron: string | null; schedule_tz: string; confirmed_cost_pence: number | null; enabled: number;
  deleted_at: string | null; created_at: string; updated_at: string;
}

export function readDefinition(r: DefinitionDbRow): SearchDefinition {
  const c = 'search_definitions';
  const input = readDefinitionInput(c, {
    name: r.name, origins: readJson(`${c}.origins_json`, r.origins_json), destinations: readJson(`${c}.destinations_json`, r.destinations_json),
    window: readJson(`${c}.window_json`, r.window_json), minNights: r.min_nights, maxNights: r.max_nights, cabin: r.cabin,
    constraints: readJson(`${c}.constraints_json`, r.constraints_json), verification: readJson(`${c}.verification_policy_json`, r.verification_policy_json),
    budget: readJson(`${c}.budget_json`, r.budget_json), providers: readJson(`${c}.providers_json`, r.providers_json),
    destinationNotes: r.destination_notes_json === null ? {} : readJson(`${c}.destination_notes_json`, r.destination_notes_json),
    onwardCostPence: r.onward_cost_json === null ? {} : readJson(`${c}.onward_cost_json`, r.onward_cost_json),
    scheduleCron: r.schedule_cron, scheduleTz: r.schedule_tz, enabled: r.enabled === 1,
  });
  return {
    ...input, id: r.id, confirmedCostPence: r.confirmed_cost_pence, createdAt: readTime(`${c}.created_at`, r.created_at),
    updatedAt: readTime(`${c}.updated_at`, r.updated_at), deletedAt: readNullableTime(`${c}.deleted_at`, r.deleted_at),
  };
}

/** A frozen execution snapshot: the definition part through core's schema, the resolved space through the read guards. */
export function readSnapshot(column: string, text: string): DefinitionSnapshot {
  const v = readJson(column, text);
  if (!isRecord(v) || !isRecord(v.resolved) || !Array.isArray(v.droppedAlwaysInclude)) throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'not a definition snapshot');
  // why: the snapshot-only fields are taken out first and read by their own guards; only DefinitionInput fields reach the schema.
  const { id, confirmedCostPence: confirmed, createdAt, updatedAt, deletedAt, resolved: res, resolvedAt, windowClamped, droppedAlwaysInclude, ...fields } = v;
  const input = readDefinitionInput(column, fields);
  if (!isRecord(res) || !Array.isArray(droppedAlwaysInclude)) throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'not a definition snapshot');
  return {
    ...input,
    id: readString(column, id),
    confirmedCostPence: confirmed === null || confirmed === undefined ? null : readInt(column, confirmed),
    createdAt: readTime(column, readString(column, createdAt)),
    updatedAt: readTime(column, readString(column, updatedAt)),
    deletedAt: deletedAt === null || deletedAt === undefined ? null : readTime(column, readString(column, deletedAt)),
    resolved: {
      origins: readIataArray(column, res.origins), destinations: readIataArray(column, res.destinations),
      departureFrom: readDate(column, readString(column, res.departureFrom)), departureTo: readDate(column, readString(column, res.departureTo)),
      minNights: readInt(column, res.minNights), maxNights: readInt(column, res.maxNights),
    },
    resolvedAt: readTime(column, readString(column, resolvedAt)),
    windowClamped: readBool(column, windowClamped),
    droppedAlwaysInclude: droppedAlwaysInclude.map((x: unknown) => readCellKey(column, x)),
  };
}
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/definitions.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { Clock, DefinitionInput, IsoDateTime, SearchDefinition } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { type DefinitionDbRow, readDefinition } from '../internal/definitionRow.js';
import { RowRejected, assertInt, assertNullableInt } from '../internal/validate.js';

/** Integer rules the repository owns (spec §6.3); shape and range rules are zod's in `api-contract`. */
function validate(d: DefinitionInput): void {
  assertInt('min_nights', d.minNights);
  assertInt('max_nights', d.maxNights);
  assertInt('budget.maxCalls', d.budget.maxCalls);
  assertInt('budget.maxFreeCalls', d.budget.maxFreeCalls);
  assertInt('budget.maxCostMicroGbp', d.budget.maxCostMicroGbp);
  for (const [dest, pence] of Object.entries(d.onwardCostPence)) if (pence !== undefined) assertInt(`onward cost for ${dest}`, pence);
  if (d.scheduleTz.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('schedule-tz', 'schedule_tz is required (spec §7 NOT NULL)');
}

const params = (d: DefinitionInput, updatedAt: IsoDateTime): Record<string, unknown> => ({
  name: d.name, origins: JSON.stringify(d.origins), destinations: JSON.stringify(d.destinations), window: JSON.stringify(d.window), minNights: d.minNights,
  maxNights: d.maxNights, cabin: d.cabin, constraints: JSON.stringify(d.constraints), verification: JSON.stringify(d.verification), budget: JSON.stringify(d.budget),
  providers: JSON.stringify(d.providers), destinationNotes: JSON.stringify(d.destinationNotes), onwardCost: JSON.stringify(d.onwardCostPence),
  scheduleCron: d.scheduleCron, scheduleTz: d.scheduleTz, enabled: d.enabled ? 1 : 0, updatedAt,
});

/** Spec §7 `search_definitions`, soft-deleted. `confirmed_cost_pence` is set by `create` or `setConfirmedCost`, never by `update`. */
export class DefinitionsRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
  ) {}

  /** Decision 14: the create click's confirmation is stored in the same INSERT, so it can never be lost between two writes. */
  create(input: DefinitionInput & { confirmedCostPence?: number }): SearchDefinition {
    const { confirmedCostPence, ...definition } = input;
    validate(definition);
    assertNullableInt('confirmed_cost_pence', confirmedCostPence ?? null);
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = this.clock.now();
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO search_definitions (id, created_at, name, origins_json, destinations_json, window_json, min_nights, max_nights, cabin, constraints_json,
          verification_policy_json, budget_json, providers_json, destination_notes_json, onward_cost_json, schedule_cron, schedule_tz, confirmed_cost_pence, enabled, updated_at)
         VALUES (@id, @createdAt, @name, @origins, @destinations, @window, @minNights, @maxNights, @cabin, @constraints, @verification, @budget, @providers,
          @destinationNotes, @onwardCost, @scheduleCron, @scheduleTz, @confirmedCostPence, @enabled, @updatedAt)`,
      )
      .run({ id, createdAt: now, ...params(definition, now), confirmedCostPence: confirmedCostPence ?? null });
    return this.mustGet(id);
  }

  update(id: string, patch: Partial<DefinitionInput>): SearchDefinition {
    const { id: _id, confirmedCostPence: _c, createdAt: _ca, updatedAt: _u, deletedAt, ...current } = this.mustGet(id);
    if (deletedAt !== null) throw new RowRejected('deleted', `definition ${id} is deleted`);
    const merged: DefinitionInput = { ...current, ...patch };
    validate(merged);
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(
        `UPDATE search_definitions SET name = @name, origins_json = @origins, destinations_json = @destinations, window_json = @window, min_nights = @minNights,
           max_nights = @maxNights, cabin = @cabin, constraints_json = @constraints, verification_policy_json = @verification, budget_json = @budget,
           providers_json = @providers, destination_notes_json = @destinationNotes, onward_cost_json = @onwardCost, schedule_cron = @scheduleCron,
           schedule_tz = @scheduleTz, enabled = @enabled, updated_at = @updatedAt WHERE id = @id`,
      )
      .run({ id, ...params(merged, this.clock.now()) });
    return this.mustGet(id);
  }

  /** Returns soft-deleted definitions too (history needs them). */
  get(id: string): SearchDefinition | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], DefinitionDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_definitions WHERE id = ?').get(id);
    return r === undefined ? null : readDefinition(r);
  }

  list(opts: { includeDeleted?: boolean } = {}): SearchDefinition[] {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[number], DefinitionDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_definitions WHERE (? = 1 OR deleted_at IS NULL) ORDER BY created_at, rowid').all(opts.includeDeleted ? 1 : 0).map(readDefinition);
  }

  /** Spec §7 "Soft-deleted": executions, prices and history keep their foreign keys; the definition is disabled and never scheduled or resumed. */
  softDelete(id: string): void {
    const now = this.clock.now();
    const changes = this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_definitions SET deleted_at = @now, enabled = 0, updated_at = @now WHERE id = @id AND deleted_at IS NULL').run({ id, now }).changes;
    if (changes === 0 && this.get(id) === null) throw new RowRejected('missing', `definition ${id} does not exist`);
  }

  /** Spec §7: set only from an explicit operator confirmation, never recomputed automatically. */
  setConfirmedCost(id: string, pence: number | null): void {
    assertNullableInt('confirmed_cost_pence', pence);
    if (this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_definitions SET confirmed_cost_pence = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(pence, this.clock.now(), id).changes === 0) {
      throw new RowRejected('missing', `definition ${id} does not exist`);
    }
  }

  private mustGet(id: string): SearchDefinition {
    const d = this.get(id);
    if (d === null) throw new RowRejected('missing', `definition ${id} does not exist`);
    return d;
  }
}
```

```ts
import type { Clock, DefinitionSnapshot, Execution, ExecutionStatus, SearchDefinition } from '@gmt/core';
import { DefinitionsRepo } from '../../src/repositories/definitions.js';
import { ExecutionsRepo } from '../../src/repositories/executions.js';
import type { ExecutionCreateInput } from '../../src/types.js';

/** A frozen snapshot of a definition resolved at T0 (spec §3 "snapshot"). */
export function snapshotOf(d: SearchDefinition): DefinitionSnapshot {
  return {
    ...d,
    resolved: { origins: d.origins, destinations: d.destinations, departureFrom: asIsoDate('2026-10-21'), departureTo: asIsoDate('2027-04-20'), minNights: d.minNights, maxNights: d.maxNights },
    resolvedAt: T0, windowClamped: false, droppedAlwaysInclude: [],
  };
}

/** The statuses `create` accepts; anything else is reached through `setStatus`, the path the engine takes. */
function creatable(s: ExecutionStatus): ExecutionCreateInput['status'] | null {
  switch (s) {
    case 'pending':
    case 'running':
    case 'skipped_budget':
    case 'skipped_confirm':
    case 'skipped_window':
    case 'skipped_missed':
      return s;
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

/** Creates (or reuses) a definition and one execution through the repositories. */
export function newExecution(
  db: Db,
  clock: Clock,
  opts: { definition?: SearchDefinition; status?: ExecutionStatus; cell?: CellKey | null; trigger?: Execution['trigger'] } = {},
): { definition: SearchDefinition; execution: Execution } {
  const definition = opts.definition ?? new DefinitionsRepo(db, clock).create(exampleDefinitionInput());
  const executions = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
  const wanted = opts.status ?? 'running';
  const createAs = creatable(wanted) ?? 'running';
  const cell = opts.cell ?? null;
  const created = executions.create({ definitionId: definition.id, isChild: cell !== null, cell, snapshot: snapshotOf(definition), estimate: null, trigger: opts.trigger ?? 'manual', status: createAs });
  if (createAs === wanted) return { definition, execution: created };
  executions.setStatus(created.id, wanted, { finishedAt: clock.now() });
  const moved = executions.get(created.id);
  if (moved === null) throw new Error('execution vanished');
  return { definition, execution: moved };
}
```

`packages/db/test/helpers/claim-worker.mjs`:

```js
// One contender in the compare-and-set race (spec §8.1): its own connection, a shared start barrier, then the
// repository's own claim statement (passed in as text); reports `changes`.
import { parentPort, workerData } from 'node:worker_threads';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';

const { path, sql, id, barrier } = workerData;
const db = new Database(path);
db.pragma('busy_timeout = 5000');
const gate = new Int32Array(barrier);
Atomics.add(gate, 0, 1);
Atomics.notify(gate, 0);
while (Atomics.load(gate, 0) < 2) Atomics.wait(gate, 0, 1, 50);
const { changes } = db.prepare(sql).run(id);
db.close();
parentPort.postMessage(changes);
```

`packages/db/test/executions.test.ts`:

```ts
import { Worker } from 'node:worker_threads';
import { ACTIVE_STATUSES, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RowCorrupt } from '../src/internal/read.js';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { DefinitionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/definitions.js';
import { CLAIM_RESUME_SQL, ExecutionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/executions.js';
import { ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL } from '../src/schema/checks.js';
import { T0, at, freshDb, openTracked, tmpDbPath } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, cell, exampleDefinitionInput, newExecution, snapshotOf } from './helpers/factories.js';

/** test-005: 'ok' when the call succeeds, 'unique' on a unique violation; any other failure is rethrown. */
function attempt(fn: () => unknown): 'ok' | 'unique' {
  try {
    fn();
    return 'ok';
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof Database.SqliteError && e.code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE') return 'unique';
    throw e;
  }
}

describe('ExecutionsRepo (spec §7 search_executions, §8.1)', () => {
  it('the schema active set matches core ACTIVE_STATUSES', () => {
    expect(ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL).toBe(ACTIVE_STATUSES.map((s) => `'${s}'`).join(', '));
  });

  it('round-trips parent and child; pending has no startedAt, running does, skipped is created finished', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'pending', trigger: 'schedule' });
    expect(execution).toMatchObject({ definitionId: definition.id, isChild: false, cell: null, status: 'pending', stage: null, trigger: 'schedule', createdAt: T0, startedAt: null, finishedAt: null, autoResumeCount: 0, cancelRequested: false, errorText: null });
    expect(execution.snapshot).toEqual(snapshotOf(definition));
    expect(newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell() }).execution).toMatchObject({ isChild: true, cell: cell(), startedAt: T0 });
    expect(newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'skipped_window' }).execution).toMatchObject({ status: 'skipped_window', startedAt: null, finishedAt: T0 });
    expect(() => new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock).create({ definitionId: definition.id, isChild: true, cell: null, snapshot: snapshotOf(definition), estimate: null, trigger: 'manual', status: 'running' })).toThrow(RowRejected);
  });

  it('a child-shaped snapshot (maxCells 1, alwaysInclude [cell], inherited topN 10, no confirmed cost) round-trips through create and get (dm-r2-001, arch-r2-003)', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const definition = new DefinitionsRepo(db, clock).create(exampleDefinitionInput());
    expect(definition.confirmedCostPence).toBeNull();
    const parent = snapshotOf(definition);
    // why: the read path must not depend on WP7 pinning topN to 1; a save-time refinement (topN ≤ maxCells) must not run on stored rows.
    const childSnapshot = { ...parent, verification: { ...parent.verification, maxCells: 1, alwaysInclude: [cell()] }, providers: { ...parent.providers, discovery: [] } };
    const repo = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    const child = repo.create({ definitionId: definition.id, isChild: true, cell: cell(), snapshot: childSnapshot, estimate: null, trigger: 'manual', status: 'running' });
    expect(child.snapshot).toEqual(childSnapshot);
    expect(repo.get(child.id)?.snapshot.verification).toEqual({ ...parent.verification, maxCells: 1, topN: 10, alwaysInclude: [cell()] });
  });

  it('a corrupt stored snapshot is reported as RowCorrupt (dm-006)', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
    db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE search_executions SET definition_snapshot_json = '{"budget":{}}' WHERE id = ?`).run(execution.id);
    expect(() => new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock).get(execution.id)).toThrow(RowCorrupt);
  });

  it('partial unique indexes: one active parent per definition, one active child per cell; parent and child coexist', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'pending' });
    expect(attempt(() => newExecution(db, clock, { definition }))).toBe('unique');
    expect(attempt(() => newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell() }))).toBe('ok');
    expect(attempt(() => newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell() }))).toBe('unique');
    expect(attempt(() => newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell(undefined, EWR) }))).toBe('ok');
    new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock).setStatus(execution.id, 'completed', { finishedAt: T0 });
    expect(newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.status).toBe('running');
    const sqlOf = (name: string) => db.raw.prepare<[string], { sql: string }>(`SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name = ?`).get(name)?.sql;
    expect(sqlOf('search_executions_active_parent_uq')).toMatch(/WHERE is_child = 0 AND status IN \('pending', 'running', 'paused_budget', 'paused_error', 'interrupted'\)/);
    expect(sqlOf('search_executions_active_child_uq')).toMatch(/WHERE is_child = 1 AND status IN/);
  });

  it('reads: activeParent, activeChild, latestCompleted, listForDefinition, listByStatus, lastScheduledFiring', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    const { definition, execution: first } = newExecution(db, clock, { trigger: 'schedule' });
    repo.setStatus(first.id, 'completed');
    clock.advance(1000);
    const missed = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'skipped_missed', trigger: 'schedule' }).execution;
    clock.advance(1000);
    const second = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution;
    const child = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell() }).execution;
    expect(repo.activeParent(definition.id)?.id).toBe(second.id);
    expect(repo.activeChild(definition.id, cell())?.id).toBe(child.id);
    expect(repo.activeChild(definition.id, cell(undefined, EWR))).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.latestCompleted(definition.id)?.id).toBe(first.id);
    expect(repo.listForDefinition(definition.id, { limit: 10, includeChildren: false }).map((e) => e.id)).toEqual([second.id, missed.id, first.id]);
    expect(repo.listForDefinition(definition.id, { limit: 10, includeChildren: true })).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(repo.listForDefinition(definition.id, { limit: 1, includeChildren: true })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(repo.listByStatus(['running']).map((e) => e.id).sort()).toEqual([second.id, child.id].sort());
    expect(repo.lastScheduledFiring(definition.id)).toBe('2026-10-14T12:00:01.000Z');
    expect(repo.lastScheduledFiring('none')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('setStatus and patch write only the keys given; error_text is URL-stripped; requestCancel and resetAutoResume', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
    repo.patch(execution.id, { stage: 'discovery' });
    repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'paused_error', { resumeAfter: at('2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z'), errorText: 'SQLITE_FULL: database or disk is full while fetching https://user:pw@api.example.com/v1/x?api_key=SECRET' });
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'paused_error', stage: 'discovery', resumeAfter: '2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z', errorText: 'SQLITE_FULL: database or disk is full while fetching https://api.example.com/v1/x' });
    repo.patch(execution.id, { snapshot: { ...snapshotOf(definition), budget: { maxCalls: 5, maxFreeCalls: 5, maxCostMicroGbp: 0 } }, startedAt: at('2026-10-14T12:30:00.000Z') });
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'paused_error', startedAt: '2026-10-14T12:30:00.000Z', snapshot: { budget: { maxCalls: 5 } } });
    repo.requestCancel(execution.id);
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)?.cancelRequested).toBe(true);
    repo.incrementAutoResume(execution.id);
    repo.resetAutoResume(execution.id);
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)?.autoResumeCount).toBe(0);
    expect(() => repo.setStatus('missing', 'running')).toThrow(RowRejected);
  });

  it('claimResume is a compare-and-set that clears resume_after, cancel_requested and finished_at; conflicting claims lose', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
    repo.requestCancel(execution.id);
    repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'cancelled', { finishedAt: T0, resumeAfter: at('2026-11-01T00:00:00.000Z') });
    expect(repo.claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(true);
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'running', resumeAfter: null, cancelRequested: false, finishedAt: null }); // dm-004
    expect(repo.claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(false);
    repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'completed');
    const other = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution;
    expect(repo.claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(false); // another parent is active: the partial index refuses, the claim loses
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)?.status).toBe('completed');
    expect(repo.claimResume(other.id)).toBe(false);
    expect(repo.claimResume('missing')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('two repositories on two connections to one file: the second claim loses', () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const a = freshDb(path);
    const b = openTracked(path);
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const { execution } = newExecution(a, clock);
    new ExecutionsRepo(a, clock).setStatus(execution.id, 'interrupted');
    expect(new ExecutionsRepo(b, clock).get(execution.id)?.status).toBe('interrupted');
    expect(new ExecutionsRepo(a, clock).claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(true);
    expect(new ExecutionsRepo(b, clock).claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('two concurrent claimers in two threads: exactly one wins, every round', async () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const db = freshDb(path);
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    const { definition } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'skipped_window' });
    const contender = (id: string, barrier: SharedArrayBuffer) =>
      new Promise<number>((resolve, reject) => {
        const w = new Worker(new URL('./helpers/claim-worker.mjs', import.meta.url), { workerData: { path, sql: CLAIM_RESUME_SQL, id, barrier } });
        w.once('message', resolve);
        w.once('error', reject);
      });
    for (let round = 0; round < 10; round++) {
      const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock, { definition });
      repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'interrupted');
      const barrier = new SharedArrayBuffer(4);
      const results = await Promise.all([contender(execution.id, barrier), contender(execution.id, barrier)]);
      expect(results.sort()).toEqual([0, 1]);
      expect(repo.get(execution.id)?.status).toBe('running');
      repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'completed');
    }
  }, 30_000);

  it('bootSweep: running and pending-created-before-boot become interrupted with auto_resume_count + 1', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    const { definition, execution: running } = newExecution(db, clock);
    const oldPending = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'pending', cell: cell() }).execution;
    const paused = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell(undefined, EWR) }).execution;
    repo.setStatus(paused.id, 'paused_budget');
    clock.advance(60_000);
    const boot = clock.now();
    const freshPending = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'pending', cell: cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-15', '2027-03-29') }).execution;
    expect(repo.bootSweep(boot).interrupted.sort()).toEqual([oldPending.id, running.id].sort());
    expect(repo.get(running.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'interrupted', autoResumeCount: 1 });
    expect(repo.get(oldPending.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'interrupted', autoResumeCount: 1 });
    expect(repo.get(freshPending.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'pending', autoResumeCount: 0 });
    expect(repo.get(paused.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'paused_budget', autoResumeCount: 0 });
    expect(repo.incrementAutoResume(running.id)).toBe(2);
    expect(() => repo.incrementAutoResume('missing')).toThrow(RowRejected);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`packages/db/src/repositories/executions.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { type CellKey, type Clock, type Execution, type ExecutionStatus, type IsoDateTime, RESUMABLE_STATUSES, stripUrlsInText } from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { readSnapshot } from '../internal/definitionRow.js';
import { EXECUTION_STATUSES, STAGES } from '../internal/enums.js';
import { nullableOneOf, oneOf, readDate, readEstimate, readIata, readNullableTime, readTime } from '../internal/read.js';
import { RowRejected, assertIsoDateTime, assertNullableIsoDateTime } from '../internal/validate.js';
import { ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL } from '../schema/checks.js';
import type { ExecutionCreateInput, ExecutionPatch } from '../types.js';

interface ExecutionDbRow {
  id: string; definition_id: string; is_child: number; child_origin: string | null; child_destination: string | null; child_departure_date: string | null; child_return_date: string | null;
  definition_snapshot_json: string; trigger: string; status: string; stage: string | null; estimate_json: string | null; cancel_requested: number;
  resume_after: string | null; auto_resume_count: number; created_at: string; started_at: string | null; finished_at: string | null; error_text: string | null;
}

function toExecution(r: ExecutionDbRow): Execution {
  const c = 'search_executions';
  const cell: CellKey | null =
    r.is_child === 1 && r.child_origin !== null && r.child_destination !== null && r.child_departure_date !== null && r.child_return_date !== null
      ? { origin: readIata(`${c}.child_origin`, r.child_origin), destination: readIata(`${c}.child_destination`, r.child_destination), departureDate: readDate(`${c}.child_departure_date`, r.child_departure_date), returnDate: readDate(`${c}.child_return_date`, r.child_return_date) }
      : null;
  return {
    id: r.id, definitionId: r.definition_id, isChild: r.is_child === 1, cell, snapshot: readSnapshot(`${c}.definition_snapshot_json`, r.definition_snapshot_json),
    status: oneOf(`${c}.status`, EXECUTION_STATUSES, r.status), stage: nullableOneOf(`${c}.stage`, STAGES, r.stage),
    estimate: r.estimate_json === null ? null : readEstimate(`${c}.estimate_json`, r.estimate_json), cancelRequested: r.cancel_requested === 1,
    resumeAfter: readNullableTime(`${c}.resume_after`, r.resume_after), autoResumeCount: r.auto_resume_count, startedAt: readNullableTime(`${c}.started_at`, r.started_at),
    finishedAt: readNullableTime(`${c}.finished_at`, r.finished_at), errorText: r.error_text, createdAt: readTime(`${c}.created_at`, r.created_at),
    trigger: oneOf(`${c}.trigger`, ['manual', 'schedule', 'tool'], r.trigger),
  };
}

/**
 * Spec §8.1 "Resume is a compare-and-set": the tick, the one-shot job, boot auto-resume and `/resume` all run this one
 * statement; exactly one caller sees `changes = 1`. It also clears the one-shot schedule, a stale cancel flag and the
 * finish time in the same step (dm-004), so a resumed run is never both running and cancel-requested.
 */
export const CLAIM_RESUME_SQL = `UPDATE search_executions SET status = 'running', resume_after = NULL, cancel_requested = 0, finished_at = NULL WHERE id = ? AND status IN (${RESUMABLE_STATUSES.map((s) => `'${s}'`).join(', ')})`;

/** Patch keys and their columns, a typed constant (ruling 15). */
const PATCH_COLUMNS = [
  ['stage', 'stage'], ['resumeAfter', 'resume_after'], ['errorText', 'error_text'], ['startedAt', 'started_at'], ['finishedAt', 'finished_at'],
  ['cancelRequested', 'cancel_requested'], ['estimate', 'estimate_json'], ['snapshot', 'definition_snapshot_json'],
] as const satisfies ReadonlyArray<readonly [keyof ExecutionPatch, string]>;

function encode(key: keyof ExecutionPatch, patch: ExecutionPatch): unknown {
  switch (key) {
    case 'resumeAfter':
    case 'startedAt':
    case 'finishedAt': {
      const v = patch[key] ?? null;
      assertNullableIsoDateTime(key, v);
      return v;
    }
    case 'errorText':
      return patch.errorText === null || patch.errorText === undefined ? null : stripUrlsInText(patch.errorText);
    case 'cancelRequested':
      return patch.cancelRequested === true ? 1 : 0;
    case 'estimate':
      return patch.estimate === null || patch.estimate === undefined ? null : JSON.stringify(patch.estimate);
    case 'snapshot':
      if (patch.snapshot === undefined) throw new RowRejected('snapshot', 'a snapshot patch needs a snapshot');
      return JSON.stringify(patch.snapshot);
    case 'stage':
      return patch.stage ?? null;
  }
}

const SKIPPED: readonly ExecutionStatus[] = ['skipped_budget', 'skipped_confirm', 'skipped_window', 'skipped_missed'];

/** Spec §7 `search_executions`. Every write is a single statement or one transaction. */
export class ExecutionsRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
  ) {}

  create(input: ExecutionCreateInput): Execution {
    if (input.isChild !== (input.cell !== null)) throw new RowRejected('child-cell', 'a child execution carries a cell and a parent does not');
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = this.clock.now();
    const c = input.cell;
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO search_executions (id, definition_id, is_child, child_origin, child_destination, child_departure_date, child_return_date, definition_snapshot_json, trigger, status, estimate_json, created_at, started_at, finished_at)
         VALUES (@id, @definitionId, @isChild, @o, @d, @dep, @ret, @snapshot, @trigger, @status, @estimate, @now, @startedAt, @finishedAt)`,
      )
      .run({
        id, definitionId: input.definitionId, isChild: input.isChild ? 1 : 0, o: c?.origin ?? null, d: c?.destination ?? null, dep: c?.departureDate ?? null, ret: c?.returnDate ?? null,
        snapshot: JSON.stringify(input.snapshot), trigger: input.trigger, status: input.status, estimate: input.estimate === null ? null : JSON.stringify(input.estimate),
        now, startedAt: input.status === 'running' ? now : null, finishedAt: SKIPPED.includes(input.status) ? now : null,
      });
    return this.mustGet(id);
  }

  get(id: string): Execution | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], ExecutionDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE id = ?').get(id);
    return r === undefined ? null : toExecution(r);
  }

  /** Newest first by creation. */
  listForDefinition(definitionId: string, opts: { limit: number; includeChildren: boolean }): Execution[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, ExecutionDbRow>(`SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = @definitionId AND (@children = 1 OR is_child = 0) ORDER BY created_at DESC, rowid DESC LIMIT @limit`)
      .all({ definitionId, children: opts.includeChildren ? 1 : 0, limit: opts.limit })
      .map(toExecution);
  }

  /** Executions in any of the statuses, oldest first (boot auto-resume, one-shot re-registration). */
  listByStatus(statuses: ExecutionStatus[]): Execution[] {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string], ExecutionDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE status IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?)) ORDER BY created_at, rowid').all(JSON.stringify(statuses)).map(toExecution);
  }

  activeParent(definitionId: string): Execution | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], ExecutionDbRow>(`SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = ? AND is_child = 0 AND status IN (${ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL})`).get(definitionId);
    return r === undefined ? null : toExecution(r);
  }

  activeChild(definitionId: string, cell: CellKey): Execution | null {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, ExecutionDbRow>(
        `SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = @definitionId AND is_child = 1 AND status IN (${ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL})
         AND child_origin = @origin AND child_destination = @destination AND child_departure_date = @departureDate AND child_return_date = @returnDate`,
      )
      .get({ definitionId, ...cell });
    return r === undefined ? null : toExecution(r);
  }

  latestCompleted(definitionId: string): Execution | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], ExecutionDbRow>(`SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = ? AND is_child = 0 AND status = 'completed' ORDER BY created_at DESC, rowid DESC LIMIT 1`).get(definitionId);
    return r === undefined ? null : toExecution(r);
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 compare-and-set. A claim that would make a second active run loses instead of throwing (`/resume` answers 409). */
  claimResume(id: string): boolean {
    try {
      return this.db.raw.prepare(CLAIM_RESUME_SQL).run(id).changes === 1;
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof Database.SqliteError && err.code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE') return false;
      throw err;
    }
  }

  setStatus(id: string, status: ExecutionStatus, patch: ExecutionPatch = {}): void {
    this.apply(id, status, patch);
  }

  patch(id: string, patch: ExecutionPatch): void {
    this.apply(id, null, patch);
  }

  requestCancel(id: string): void {
    this.apply(id, null, { cancelRequested: true });
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 "Boot order": `running` and `pending` created before this boot become `interrupted`, counting a crash restart. */
  bootSweep(bootAt: IsoDateTime): { interrupted: string[] } {
    assertIsoDateTime('bootAt', bootAt);
    const rows = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], { id: string }>(`UPDATE search_executions SET status = 'interrupted', auto_resume_count = auto_resume_count + 1 WHERE status = 'running' OR (status = 'pending' AND created_at < ?) RETURNING id`)
      .all(bootAt);
    return { interrupted: rows.map((r) => r.id) };
  }

  incrementAutoResume(id: string): number {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { n: number }>('UPDATE search_executions SET auto_resume_count = auto_resume_count + 1 WHERE id = ? RETURNING auto_resume_count AS n').get(id);
    if (r === undefined) throw new RowRejected('missing', `execution ${id} does not exist`);
    return r.n;
  }

  /** Spec §7 "reset when a tile or cell commits"; the tile and cell writes also reset it inside their own transactions. */
  resetAutoResume(id: string): void {
    if (this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_executions SET auto_resume_count = 0 WHERE id = ?').run(id).changes === 0) throw new RowRejected('missing', `execution ${id} does not exist`);
  }

  /** The newest schedule-triggered parent execution, including `skipped_*` rows (spec §8.2 missed-firing check). */
  lastScheduledFiring(definitionId: string): IsoDateTime | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { at: string | null }>(`SELECT MAX(created_at) AS at FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = ? AND is_child = 0 AND trigger = 'schedule'`).get(definitionId);
    return readNullableTime('search_executions.created_at', r?.at ?? null);
  }

  private apply(id: string, status: ExecutionStatus | null, patch: ExecutionPatch): void {
    const sets: string[] = [];
    const params: Record<string, unknown> = { id };
    if (status !== null) {
      sets.push('status = @status');
      params.status = status;
    }
    for (const [key, column] of PATCH_COLUMNS) {
      if (!(key in patch)) continue;
      sets.push(`${column} = @${key}`);
      params[key] = encode(key, patch);
    }
    if (sets.length === 0) return;
    if (this.db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE search_executions SET ${sets.join(', ')} WHERE id = @id`).run(params).changes === 0) throw new RowRejected('missing', `execution ${id} does not exist`);
  }

  private mustGet(id: string): Execution {
    const e = this.get(id);
    if (e === null) throw new RowRejected('missing', `execution ${id} does not exist`);
    return e;
  }
}
```

### Task 3.9: Cells and outcomes; stored roll-ups through core's `rollUp`; buckets

**Files:**
- Create: `src/schema/searchCells.ts`, `src/schema/cellOutcomes.ts`, `src/internal/outcomeRow.ts`, `src/internal/rollUps.ts`, `src/repositories/cells.ts`, `test/helpers/outcomes.ts`, `test/cells.rollup.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/schema/index.ts`, `test/helpers/factories.ts` (add `withNights`); regenerate the migration.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `rollUp` (WP1).
- Produces: `CellsRepo(db, clock, opts)` with `seed`, `outcomes`, `setOutcome`, `setOutcomesForProvider`, `markNoData`, `rollUps`, `buckets`, `bucketsPerDay`; internal `storeRollUps`.

Rules (ruling 2, ruling 4): every write that changes an outcome reads the cell's current outcomes for that stage inside its transaction, calls core's `rollUp`, and stores the result; WP3 has no roll-up logic of its own. `setOutcome` and `setOutcomesForProvider` set only `not_run`, `error` or `skipped`, never touch a row that is `priced`, `empty` or `no_data` (rule `terminal-outcome`), accept only `status`, `reason`, `lastError`, `attempts` and `incrementAttempts` (other data fields are rule `outcome-field`), and clear the provenance columns, which a non-terminal outcome does not carry. An `error` reason must be in the closed `ErrorReason` list (prov-009); a `skipped` reason a `SkipReason`.

```ts
import type { Cell } from '@gmt/core';

/** A Cell with its nights computed from the two calendar dates (UTC midnights, so no DST effect). */
export function withNights(c: CellKey): Cell {
  return { ...c, nights: (Date.parse(`${c.returnDate}T00:00:00Z`) - Date.parse(`${c.departureDate}T00:00:00Z`)) / 86_400_000 };
}
```

`packages/db/test/helpers/outcomes.ts` (ruling 4: the only way tests seed terminal states without a writer; it bypasses every repository rule and the stored roll-up, so tests that read roll-ups use the writers instead):

```ts
import type { CellKey, ProviderId, Stage } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../../src/client.js';

/** Test-only raw UPDATE of one outcome row. Never used to assert a roll-up that it alone produced. */
export function rawOutcome(
  db: Db, executionId: string, c: CellKey, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId,
  fields: { status: string; reason?: string | null; attempts?: number; minPence?: number; reusedFrom?: string; fetchedAt?: string },
): void {
  const terminal = ['priced', 'empty', 'no_data'].includes(fields.status);
  const changes = db.raw
    .prepare(
      `UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = @status, reason = @reason, attempts = @attempts, fetched_at = @fetchedAt, cabin = @cabin, point_of_sale = @pos,
         source_kind = @kind, min_pence = @minPence, reused_from_execution_id = @reusedFrom
       WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`,
    )
    .run({
      executionId, ...c, stage, providerId, status: fields.status, reason: fields.reason ?? null, attempts: fields.attempts ?? 0,
      fetchedAt: terminal ? (fields.fetchedAt ?? '2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z') : null, cabin: terminal ? 'economy' : null, pos: terminal ? 'unknown' : null,
      kind: terminal ? 'authorised-cached' : null, minPence: fields.status === 'priced' ? (fields.minPence ?? 100) : null, reusedFrom: fields.reusedFrom ?? null,
    }).changes;
  if (changes !== 1) throw new Error('rawOutcome matched no row');
}
```

`packages/db/test/cells.rollup.test.ts`:

```ts
import { type CellKey, type CellOutcome, type ProviderId, asIsoDate, cellKeyString, fixedClock, rollUp } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { CELL_KEY_SQL } from '../src/internal/outcomeRow.js';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { CellsRepo } from '../src/repositories/cells.js';
import { T0, freshDb, sqliteCode } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, cell, newExecution, withNights } from './helpers/factories.js';

const PROVIDERS: ProviderId[] = ['travelpayouts', 'searchapi'];
const dayCell = (j: number): CellKey => cell(undefined, undefined, `2027-03-${String(10 + j).padStart(2, '0')}`, `2027-04-${String(10 + j).padStart(2, '0')}`);
type Variant = Pick<CellOutcome, 'status' | 'reason'>;
const NON_TERMINAL: Variant[] = [
  { status: 'not_run', reason: null },
  { status: 'error', reason: 'upstream' },
  { status: 'error', reason: 'auth' },
  { status: 'skipped', reason: 'budget' },
  { status: 'skipped', reason: 'provider_disabled' },
  { status: 'skipped', reason: 'departed' },
  { status: 'skipped', reason: 'not_selected' },
];

function setup(cells: CellKey[], providers: ProviderId[] = PROVIDERS) {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
  const repo = new CellsRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic: false });
  repo.seed(execution.id, cells.map(withNights), providers);
  return { db, repo, executionId: execution.id };
}

/** The stored roll-up must equal core's rollUp over the cell's current outcomes (ruling 2: one implementation). */
function expectStoredMatchesCore(repo: CellsRepo, executionId: string, c: CellKey): void {
  expect(repo.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(c))?.discovery).toEqual(rollUp(repo.outcomes(executionId, { stage: 'discovery', cells: [c] })));
}

describe('CellsRepo seeding and stored roll-ups (spec §3, §6.6, §7)', () => {
  it('seed writes cells and not_run outcomes per discovery provider; verification has no rows (row 0 → NULL)', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup([cell(), cell(undefined, EWR)]);
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId)).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId, { providerId: 'searchapi', cells: [cell()] })).toEqual([
      { ...cell(), executionId, stage: 'discovery', providerId: 'searchapi', status: 'not_run', reason: null, attempts: 0, lastError: null, fetchedAt: null, minPence: null, cabin: null, pointOfSale: null, sourceKind: null, reusedFromExecutionId: null },
    ]);
    expect(repo.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(cell()))).toEqual({ discovery: { status: 'not_run', reason: null, minPence: null }, verification: { status: null, reason: null, minPence: null } });
    expect(repo.buckets(executionId, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts')).toEqual({ priced: 0, empty: 0, no_data: 0, error: 0, skipped: 0, not_run: 2, reused: 0 });
  });

  it('a stage with no provider configured has no outcome rows and a NULL roll-up', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup([cell()], []);
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId)).toEqual([]);
    expect(repo.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(cell()))?.discovery).toEqual({ status: null, reason: null, minPence: null });
  });

  it('every pair of non-terminal outcomes stores exactly what core rollUp returns (49 combinations)', () => {
    const cells = NON_TERMINAL.map((_, j) => dayCell(j));
    for (const a of NON_TERMINAL) {
      const { repo, executionId } = setup(cells);
      for (const [j, b] of NON_TERMINAL.entries()) {
        const c = dayCell(j);
        repo.setOutcome(executionId, c, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts', a);
        repo.setOutcome(executionId, c, 'discovery', 'searchapi', b);
        expectStoredMatchesCore(repo, executionId, c);
      }
    }
  });

  it('spec §6.6 rows that do not depend on reason rules: not_run beats error and skipped; all skipped keeps the priority', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup([cell()]);
    repo.setOutcome(executionId, cell(), 'discovery', 'travelpayouts', { status: 'error', reason: 'upstream' });
    expect(repo.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(cell()))?.discovery.status).toBe('not_run');
    repo.setOutcome(executionId, cell(), 'discovery', 'travelpayouts', { status: 'skipped', reason: 'not_selected' });
    repo.setOutcome(executionId, cell(), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: 'skipped', reason: 'budget' });
    expect(repo.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(cell()))?.discovery).toEqual({ status: 'skipped', reason: 'budget', minPence: null });
  });

  it('setOutcome rules: attempts, URL-free last_error, unknown row, closed reasons, no terminal status, no data fields', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup([cell()], ['searchapi']);
    repo.setOutcome(executionId, cell(), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: 'error', reason: 'network', lastError: 'socket hang up https://www.searchapi.io/api/v1/search?api_key=SECRET', incrementAttempts: true });
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId)[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'error', reason: 'network', attempts: 1, lastError: 'socket hang up https://www.searchapi.io/api/v1/search' });
    const set = (patch: Parameters<CellsRepo['setOutcome']>[4], c = cell()) => () => repo.setOutcome(executionId, c, 'discovery', 'searchapi', patch);
    expect(set({ status: 'error', reason: 'x' }, cell(undefined, EWR))).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(set({ status: 'skipped', reason: 'bored' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'skip-reason' }));
    expect(set({ status: 'error', reason: 'HTTP 500 https://www.searchapi.io/x?api_key=SECRET' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'error-reason' }));
    expect(set({ status: 'priced' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'terminal-outcome' }));
    expect(set({ status: 'no_data', reason: 'cabin-not-offered' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'terminal-outcome' }));
    expect(set({ minPence: 100 })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'outcome-field' }));
    expect(set({ sourceKind: 'synthetic' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'outcome-field' }));
  });

  it('setOutcomesForProvider and markNoData move one provider in bulk; terminal rows are never rewritten', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup([dayCell(0), dayCell(1), dayCell(2)]);
    repo.setOutcome(executionId, dayCell(2), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: 'error', reason: 'upstream' });
    expect(repo.setOutcomesForProvider(executionId, 'discovery', 'searchapi', ['not_run'], { status: 'skipped', reason: 'provider_disabled' })).toBe(2);
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId, { providerId: 'searchapi' }).map((o) => [o.status, o.reason])).toEqual([['skipped', 'provider_disabled'], ['skipped', 'provider_disabled'], ['error', 'upstream']]);
    expect(() => repo.setOutcomesForProvider(executionId, 'discovery', 'searchapi', ['not_run'], { status: 'skipped', reason: 'whim' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'skip-reason' }));
    expect(() => repo.setOutcomesForProvider(executionId, 'discovery', 'searchapi', ['priced'], { status: 'not_run', reason: null })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'terminal-outcome' }));
    expect(() => repo.setOutcomesForProvider(executionId, 'discovery', 'searchapi', ['not_run'], { status: 'empty', reason: null })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'terminal-outcome' }));
    expect(repo.markNoData(executionId, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts', 'cabin-not-offered')).toBe(3);
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId, { providerId: 'travelpayouts', cells: [dayCell(0)] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'no_data', reason: 'cabin-not-offered' });
    expectStoredMatchesCore(repo, executionId, dayCell(0));
    expect(repo.markNoData(executionId, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts', 'cabin-not-offered')).toBe(0); // only not_run rows move
    expect(() => repo.setOutcome(executionId, dayCell(0), 'discovery', 'travelpayouts', { status: 'not_run', reason: null })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'terminal-outcome' }));
  });

  it('bucketsPerDay groups the six buckets by departure date, stage and provider', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup([dayCell(0), cell(undefined, EWR, '2027-03-10', '2027-04-10'), dayCell(1)]);
    repo.setOutcome(executionId, dayCell(0), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: 'error', reason: 'upstream' });
    const m = repo.bucketsPerDay(executionId);
    expect([...m.keys()]).toEqual([asIsoDate('2027-03-10'), asIsoDate('2027-03-11')]);
    expect(m.get(asIsoDate('2027-03-10'))).toEqual([
      { stage: 'discovery', providerId: 'searchapi', buckets: { priced: 0, empty: 0, no_data: 0, error: 1, skipped: 0, not_run: 1, reused: 0 }, total: 2 },
      { stage: 'discovery', providerId: 'travelpayouts', buckets: { priced: 0, empty: 0, no_data: 0, error: 0, skipped: 0, not_run: 2, reused: 0 }, total: 2 },
    ]);
  });

  it('CELL_KEY_SQL builds exactly core cellKeyString, the key every map and json_each filter uses (arch-r2-008)', () => {
    const { db, executionId } = setup([cell(undefined, EWR)]);
    expect(db.raw.prepare(`SELECT ${CELL_KEY_SQL} AS k FROM search_cells WHERE execution_id = ?`).get(executionId)).toEqual({ k: cellKeyString(cell(undefined, EWR)) });
  });

  it('cell_outcomes CHECKs hold on their own: priced ⇔ min_pence, provenance on terminal rows, closed skip reasons (test-r2-004)', () => {
    const { db, executionId } = setup([cell()], ['travelpayouts']);
    const raw = (set: string) => () => db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE cell_outcomes SET ${set} WHERE execution_id = ?`).run(executionId);
    const PROV = "fetched_at = '2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z', cabin = 'economy', point_of_sale = 'unknown', source_kind = 'authorised-cached'";
    expect(sqliteCode(raw(`status = 'priced', min_pence = NULL, ${PROV}`))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(raw(`status = 'empty', min_pence = 5, ${PROV}`))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    for (const missing of ['fetched_at', 'cabin', 'point_of_sale', 'source_kind']) {
      for (const status of ['priced', 'empty', 'no_data']) {
        expect(sqliteCode(raw(`status = '${status}', min_pence = ${status === 'priced' ? 100 : 'NULL'}, ${PROV}, ${missing} = NULL`)), `${status} without ${missing}`).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
      }
    }
    expect(sqliteCode(raw(`status = 'skipped', reason = 'whim'`))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    raw(`status = 'no_data', reason = 'cabin-not-offered'`)(); // the one data-less terminal outcome
    raw(`status = 'priced', reason = NULL, min_pence = 100, ${PROV}`)(); // positive control
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT status FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = ?').get(executionId)).toEqual({ status: 'priced' });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { check, integer, primaryKey, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { OUTCOME_STATUSES, SELECTION_REASONS } from '../internal/enums.js';
import { isBool, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/** Spec §7 `search_cells` with the stored roll-ups (spec §6.6). NULL status = no provider or no outcome rows yet. `best_observation_id` gains its FK in Task 3.13. */
export const searchCells = sqliteTable(
  'search_cells',
  {
    executionId: text('execution_id').notNull().references(() => searchExecutions.id),
    origin: text('origin').notNull(),
    destination: text('destination').notNull(),
    departureDate: text('departure_date').notNull(),
    returnDate: text('return_date').notNull(),
    discoveryStatus: text('discovery_status'),
    discoveryReason: text('discovery_reason'),
    discoveryMinPence: integer('discovery_min_pence'),
    verificationSelected: integer('verification_selected').notNull().default(0),
    selectionReason: text('selection_reason'),
    verificationStatus: text('verification_status'),
    verificationReason: text('verification_reason'),
    verificationMinPence: integer('verification_min_pence'),
    bestObservationId: integer('best_observation_id'),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.executionId, t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate] }),
    check('search_cells_discovery_status_ck', isOneOf('discovery_status', OUTCOME_STATUSES, true)),
    check('search_cells_verification_status_ck', isOneOf('verification_status', OUTCOME_STATUSES, true)),
    check('search_cells_selected_ck', isBool('verification_selected')),
    check('search_cells_selection_reason_ck', isOneOf('selection_reason', SELECTION_REASONS, true)),
    check('search_cells_discovery_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('discovery_min_pence')),
    check('search_cells_verification_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('verification_min_pence')),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/schema/cellOutcomes.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, foreignKey, index, integer, primaryKey, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { CABINS, OUTCOME_STATUSES, PROVIDER_IDS, SOURCE_KINDS, STAGES } from '../internal/enums.js';
import { isIsoTimestamp, isInteger, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf, isPointOfSale } from './checks.js';
import { searchCells } from './searchCells.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/**
 * Spec §7 `cell_outcomes`. `reason` never holds a vendor message. Priced, empty and fetched no_data outcomes carry
 * their provenance columns (ruling 5); a cabin-not-offered no_data is the one data-less terminal outcome.
 */
export const cellOutcomes = sqliteTable(
  'cell_outcomes',
  {
    executionId: text('execution_id').notNull(),
    origin: text('origin').notNull(),
    destination: text('destination').notNull(),
    departureDate: text('departure_date').notNull(),
    returnDate: text('return_date').notNull(),
    stage: text('stage').notNull(),
    providerId: text('provider_id').notNull(),
    status: text('status').notNull(),
    reason: text('reason'),
    attempts: integer('attempts').notNull().default(0),
    lastError: text('last_error'),
    fetchedAt: text('fetched_at'),
    minPence: integer('min_pence'),
    cabin: text('cabin'),
    pointOfSale: text('point_of_sale'),
    sourceKind: text('source_kind'),
    reusedFromExecutionId: text('reused_from_execution_id').references(() => searchExecutions.id),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.executionId, t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate, t.stage, t.providerId] }),
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.executionId, t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate], foreignColumns: [searchCells.executionId, searchCells.origin, searchCells.destination, searchCells.departureDate, searchCells.returnDate] }),
    check('cell_outcomes_status_ck', isOneOf('status', OUTCOME_STATUSES)),
    check('cell_outcomes_stage_ck', isOneOf('stage', STAGES)),
    check('cell_outcomes_provider_ck', isOneOf('provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS)),
    check('cell_outcomes_skip_reason_ck', sql.raw("status <> 'skipped' OR reason IN ('not_selected', 'budget', 'provider_disabled', 'departed')")),
    check('cell_outcomes_attempts_ck', isInteger('attempts')),
    check('cell_outcomes_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('min_pence')),
    check('cell_outcomes_priced_min_ck', sql.raw("(status = 'priced') = (min_pence IS NOT NULL)")),
    check('cell_outcomes_cabin_ck', isOneOf('cabin', CABINS, true)),
    check('cell_outcomes_pos_ck', isPointOfSale('point_of_sale', true)),
    check('cell_outcomes_kind_ck', isOneOf('source_kind', SOURCE_KINDS, true)),
    check('cell_outcomes_fetched_ck', isIsoTimestamp('fetched_at', true)),
    // why: ruling 5 (prov-004). Reuse matches on these columns, so a terminal outcome without them could never be matched or would match wrongly.
    check(
      'cell_outcomes_provenance_ck',
      sql.raw(
        "status NOT IN ('priced', 'empty', 'no_data') OR (status = 'no_data' AND reason = 'cabin-not-offered') OR " +
          '(fetched_at IS NOT NULL AND cabin IS NOT NULL AND point_of_sale IS NOT NULL AND source_kind IS NOT NULL)',
      ),
    ),
    index('cell_outcomes_stage_provider_status_idx').on(t.executionId, t.stage, t.providerId, t.status),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/internal/outcomeRow.ts`:

```ts
import type { CellKey, CellOutcome } from '@gmt/core';
import { CABINS, OUTCOME_STATUSES, PROVIDER_IDS, SOURCE_KINDS, STAGES } from './enums.js';
import { nullableOneOf, oneOf, readDate, readIata, readNullableTime } from './read.js';

/** A `cell_outcomes` row as SQLite returns it (every enum column a plain string, narrowed by `toOutcome`). */
export interface OutcomeDbRow {
  execution_id: string; origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string; stage: string; provider_id: string; status: string;
  reason: string | null; attempts: number; last_error: string | null; fetched_at: string | null; min_pence: number | null; cabin: string | null;
  point_of_sale: string | null; source_kind: string | null; reused_from_execution_id: string | null;
}

const C = 'cell_outcomes';
export function toOutcome(r: OutcomeDbRow): CellOutcome {
  return {
    executionId: r.execution_id, origin: readIata(`${C}.origin`, r.origin), destination: readIata(`${C}.destination`, r.destination),
    departureDate: readDate(`${C}.departure_date`, r.departure_date), returnDate: readDate(`${C}.return_date`, r.return_date),
    stage: oneOf(`${C}.stage`, STAGES, r.stage), providerId: oneOf(`${C}.provider_id`, PROVIDER_IDS, r.provider_id), status: oneOf(`${C}.status`, OUTCOME_STATUSES, r.status),
    reason: r.reason, attempts: r.attempts, lastError: r.last_error, fetchedAt: readNullableTime(`${C}.fetched_at`, r.fetched_at), minPence: r.min_pence,
    cabin: nullableOneOf(`${C}.cabin`, CABINS, r.cabin), pointOfSale: r.point_of_sale, sourceKind: nullableOneOf(`${C}.source_kind`, SOURCE_KINDS, r.source_kind),
    reusedFromExecutionId: r.reused_from_execution_id,
  };
}

/** WHERE fragment for one cell of one execution, with @executionId/@origin/@destination/@departureDate/@returnDate. */
export const CELL_WHERE = 'execution_id = @executionId AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate';
/** core's `cellKeyString` format built in SQL; the one place db spells it (arch-r2-008, pinned by a test against core). */
export const CELL_KEY_SQL = "(origin || '|' || destination || '|' || departure_date || '|' || return_date)";
export const keyParams = (executionId: string, c: CellKey) => ({ executionId, origin: c.origin, destination: c.destination, departureDate: c.departureDate, returnDate: c.returnDate });

export interface CellKeyDbRow { origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string }
export const toCellKey = (r: CellKeyDbRow): CellKey => ({
  origin: readIata('origin', r.origin), destination: readIata('destination', r.destination), departureDate: readDate('departure_date', r.departure_date), returnDate: readDate('return_date', r.return_date),
});
```

`packages/db/src/internal/rollUps.ts`:

```ts
import { type CellKey, type CellOutcome, type Stage, cellKeyString, rollUp } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { CELL_WHERE, type CellKeyDbRow, type OutcomeDbRow, keyParams, toCellKey, toOutcome } from './outcomeRow.js';

const UPDATE: Record<Stage, string> = {
  discovery: `UPDATE search_cells SET discovery_status = @status, discovery_reason = @reason, discovery_min_pence = @minPence WHERE ${CELL_WHERE}`,
  verification: `UPDATE search_cells SET verification_status = @status, verification_reason = @reason, verification_min_pence = @minPence WHERE ${CELL_WHERE}`,
};

/**
 * Stores spec §6.6 roll-ups computed by core's `rollUp` (ruling 2) for the given cells, or for every cell of the
 * execution. The caller holds the write transaction, so the outcomes read here are the ones just written.
 */
export function storeRollUps(db: Db, executionId: string, stage: Stage, cells?: readonly CellKey[]): void {
  const byCell = new Map<string, CellOutcome[]>();
  let keys: readonly CellKey[];
  if (cells === undefined) {
    keys = db.raw.prepare<[string], CellKeyDbRow>('SELECT origin, destination, departure_date, return_date FROM search_cells WHERE execution_id = ?').all(executionId).map(toCellKey);
    for (const r of db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>('SELECT * FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND stage = @stage').all({ executionId, stage })) {
      const o = toOutcome(r);
      const list = byCell.get(cellKeyString(o)) ?? [];
      list.push(o);
      byCell.set(cellKeyString(o), list);
    }
  } else {
    keys = cells;
    const select = db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(`SELECT * FROM cell_outcomes WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = @stage`);
    for (const c of cells) byCell.set(cellKeyString(c), select.all({ ...keyParams(executionId, c), stage }).map(toOutcome));
  }
  const update = db.raw.prepare(UPDATE[stage]);
  for (const c of keys) {
    const r = rollUp(byCell.get(cellKeyString(c)) ?? []);
    update.run({ ...keyParams(executionId, c), status: r.status, reason: r.reason, minPence: r.minPence });
  }
}
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/cells.ts` (Task 3.9 methods; Tasks 3.11–3.15 add the rest):

```ts
import { type Buckets, type Cell, type CellKey, type CellOutcome, type CellOutcomeStatus, type Clock, type IsoDate, type NoDataReason, type ProviderId, type RollUp, type Stage, cellKeyString, stripUrlsInText } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { OUTCOME_STATUSES, PROVIDER_IDS, SKIP_REASONS, STAGES, TERMINAL_STATUSES } from '../internal/enums.js';
import { CELL_KEY_SQL, CELL_WHERE, type OutcomeDbRow, keyParams, toCellKey, toOutcome } from '../internal/outcomeRow.js';
import { nullableOneOf, oneOf, readDate } from '../internal/read.js';
import { storeRollUps } from '../internal/rollUps.js';
import { RowRejected, assertErrorReason, assertInt } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { RepoOptions } from '../types.js';

interface CellDbRow {
  origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string; discovery_status: string | null; discovery_reason: string | null; discovery_min_pence: number | null;
  verification_status: string | null; verification_reason: string | null; verification_min_pence: number | null;
}
interface BucketRow { priced: number; empty: number; no_data: number; error: number; skipped: number; not_run: number; reused: number; total: number }
const BUCKET_COLUMNS = `COALESCE(SUM(status = 'priced'), 0) AS priced, COALESCE(SUM(status = 'empty'), 0) AS empty, COALESCE(SUM(status = 'no_data'), 0) AS no_data,
  COALESCE(SUM(status = 'error'), 0) AS error, COALESCE(SUM(status = 'skipped'), 0) AS skipped, COALESCE(SUM(status = 'not_run'), 0) AS not_run,
  COALESCE(SUM(reused_from_execution_id IS NOT NULL), 0) AS reused, COUNT(*) AS total`;
const toBuckets = (r: BucketRow | undefined): Buckets => ({ priced: r?.priced ?? 0, empty: r?.empty ?? 0, no_data: r?.no_data ?? 0, error: r?.error ?? 0, skipped: r?.skipped ?? 0, not_run: r?.not_run ?? 0, reused: r?.reused ?? 0 });
const isTerminal = (s: CellOutcomeStatus) => TERMINAL_STATUSES.some((t) => t === s);
/** Fields `setOutcome` may carry (ruling 4); identity fields are ignored, every other data field is refused. */
const DATA_FIELDS = ['lastError', 'fetchedAt', 'minPence', 'cabin', 'pointOfSale', 'sourceKind', 'reusedFromExecutionId'] as const satisfies ReadonlyArray<keyof CellOutcome>;
const CLEARED = 'fetched_at = NULL, min_pence = NULL, cabin = NULL, point_of_sale = NULL, source_kind = NULL, reused_from_execution_id = NULL';

/** A non-terminal target and its reason, per spec §3 (ruling 4, prov-009). */
function assertNonTerminalTarget(status: CellOutcomeStatus, reason: string | null): void {
  if (isTerminal(status)) throw new RowRejected('terminal-outcome', `${status} is written only by writeTile, writeVerifiedCell, copyReused or markNoData`);
  if (status === 'skipped' && (reason === null || !SKIP_REASONS.some((r) => r === reason))) throw new RowRejected('skip-reason', `a skipped outcome needs a skip reason (spec §3), got ${String(reason)}`);
  if (status === 'error') assertErrorReason(reason);
}

/**
 * Spec §7 `search_cells` + `cell_outcomes` and the stored roll-ups. Every method that changes an outcome stores the
 * affected roll-ups, computed by core's `rollUp`, in the same transaction.
 */
export class CellsRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
    private readonly opts: RepoOptions,
  ) {}

  /** Spec §7 atomic unit "seeding search_cells + discovery cell_outcomes at start". */
  seed(executionId: string, cells: Cell[], discoveryProviders: ProviderId[]): void {
    const insertCell = this.db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO search_cells (execution_id, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, discovery_status, verification_selected) VALUES (@executionId, @origin, @destination, @departureDate, @returnDate, @status, 0)`);
    const insertOutcome = this.db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO cell_outcomes (execution_id, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, stage, provider_id, status, attempts) VALUES (@executionId, @origin, @destination, @departureDate, @returnDate, 'discovery', @providerId, 'not_run', 0)`);
    const status = discoveryProviders.length > 0 ? 'not_run' : null;
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const c of cells) {
        insertCell.run({ ...keyParams(executionId, c), status });
        for (const providerId of discoveryProviders) insertOutcome.run({ ...keyParams(executionId, c), providerId });
      }
    });
  }

  outcomes(executionId: string, filter: { stage?: Stage; providerId?: ProviderId; cells?: CellKey[] } = {}): CellOutcome[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(
        `SELECT * FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND (@stage IS NULL OR stage = @stage) AND (@providerId IS NULL OR provider_id = @providerId)
           AND (@keys IS NULL OR ${CELL_KEY_SQL} IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@keys)))
         ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, stage, provider_id`,
      )
      .all({ executionId, stage: filter.stage ?? null, providerId: filter.providerId ?? null, keys: filter.cells === undefined ? null : JSON.stringify(filter.cells.map(cellKeyString)) })
      .map(toOutcome);
  }

  /**
   * Moves one outcome to `not_run`, `error` or `skipped` (abort, budget skip, normalisation error) and stores the cell's
   * roll-up, in one transaction. Never touches a priced, empty or no_data row (ruling 4).
   */
  setOutcome(executionId: string, cell: CellKey, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId, patch: Partial<CellOutcome> & { incrementAttempts?: boolean }): void {
    for (const f of DATA_FIELDS) if (f in patch && f !== 'lastError') throw new RowRejected('outcome-field', `setOutcome cannot write ${f}; data comes with writeTile, writeVerifiedCell or copyReused`);
    const key = { ...keyParams(executionId, cell), stage, providerId };
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      const current = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(`SELECT * FROM cell_outcomes WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`).get(key);
      if (current === undefined) throw new RowRejected('missing', `no ${stage} outcome for ${providerId} at ${cellKeyString(cell)} in ${executionId}`);
      const existing = toOutcome(current);
      if (isTerminal(existing.status)) throw new RowRejected('terminal-outcome', `${cellKeyString(cell)} is ${existing.status}; spec §7 never rewrites priced, empty or no_data`);
      const status = patch.status ?? existing.status;
      const reason = patch.reason !== undefined ? patch.reason : existing.reason;
      assertNonTerminalTarget(status, reason);
      if (patch.attempts !== undefined) assertInt('attempts', patch.attempts);
      const attempts = (patch.attempts ?? existing.attempts) + (patch.incrementAttempts === true ? 1 : 0);
      const lastError = patch.lastError !== undefined ? (patch.lastError === null ? null : stripUrlsInText(patch.lastError)) : existing.lastError;
      this.db.raw
        .prepare(`UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = @status, reason = @reason, attempts = @attempts, last_error = @lastError, ${CLEARED} WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`)
        .run({ ...key, status, reason, attempts, lastError });
      storeRollUps(this.db, executionId, stage, [cell]);
    });
  }

  /** Moves every outcome of one (stage, provider) in `from` to a non-terminal `to` (provider disabled, budget skip). Returns the count. */
  setOutcomesForProvider(executionId: string, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId, from: CellOutcomeStatus[], to: { status: CellOutcomeStatus; reason: string | null }): number {
    if (from.some(isTerminal)) throw new RowRejected('terminal-outcome', 'priced, empty and no_data outcomes are never rewritten (spec §7)');
    assertNonTerminalTarget(to.status, to.reason);
    return this.db.transaction(() => {
      const changes = this.db.raw
        .prepare(`UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = @status, reason = @reason, ${CLEARED} WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId AND status IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@from))`)
        .run({ executionId, stage, providerId, from: JSON.stringify(from), status: to.status, reason: to.reason }).changes;
      if (changes > 0) storeRollUps(this.db, executionId, stage);
      return changes;
    });
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 cabin-not-offered: the provider is not called; its not_run outcomes become no_data with the reason. */
  markNoData(executionId: string, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId, reason: NoDataReason): number {
    return this.db.transaction(() => {
      const changes = this.db.raw
        .prepare(`UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = 'no_data', reason = @reason WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId AND status = 'not_run'`)
        .run({ executionId, stage, providerId, reason }).changes;
      if (changes > 0) storeRollUps(this.db, executionId, stage);
      return changes;
    });
  }

  rollUps(executionId: string): Map<string, { discovery: RollUp; verification: RollUp }> {
    const out = new Map<string, { discovery: RollUp; verification: RollUp }>();
    for (const r of this.db.raw.prepare<[string], CellDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_cells WHERE execution_id = ? ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date').all(executionId)) {
      out.set(cellKeyString(toCellKey(r)), {
        discovery: { status: nullableOneOf('search_cells.discovery_status', OUTCOME_STATUSES, r.discovery_status), reason: r.discovery_reason, minPence: r.discovery_min_pence },
        verification: { status: nullableOneOf('search_cells.verification_status', OUTCOME_STATUSES, r.verification_status), reason: r.verification_reason, minPence: r.verification_min_pence },
      });
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** The six buckets of spec §3 plus `reused` for one stage and provider. */
  buckets(executionId: string, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId): Buckets {
    return toBuckets(this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, BucketRow>(`SELECT ${BUCKET_COLUMNS} FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`).get({ executionId, stage, providerId }));
  }

  /** Per departure date (View B): buckets per (stage, provider), dates ascending. */
  bucketsPerDay(executionId: string): Map<IsoDate, Array<{ stage: Stage; providerId: ProviderId; buckets: Buckets; total: number }>> {
    const out = new Map<IsoDate, Array<{ stage: Stage; providerId: ProviderId; buckets: Buckets; total: number }>>();
    const rows = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], BucketRow & { day: string; stage: string; providerId: string }>(
        `SELECT departure_date AS day, stage, provider_id AS providerId, ${BUCKET_COLUMNS} FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = ? GROUP BY departure_date, stage, provider_id ORDER BY departure_date, stage, provider_id`,
      )
      .all(executionId);
    for (const r of rows) {
      const day = readDate('cell_outcomes.departure_date', r.day);
      const list = out.get(day) ?? [];
      list.push({ stage: oneOf('cell_outcomes.stage', STAGES, r.stage), providerId: oneOf('cell_outcomes.provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS, r.providerId), buckets: toBuckets(r), total: r.total });
      out.set(day, list);
    }
    return out;
  }
}
```
