```ts
import { asIsoDate, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Db } from '../src/client.js';
import { CalendarPricesRepo } from '../src/repositories/calendarPrices.js';
import { ExecutionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/executions.js';
import { T0, at, count, freshDb, sqliteCode } from './helpers/db.js';
import { calendarPrice, cell, newExecution } from './helpers/factories.js';

function setup(allowSynthetic = false) {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
  return { db, clock, executionId: execution.id, repo: new CalendarPricesRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic }) };
}

/** Raw insert bypassing the repository, to prove the SQL constraints hold on their own. */
function rawInsert(db: Db, executionId: string, over: Record<string, unknown>): void {
  const row = {
    execution_id: executionId, provider_id: 'travelpayouts', source_kind: 'authorised-cached', cabin: 'economy', origin: 'LHR', destination: 'JFK', departure_date: '2027-03-14',
    return_date: '2027-03-28', price_pence: 41_200, price_native_minor: 41_200, currency_native: 'GBP', fx_rate_nano: null, fx_rate_date: null, fx_source: null, fx_converted: 0,
    point_of_sale: 'unknown', fetched_at: T0, ...over,
  };
  const cols = Object.keys(row);
  db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO calendar_prices (${cols.join(', ')}) VALUES (${cols.map((c) => `@${c}`).join(', ')})`).run(row);
}

describe('calendar_prices writes (spec §5.5, §6.3, §7)', () => {
  it('stores provenance explicitly and reads it back: max_age_hours NULL ⇔ unknown, FX columns when converted', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup();
    const converted = calendarPrice(cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-15', '2027-03-29'), {
      providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-aggregate', maxAgeHours: null, pointOfSale: 'GB', currencyNative: 'USD', priceNativeMinor: 54_900, pricePence: 41_175,
      fx: { converted: true, rateNano: 750_000_000, rateDate: asIsoDate('2026-10-13'), source: 'frankfurter' }, providerObservedAt: at('2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z'),
    });
    const ids = repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell()), converted]);
    expect(ids).toHaveLength(2);
    const [first, second] = repo.fetchedBy(executionId);
    expect(first).toMatchObject({ id: ids[0], executionId, maxAgeHours: 48, provenance: { providerId: 'travelpayouts', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached', fetchedAt: T0, maxAgeHours: 48, fx: { converted: false }, pointOfSale: 'unknown', cabin: 'economy' } });
    expect(first?.provenance).not.toHaveProperty('providerObservedAt');
    expect(second).toMatchObject({ maxAgeHours: null, currencyNative: 'USD', provenance: { maxAgeHours: 'unknown', providerObservedAt: '2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z', fx: { converted: true, rateNano: 750_000_000, rateDate: '2026-10-13', source: 'frankfurter' } } });
  });

  it('provider ⇔ kind, both directions, in the repository and in SQL; the gate refuses fixture rows unless allowed', () => {
    const { db, repo, executionId } = setup(true);
    const write = (over: Parameters<typeof calendarPrice>[1]) => () => repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), over)]);
    expect(write({ providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'provider-kind' }));
    expect(write({ providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'synthetic' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'provider-kind' }));
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { provider_id: 'fixture', source_kind: 'authorised-cached' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { provider_id: 'searchapi', source_kind: 'synthetic' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(write({ providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'synthetic' })).not.toThrow();
    const closed = new CalendarPricesRepo(db, fixedClock(T0), { allowSynthetic: false });
    expect(() => closed.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-20', '2027-04-03'), { providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'synthetic' })])).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'synthetic-gate' }));
  });

  it('FX CHECK constraints, both directions, and price_pence = price_native_minor when unconverted', () => {
    const { db, repo, executionId } = setup();
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fx_converted: 1, currency_native: 'GBP', fx_rate_nano: 1, fx_rate_date: '2026-10-13', fx_source: 'x' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fx_converted: 0, currency_native: 'USD' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fx_converted: 1, currency_native: 'USD', fx_rate_nano: null, fx_rate_date: '2026-10-13', fx_source: 'x' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    const write = (over: Parameters<typeof calendarPrice>[1]) => () => repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), over)]);
    expect(write({ currencyNative: 'USD' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'fx' }));
    expect(write({ priceNativeMinor: 41_201 })).toThrow(/must equal price_native_minor/);
    expect(write({ fx: { converted: true, rateNano: 1, rateDate: asIsoDate('2026-10-13'), source: 'x' } })).toThrow(/GBP price cannot be FX-converted/);
  });

  it('price tables reject non-integers and non-toISOString timestamps (repository and SQL); a numeric text is stored as an integer by affinity', () => {
    const { db, repo, executionId } = setup();
    const write = (over: Parameters<typeof calendarPrice>[1]) => () => repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), over)]);
    expect(write({ pricePence: 412.5, priceNativeMinor: 412.5 })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'integer' }));
    // why: a non-ISO timestamp cannot reach the typed repository without a cast; the SQL CHECK below is its guard (arch-r2-006).
    expect(write({ maxAgeHours: 1.5 })).toThrow(/max_age_hours/);
    expect(write({ pointOfSale: 'gb' })).toThrow(/point_of_sale/);
    expect(write({ reusedFromExecutionId: 'someone-else' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'reused' }));
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { price_pence: 412.5, price_native_minor: 412.5 }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { price_pence: 'abc', price_native_minor: 'abc' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fetched_at: '2026-10-14T12:00:00Z' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fetched_at: '2026-10-14 12:00:00.000' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(0);
    rawInsert(db, executionId, { price_pence: '412', price_native_minor: '412' }); // test-017: INTEGER affinity stores 412, which is an integer
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT typeof(price_pence) AS t, price_pence AS p FROM calendar_prices').get()).toEqual({ t: 'integer', p: 412 });
  });

  it('UNIQUE (execution, provider, cell): a second row for the same key throws; another provider or execution does not', () => {
    const { db, clock, repo, executionId } = setup();
    repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell())]);
    expect(sqliteCode(() => repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), { pricePence: 40_000, priceNativeMinor: 40_000 })]))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE');
    repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), { providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-aggregate', pointOfSale: 'GB' })]);
    repo.insert(newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'completed' }).execution.id, [calendarPrice(cell())]);
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(3);
  });
});

describe('plan Review Focus 5: a SqliteError inside a repository transaction', () => {
  it('propagates with its SQLite code and message, holds no URL, and rolls the whole transaction back', () => {
    const { db, clock, repo, executionId } = setup();
    const link = 'https://www.aviasales.com/search/LON1403NYC28031?marker=SECRET-TOKEN-0123456789';
    let thrown: unknown;
    try {
      db.transaction(() => {
        repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), { vendorLink: link })]);
        repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), { vendorLink: link })]); // same key
      });
    } catch (e) {
      thrown = e;
    }
    if (!(thrown instanceof Database.SqliteError)) throw new Error('expected a SqliteError');
    expect(thrown.code).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE');
    expect(thrown.message).toMatch(/^UNIQUE constraint failed: calendar_prices\./);
    expect(thrown.message).not.toMatch(/https?:|SECRET|marker=/);
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(0);
    // the engine records it on the execution (WP7); the column never holds a URL query or userinfo
    const executions = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock);
    executions.setStatus(executionId, 'paused_error', { errorText: `${thrown.message} while writing tile 3 (${link.replace('https://', 'https://user:pw@')})` });
    const text = executions.get(executionId)?.errorText ?? '';
    expect(text).toContain('UNIQUE constraint failed: calendar_prices.');
    expect(text).not.toMatch(/SECRET|marker=|pw@/);
  });
});
```

`packages/db/src/schema/calendarPrices.ts`:

```ts
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text, uniqueIndex } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { PROVIDER_IDS } from '../internal/enums.js';
import { isOneOf, isProviderKind, priceTableChecks } from './checks.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/** Spec §7 `calendar_prices`: insert-only, one row per (execution, provider, cell); history reads only rows not reused. */
export const calendarPrices = sqliteTable(
  'calendar_prices',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    executionId: text('execution_id').notNull().references(() => searchExecutions.id),
    providerId: text('provider_id').notNull(),
    sourceKind: text('source_kind').notNull(),
    cabin: text('cabin').notNull(),
    origin: text('origin').notNull(),
    destination: text('destination').notNull(),
    departureDate: text('departure_date').notNull(),
    returnDate: text('return_date').notNull(),
    pricePence: integer('price_pence').notNull(),
    priceNativeMinor: integer('price_native_minor').notNull(),
    currencyNative: text('currency_native').notNull(),
    fxRateNano: integer('fx_rate_nano'),
    fxRateDate: text('fx_rate_date'),
    fxSource: text('fx_source'),
    fxConverted: integer('fx_converted').notNull(),
    pointOfSale: text('point_of_sale').notNull(),
    fetchedAt: text('fetched_at').notNull(),
    providerObservedAt: text('provider_observed_at'),
    maxAgeHours: integer('max_age_hours'),
    vendorLink: text('vendor_link'),
    reusedFromExecutionId: text('reused_from_execution_id').references(() => searchExecutions.id),
  },
  (t) => [
    uniqueIndex('calendar_prices_execution_provider_cell_uq').on(t.executionId, t.providerId, t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate),
    index('calendar_prices_cell_fetched_idx').on(t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate, t.fetchedAt),
    index('calendar_prices_route_fetched_idx').on(t.origin, t.destination, t.fetchedAt),
    index('calendar_prices_execution_idx').on(t.executionId),
    ...priceTableChecks('calendar_prices'),
    check('calendar_prices_provider_ck', isOneOf('provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS)),
    check('calendar_prices_provider_kind_ck', isProviderKind()),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/internal/priceRow.ts`:

```ts
import type { FxInfo, Provenance } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import type { CalendarPriceInsert, CalendarPriceRow } from '../types.js';
import { CABINS, PROVIDER_IDS, SOURCE_KINDS } from './enums.js';
import { RowCorrupt, oneOf, readDate, readIata, readNullableTime, readTime } from './read.js';
import { RowRejected, assertIsoDate, assertIsoDateTime, assertNullableInt, assertPointOfSale, assertPriceMoney, assertProviderKind } from './validate.js';

export interface CalendarPriceDbRow {
  id: number; execution_id: string; provider_id: string; source_kind: string; cabin: string; origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string;
  price_pence: number; price_native_minor: number; currency_native: string; fx_rate_nano: number | null; fx_rate_date: string | null; fx_source: string | null; fx_converted: number;
  point_of_sale: string; fetched_at: string; provider_observed_at: string | null; max_age_hours: number | null; vendor_link: string | null; reused_from_execution_id: string | null;
}

/** Every repository rule for a calendar price (spec §5.5, §6.3, §7). `fresh` refuses a reused marker (reuse goes through copyReused). */
export function validateCalendarPrice(row: CalendarPriceInsert, allowSynthetic: boolean, fresh: boolean): void {
  assertProviderKind(row.providerId, row.sourceKind, allowSynthetic);
  assertPriceMoney(row);
  assertIsoDateTime('fetched_at', row.fetchedAt);
  if (row.providerObservedAt !== null) assertIsoDateTime('provider_observed_at', row.providerObservedAt);
  assertNullableInt('max_age_hours', row.maxAgeHours);
  assertPointOfSale(row.pointOfSale);
  assertIsoDate('departure_date', row.cell.departureDate);
  assertIsoDate('return_date', row.cell.returnDate);
  if (fresh && row.reusedFromExecutionId !== null) throw new RowRejected('reused', 'a fetched price cannot carry reused_from_execution_id; reuse goes through CellsRepo.copyReused');
}

const INSERT_SQL = `
INSERT INTO calendar_prices (execution_id, provider_id, source_kind, cabin, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, price_pence, price_native_minor,
  currency_native, fx_rate_nano, fx_rate_date, fx_source, fx_converted, point_of_sale, fetched_at, provider_observed_at, max_age_hours, vendor_link, reused_from_execution_id)
VALUES (@executionId, @providerId, @sourceKind, @cabin, @origin, @destination, @departureDate, @returnDate, @pricePence, @priceNativeMinor,
  @currencyNative, @fxRateNano, @fxRateDate, @fxSource, @fxConverted, @pointOfSale, @fetchedAt, @providerObservedAt, @maxAgeHours, @vendorLink, @reusedFrom)`;

/** Inserts one validated row; the caller owns the transaction. `max_age_hours` NULL = unknown, written explicitly (spec §7). */
export function insertCalendarPrice(db: Db, executionId: string, row: CalendarPriceInsert): number {
  const fx = row.fx;
  return Number(
    db.raw.prepare(INSERT_SQL).run({
      executionId, providerId: row.providerId, sourceKind: row.sourceKind, cabin: row.cabin, origin: row.cell.origin, destination: row.cell.destination,
      departureDate: row.cell.departureDate, returnDate: row.cell.returnDate, pricePence: row.pricePence, priceNativeMinor: row.priceNativeMinor, currencyNative: row.currencyNative,
      fxRateNano: fx.converted ? fx.rateNano : null, fxRateDate: fx.converted ? fx.rateDate : null, fxSource: fx.converted ? fx.source : null, fxConverted: fx.converted ? 1 : 0,
      pointOfSale: row.pointOfSale, fetchedAt: row.fetchedAt, providerObservedAt: row.providerObservedAt, maxAgeHours: row.maxAgeHours, vendorLink: row.vendorLink, reusedFrom: row.reusedFromExecutionId,
    }).lastInsertRowid,
  );
}

/** FX info from the four stored columns; converted without rate data is corruption the CHECK should have prevented. */
export function fxOf(column: string, r: { fx_converted: number; fx_rate_nano: number | null; fx_rate_date: string | null; fx_source: string | null }): FxInfo {
  if (r.fx_converted !== 1) return { converted: false };
  if (r.fx_rate_nano === null || r.fx_rate_date === null || r.fx_source === null) throw new RowCorrupt(column, 'converted without rate data');
  return { converted: true, rateNano: r.fx_rate_nano, rateDate: readDate(column, r.fx_rate_date), source: r.fx_source };
}

/** Rebuilds the row and its `Provenance` from the stored columns; nothing is defaulted (spec §3). */
export function toCalendarPriceRow(r: CalendarPriceDbRow): CalendarPriceRow {
  const c = 'calendar_prices';
  const fx = fxOf(c, r);
  const providerId = oneOf(`${c}.provider_id`, PROVIDER_IDS, r.provider_id);
  const sourceKind = oneOf(`${c}.source_kind`, SOURCE_KINDS, r.source_kind);
  const cabin = oneOf(`${c}.cabin`, CABINS, r.cabin);
  const fetchedAt = readTime(`${c}.fetched_at`, r.fetched_at);
  const providerObservedAt = readNullableTime(`${c}.provider_observed_at`, r.provider_observed_at);
  const provenance: Provenance = { providerId, sourceKind, fetchedAt, ...(providerObservedAt === null ? {} : { providerObservedAt }), maxAgeHours: r.max_age_hours ?? 'unknown', fx, pointOfSale: r.point_of_sale, cabin };
  return {
    id: r.id, executionId: r.execution_id, provenance, providerId, sourceKind, cabin,
    cell: { origin: readIata(`${c}.origin`, r.origin), destination: readIata(`${c}.destination`, r.destination), departureDate: readDate(`${c}.departure_date`, r.departure_date), returnDate: readDate(`${c}.return_date`, r.return_date) },
    pricePence: r.price_pence, priceNativeMinor: r.price_native_minor, currencyNative: r.currency_native, fx, pointOfSale: r.point_of_sale, fetchedAt, providerObservedAt,
    maxAgeHours: r.max_age_hours, vendorLink: r.vendor_link, reusedFromExecutionId: r.reused_from_execution_id,
  };
}
```

```ts
import type { Clock } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { type CalendarPriceDbRow, insertCalendarPrice, toCalendarPriceRow, validateCalendarPrice } from '../internal/priceRow.js';
import type { CalendarPriceInsert, CalendarPriceRow, RepoOptions } from '../types.js';

/** Spec §7 `calendar_prices`. Insert-only except the reused-row supersede. */
export class CalendarPricesRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
    private readonly opts: RepoOptions,
  ) {}

  /** Validates every row, then inserts all in one transaction and returns the ids in order. A UNIQUE conflict throws SqliteError. */
  insert(executionId: string, rows: CalendarPriceInsert[]): number[] {
    for (const r of rows) validateCalendarPrice(r, this.opts.allowSynthetic, true);
    return this.db.transaction(() => rows.map((r) => insertCalendarPrice(this.db, executionId, r)));
  }

  /** Prices this execution fetched itself (reused copies excluded): the calendar-basis alert candidates (spec §6.8). */
  fetchedBy(executionId: string): CalendarPriceRow[] {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string], CalendarPriceDbRow>('SELECT * FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = ? AND reused_from_execution_id IS NULL ORDER BY id').all(executionId).map(toCalendarPriceRow);
  }
}
```

### Task 3.11: `writeTile` and `writeTileError`: one tile, atomically, merged by core

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/repositories/cells.ts`
- Create: `test/cells.writeTile.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `mergeWithinProvider` (WP1).
- Produces: `CellsRepo.writeTile`, `CellsRepo.writeTileError`.

Rules:
- A tile's outcomes are `priced | empty | no_data` (a failed tile goes through `writeTileError`); every covered cell has exactly one outcome and every outcome is a covered cell (spec §5.2).
- Each outcome carries `fetchedAt`, `cabin`, `pointOfSale` and `sourceKind` (rule `outcome-provenance`, ruling 5). A `priced` outcome has exactly one price for its cell; its `minPence`, `fetchedAt`, `cabin`, `pointOfSale` and `sourceKind` equal that price's (rules `min-pence`, `outcome-price-mismatch`).
- `discards` is validated and not stored.
- Merge (ruling 3): over `not_run`/`skipped`, or over a **reused copy**, the fresh outcome replaces the row outright; for every outcome cell the reused price row is deleted first, so a fresh `empty` or `no_data` also removes it. Only fresh-on-fresh goes through core's `mergeWithinProvider(existing, incoming)` (priced > empty > no_data > error, min of priced).
- A tile error stores `error` with the kind as `reason` (closed list, prov-009) and the URL-stripped message as `last_error`, counts an attempt, and merges with core like any fresh write (an error never displaces a stronger result, reused or fresh).
- A committed tile resets `auto_resume_count` (spec §7).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```ts
import { type CellKey, type ProviderId, cellKeyString, fixedClock, rollUp } from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { CalendarPricesRepo } from '../src/repositories/calendarPrices.js';
import { CellsRepo } from '../src/repositories/cells.js';
import { ExecutionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/executions.js';
import type { TileWrite } from '../src/types.js';
import { T0, at, count, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, calendarPrice, cell, newExecution, withNights } from './helpers/factories.js';
import { rawOutcome } from './helpers/outcomes.js';

const A = cell();
const B = cell(undefined, EWR);
const C = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-15', '2027-03-29');
type TileOutcome = TileWrite['outcomes'][number];
const out = (c: CellKey, status: TileOutcome['status'], minPence: number | null = null, over: Partial<TileOutcome> = {}): TileOutcome => ({
  ...c, status, reason: null, lastError: null, fetchedAt: T0, minPence, cabin: 'economy', pointOfSale: 'unknown', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached', ...over,
});
const tile = (cells: CellKey[], outcomes: TileOutcome[], prices: TileWrite['prices'] = []): TileWrite => ({ tileIndex: 0, cells, prices, outcomes, discards: 0 });
const opts = { allowSynthetic: false };

function setup(providers: ProviderId[] = ['travelpayouts']) {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
  const cells = new CellsRepo(db, clock, opts);
  cells.seed(execution.id, [A, B, C].map(withNights), providers);
  return { db, clock, definition, executionId: execution.id, cells, executions: new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock) };
}
const stored = (cells: CellsRepo, executionId: string, c: CellKey) => cells.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(c))?.discovery;
const core = (cells: CellsRepo, executionId: string, c: CellKey) => rollUp(cells.outcomes(executionId, { stage: 'discovery', cells: [c] }));

describe('CellsRepo.writeTile (spec §7 atomic unit "each tile\'s prices, outcomes and roll-ups")', () => {
  it('writes prices, outcomes and roll-ups together and resets auto_resume_count', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells, executions } = setup();
    executions.incrementAutoResume(executionId);
    cells.writeTile(executionId, 'travelpayouts', tile([A, B, C], [out(A, 'priced', 39_900), out(B, 'empty'), out(C, 'no_data')], [calendarPrice(A, { pricePence: 39_900, priceNativeMinor: 39_900 })]));
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(1);
    expect(stored(cells, executionId, A)).toEqual({ status: 'priced', reason: null, minPence: 39_900 });
    expect([stored(cells, executionId, B)?.status, stored(cells, executionId, C)?.status]).toEqual(['empty', 'no_data']);
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { cells: [A] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'priced', minPence: 39_900, fetchedAt: T0, cabin: 'economy', pointOfSale: 'unknown', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached', attempts: 0, reusedFromExecutionId: null });
    expect(executions.get(executionId)?.autoResumeCount).toBe(0);
  });

  it('writeTileError marks exactly its cells error, attempts + 1 per pass; reason is the kind, the message goes to last_error URL-free', () => {
    const { executionId, cells } = setup();
    const msg = 'HTTP 503 from https://api.travelpayouts.com/aviasales/v3/prices_for_dates?token=SECRET';
    cells.writeTileError(executionId, 'travelpayouts', [A, B], 'upstream', msg);
    cells.writeTileError(executionId, 'travelpayouts', [A], 'upstream', msg);
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { cells: [A] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'error', reason: 'upstream', attempts: 2, lastError: 'HTTP 503 from https://api.travelpayouts.com/aviasales/v3/prices_for_dates' });
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { cells: [B] })[0]).toMatchObject({ attempts: 1 });
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { cells: [C] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'not_run', attempts: 0 });
    expect(stored(cells, executionId, A)).toEqual(core(cells, executionId, A));
    expect(() => cells.writeTileError(executionId, 'travelpayouts', [C], msg, null)).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'error-reason' }));
  });

  it('roll-ups are atomic: a SQL failure inside the write leaves no partial rows', () => {
    const { db, clock, executionId, cells, executions } = setup();
    executions.incrementAutoResume(executionId);
    new CalendarPricesRepo(db, clock, opts).insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(B)]); // a fresh price for B already exists
    let thrown: unknown;
    try {

    } catch (e) {
      thrown = e;
    }
    expect(thrown).toBeInstanceOf(Database.SqliteError);
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(1);
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId).map((o) => o.status)).toEqual(['not_run', 'not_run', 'not_run']);
    expect(stored(cells, executionId, A)?.status).toBe('not_run');
    expect(executions.get(executionId)?.autoResumeCount).toBe(1);
  });

  it('a rejection after an outcome and roll-up were written rolls those back too (test-011)', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells } = setup();

    expect(() => cells.writeTile(executionId, 'travelpayouts', tile([A, stranger], [out(A, 'priced', 41_200), out(stranger, 'priced', 41_200)], [calendarPrice(A), calendarPrice(stranger)]))).toThrow(

    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'empty')], [calendarPrice(A)]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'price-needs-priced' }));
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'priced', 1)], [calendarPrice(A)]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'min-pence' }));
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'empty', null, { reason: 'HTTP 500 https://api.travelpayouts.com/x?token=SECRET' })]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'outcome-reason' }));
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'no_data', null, { reason: 'partial-expansion' })]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'outcome-reason' }));
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'empty', null, { cabin: null })]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'outcome-provenance' }));
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'no_data', null, { fetchedAt: null })]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'outcome-provenance' }));
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'priced', 41_200)], [calendarPrice(A), calendarPrice(A, { pricePence: 1, priceNativeMinor: 1 })]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'in-tile-conflict' }));
    expect(w(tile([A, B], [out(A, 'empty')]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'tile-partition' }));
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'empty'), out(B, 'empty')]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'tile-partition' }));

  // test-r2-001: every field the outcome must share with its price, one row each.
  it.each([
    ['fetchedAt', { fetchedAt: at('2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z') }],

    ['sourceKind', { sourceKind: 'scraped-aggregate' }],
  ] as const)('refuses an outcome whose %s differs from its price', (_field, over) => {
    const { db, executionId, cells } = setup();
    expect(() => cells.writeTile(executionId, 'travelpayouts', tile([A], [out(A, 'priced', 41_200, over)], [calendarPrice(A)]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'outcome-price-mismatch' }));
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(0);
  });

  it('fresh-on-fresh merges through core: an error never displaces priced; empty beats no_data; stored roll-ups equal core', () => {
    const { executionId, cells } = setup();
    cells.writeTile(executionId, 'travelpayouts', tile([A], [out(A, 'priced', 40_000)], [calendarPrice(A, { pricePence: 40_000, priceNativeMinor: 40_000 })]));
    cells.writeTileError(executionId, 'travelpayouts', [A, B], 'network', null);

  it('a fresh price replaces a reused copy outright, even when the reused price was cheaper (ruling 3)', () => {

  it('a tile error over a reused priced copy keeps the copy and its price and only counts the attempt (prov-r2-003, test-r2-002)', () => {
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Add to `packages/db/src/repositories/cells.ts` (imports: `mergeWithinProvider`, `type CellOutcome` from `@gmt/core`; `insertCalendarPrice`, `validateCalendarPrice` from `../internal/priceRow.js`; `CalendarPriceInsert`, `TileWrite` from `../types.js`):

```ts
type Incoming = TileWrite['outcomes'][number];
const SELECT_OUTCOME_SQL = `SELECT * FROM cell_outcomes WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`;
const UPDATE_OUTCOME_SQL = `
UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = @status, reason = @reason, last_error = @lastError, fetched_at = @fetchedAt, min_pence = @minPence,
  cabin = @cabin, point_of_sale = @pointOfSale, source_kind = @sourceKind, attempts = @attempts, reused_from_execution_id = @reusedFromExecutionId
WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`;
const DELETE_REUSED_PRICE_SQL = `DELETE FROM calendar_prices WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND provider_id = @providerId AND reused_from_execution_id IS NOT NULL`;
const TILE_STATUSES = ['priced', 'empty', 'no_data'] as const;

/**
 * The outcome to store when `incoming` (fresh) meets `existing` (ruling 3, spec §6.6). Over not_run, skipped or a reused
 * copy the fresh outcome replaces the row (an error never does: it only counts an attempt); fresh on fresh goes through
 * core's mergeWithinProvider. Attempts are the existing count plus one when this pass failed.
 */
function nextOutcome(existing: CellOutcome, incoming: CellOutcome, failedPass: boolean): CellOutcome {
  const attempts = existing.attempts + (failedPass ? 1 : 0);
  if (existing.status === 'not_run' || existing.status === 'skipped') return { ...incoming, attempts };
  // why: an error never displaces a stronger result, reused or fresh (plan merge rule, prov-r2-003): the existing outcome
  // stays, with its reused price row, and only the attempt is counted. Decided here so it does not hang on core's reading.
  if (incoming.status === 'error' && existing.status !== 'error') return { ...existing, attempts };
  if (existing.reusedFromExecutionId !== null) return { ...incoming, attempts };
  const merged = mergeWithinProvider(existing, incoming);
  // why: when the merge keeps an error, the newest vendor message is the useful one; status, reason and minPence stay core's.
  return { ...merged, attempts, lastError: merged.status === 'error' && incoming.status === 'error' ? incoming.lastError : merged.lastError };
}

function storeOutcome(db: Db, o: CellOutcome): void {
  db.raw.prepare(UPDATE_OUTCOME_SQL).run({ ...keyParams(o.executionId, o), stage: o.stage, providerId: o.providerId, status: o.status, reason: o.reason, lastError: o.lastError === null ? null : stripUrlsInText(o.lastError), fetchedAt: o.fetchedAt, minPence: o.minPence, cabin: o.cabin, pointOfSale: o.pointOfSale, sourceKind: o.sourceKind, attempts: o.attempts, reusedFromExecutionId: o.reusedFromExecutionId });
}

/**
 * Spec §3: `reason` never holds a vendor message (prov-r2-004). A fetched outcome's reason is closed per status:
 * priced → null | 'partial-expansion', empty → null, no_data → null | 'cabin-not-offered'.
 */
function assertOutcomeReason(o: { status: CellOutcomeStatus; reason: string | null } & CellKey): void {
  const allowed: Partial<Record<CellOutcomeStatus, ReadonlyArray<string | null>>> = { priced: [null, 'partial-expansion'], empty: [null], no_data: [null, 'cabin-not-offered'] };
  if (!(allowed[o.status] ?? []).includes(o.reason)) throw new RowRejected('outcome-reason', `${cellKeyString(o)} ${o.status} cannot carry reason ${String(o.reason)}`);
}

/** Ruling 5: a fetched terminal outcome carries the provenance reuse matches on. */
function assertOutcomeProvenance(o: Incoming): void {
  if (o.fetchedAt === null || o.cabin === null || o.pointOfSale === null || o.sourceKind === null) throw new RowRejected('outcome-provenance', `${cellKeyString(o)} ${o.status} needs fetchedAt, cabin, pointOfSale and sourceKind`);
}
```

```ts
  /**
   * Spec §7 atomic unit: one tile's prices, outcomes and roll-ups. Validation first (a malformed tile writes nothing);
   * any SQL failure inside rolls the whole tile back.
   */
  writeTile(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, w: TileWrite): void {
    assertInt('tileIndex', w.tileIndex);
    assertInt('discards', w.discards);
    const prices = new Map<string, CalendarPriceInsert>();
    for (const p of w.prices) {
      if (p.providerId !== providerId) throw new RowRejected('provider', `price from ${p.providerId} in a ${providerId} tile`);
      const key = cellKeyString(p.cell);
      if (prices.has(key)) throw new RowRejected('in-tile-conflict', `two prices for ${key} in one tile; the engine keeps one per cell (spec §5.6)`);
      validateCalendarPrice(p, this.opts.allowSynthetic, true);
      prices.set(key, p);
    }
    const covered = new Set(w.cells.map(cellKeyString));
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    for (const o of w.outcomes) {
      const key = cellKeyString(o);
      if (!TILE_STATUSES.some((s) => s === o.status)) throw new RowRejected('tile-status', `a tile outcome is priced, empty or no_data; got ${o.status} (errors go through writeTileError)`);
      if (!covered.has(key) || seen.has(key)) throw new RowRejected('tile-partition', `${key} is outside the tile or repeated`);
      seen.add(key);
      assertOutcomeProvenance(o);
      assertOutcomeReason(o);
      const price = prices.get(key);
      if (o.status === 'priced' && price === undefined) throw new RowRejected('priced-needs-price', `${key} is priced but the tile wrote no price for it (spec §3)`);
      if (o.status !== 'priced' && price !== undefined) throw new RowRejected('price-needs-priced', `${key} has a price but its outcome is ${o.status}`);
      if (price !== undefined) {
        if (o.minPence !== price.pricePence) throw new RowRejected('min-pence', `${key} min_pence ${String(o.minPence)} differs from its price ${price.pricePence}`);
        if (o.fetchedAt !== price.fetchedAt || o.cabin !== price.cabin || o.pointOfSale !== price.pointOfSale || o.sourceKind !== price.sourceKind) {
          throw new RowRejected('outcome-price-mismatch', `${key} outcome provenance differs from its price row (ruling 5)`);
        }
      }
    }
    if (seen.size !== covered.size) throw new RowRejected('tile-partition', 'every covered cell needs exactly one outcome');
    for (const key of prices.keys()) if (!seen.has(key)) throw new RowRejected('price-needs-priced', `${key} has a price but no outcome`);
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const o of w.outcomes) this.db.raw.prepare(DELETE_REUSED_PRICE_SQL).run({ ...keyParams(executionId, o), providerId });
      for (const p of w.prices) insertCalendarPrice(this.db, executionId, p);
      for (const o of w.outcomes) {
        const existing = this.current(executionId, o, 'discovery', providerId);
        const incoming: CellOutcome = { ...existing, ...o, attempts: existing.attempts, reusedFromExecutionId: null };
        storeOutcome(this.db, nextOutcome(existing, incoming, false));
        // why: per cell, so a later failure in the same tile also rolls back roll-ups already stored (test-011 pins it).
        storeRollUps(this.db, executionId, 'discovery', [o]);
      }
      this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_executions SET auto_resume_count = 0 WHERE id = ?').run(executionId);
    });
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 "tile-error marks exactly its cells": error with the kind as reason, attempts + 1, merged through core. */
  writeTileError(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, cells: CellKey[], reason: string, lastError: string | null): void {
    assertErrorReason(reason);
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const c of cells) {
        const existing = this.current(executionId, c, 'discovery', providerId);
        const incoming: CellOutcome = { ...existing, status: 'error', reason, lastError, fetchedAt: null, minPence: null, reusedFromExecutionId: null };
        storeOutcome(this.db, nextOutcome(existing, incoming, true));
      }
      storeRollUps(this.db, executionId, 'discovery', cells);
    });
  }

  /** The current outcome row inside the caller's transaction; a missing seeded row is a rejection. */
  private current(executionId: string, c: CellKey, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId): CellOutcome {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(SELECT_OUTCOME_SQL).get({ ...keyParams(executionId, c), stage, providerId });
    if (r === undefined) throw new RowRejected('missing', `no seeded ${stage} outcome for ${providerId} at ${cellKeyString(c)}`);
    return toOutcome(r);
  }
```

`// why:` `incoming` is built from `existing` so the identity fields (execution, cell, stage, provider) are the row's own, which `mergeWithinProvider` requires; the tile's fields then overwrite the data fields. In `writeTileError` the incoming error keeps the row's cabin/point-of-sale/kind columns, which a non-fetched error does not need but the merge carries harmlessly.

Add to `packages/db/src/repositories/cells.ts` (imports: `SelectionReason` from `@gmt/core`; `SELECTION_REASONS` from `../internal/enums.js`; `type CellKeyDbRow` from `../internal/outcomeRow.js`):

```ts
/** Spec §7 "Resume": reasons never retried automatically; a manual resume re-admits auth, quota, parse. */
const NON_RETRYABLE = ['auth', 'quota', 'parse', 'no-return-legs', 'fx-unavailable', 'currency-unsupported', 'unknown-airport-tz'] as const;
const MANUAL_RETRYABLE = ['auth', 'quota', 'parse'] as const;
const sqlList = (xs: readonly string[]) => xs.map((r) => `'${r}'`).join(', ');

/**
 * Spec §7 resume selection for one (stage, provider). `skipped:budget` is returned; the engine keeps it only when
 * headroom exists (WP7). `priced | empty | no_data` are never returned.
 */
export const RESUMABLE_SQL = `
SELECT origin, destination, departure_date, return_date FROM cell_outcomes
WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId AND (
  status = 'not_run'
  OR (status = 'error' AND attempts < 3 AND (reason IS NULL OR reason NOT IN (${sqlList(NON_RETRYABLE)})))
  OR (status = 'skipped' AND reason = 'budget')
  OR (@manual = 1 AND ((status = 'skipped' AND reason = 'provider_disabled') OR (status = 'error' AND reason IN (${sqlList(MANUAL_RETRYABLE)}))))
)
ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date`;
```

```ts
  /**
   * Spec §7 atomic unit "selection persistence + verification seeding": marks the selection, seeds one verification
   * outcome per cell of `allCells` × provider (selected → not_run, others → skipped:not_selected) and stores the
   * roll-ups. Selection runs once per execution (spec §6.7).
   */
  seedVerification(executionId: string, selected: Array<{ cell: CellKey; reason: SelectionReason }>, allCells: CellKey[], verificationProviders: ProviderId[]): void {
    const all = new Set(allCells.map(cellKeyString));
    for (const s of selected) if (!all.has(cellKeyString(s.cell))) throw new RowRejected('selection-member', `${cellKeyString(s.cell)} is not among allCells`);
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      if (this.hasVerificationOutcomes(executionId)) throw new RowRejected('selection-once', `verification outcomes already exist for ${executionId}; selection runs once per execution`);
      const inExecution = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, { n: number }>(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM search_cells WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND ${CELL_KEY_SQL} IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@keys))`).get({ executionId, keys: JSON.stringify([...all]) })?.n ?? 0;
      if (inExecution !== all.size) throw new RowRejected('selection-member', `allCells names ${all.size - inExecution} cell(s) that are not cells of ${executionId}`);
      this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_cells SET verification_selected = 0, selection_reason = NULL WHERE execution_id = ?').run(executionId);
      const mark = this.db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE search_cells SET verification_selected = 1, selection_reason = @reason WHERE ${CELL_WHERE}`);
      for (const s of selected) mark.run({ ...keyParams(executionId, s.cell), reason: s.reason });
      const seed = this.db.raw.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO cell_outcomes (execution_id, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, stage, provider_id, status, reason, attempts)
         SELECT execution_id, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, 'verification', @providerId,
                CASE WHEN verification_selected = 1 THEN 'not_run' ELSE 'skipped' END, CASE WHEN verification_selected = 1 THEN NULL ELSE 'not_selected' END, 0
         FROM search_cells WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND ${CELL_KEY_SQL} IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@keys))`,
      );
      for (const providerId of verificationProviders) seed.run({ executionId, providerId, keys: JSON.stringify([...all]) });
      storeRollUps(this.db, executionId, 'verification', allCells);
    });
  }

  selected(executionId: string): Array<{ cell: CellKey; reason: SelectionReason }> {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], CellKeyDbRow & { selection_reason: string }>('SELECT origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, selection_reason FROM search_cells WHERE execution_id = ? AND verification_selected = 1 ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date')
      .all(executionId)
      .map((r) => ({ cell: toCellKey(r), reason: oneOf('search_cells.selection_reason', SELECTION_REASONS, r.selection_reason) }));
  }

  /** Spec §7: resume re-runs selection only when no verification outcomes exist. */
  hasVerificationOutcomes(executionId: string): boolean {
    return this.db.raw.prepare(`SELECT 1 AS present FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = ? AND stage = 'verification' LIMIT 1`).get(executionId) !== undefined;
  }

  resumable(executionId: string, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId, manual: boolean): CellKey[] {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CellKeyDbRow>(RESUMABLE_SQL).all({ executionId, stage, providerId, manual: manual ? 1 : 0 }).map(toCellKey);
  }

  /** Spec §8.1: not_run and skipped outcomes of cells departing on or before `today` become skipped:departed; priced/empty/no_data untouched. */
  markDeparted(executionId: string, today: IsoDate): number {
    return this.db.transaction(() => {
      const changes = this.db.raw
        .prepare(`UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = 'skipped', reason = 'departed' WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND departure_date <= @today AND (status = 'not_run' OR (status = 'skipped' AND reason <> 'departed'))`)
        .run({ executionId, today }).changes;
      if (changes > 0) {
        storeRollUps(this.db, executionId, 'discovery');
        storeRollUps(this.db, executionId, 'verification');
      }
      return changes;
    });
  }
```

### Task 3.13: Itineraries, offers, observations and `writeVerifiedCell`

**Files:**
- Create: `src/schema/itineraries.ts`, `src/schema/segments.ts`, `src/schema/fareOffers.ts`, `src/schema/priceObservations.ts`, `src/repositories/itineraries.ts`, `test/itineraries.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/schema/searchCells.ts` (`best_observation_id` references `price_observations.id`), `src/schema/index.ts`, `src/repositories/cells.ts` (add `writeVerifiedCell`), `test/helpers/factories.ts` (add `itinerary`, `observationWrite`); regenerate the migration.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `ItinerariesRepo(db, clock, opts)` (every method in the contract; `latestVerificationOutcomeForCell(definitionId, cell, cabin)`, ruling 9), `CellsRepo.writeVerifiedCell`.

Rules: `itineraries.layovers_json` (spec §7) lets `get` return a complete `NormalisedItinerary`. `best_observation_id` is the cheapest observation written for the cell in this execution (ruling 13; verdicts are read-time, spec §6.2). `writeVerifiedCell` applies the same merge as `writeTile` (ruling 3): an error or empty never downgrades a priced cell, and a second priced write keeps the lower minimum, which is the observation `best_observation_id` points to. A priced or empty outcome carries its provenance (ruling 5); every observation's provider, cabin, point of sale and kind equal the outcome's, and the outcome's `fetchedAt` is the oldest observation's (spec §2: a chain's `fetchedAt` is its oldest call).

```ts
import type { NormalisedItinerary } from '@gmt/core';
import type { VerifiedCellWrite } from '../../src/types.js';

/** A nonstop round trip on the worked example; `hash` stands in for WP2's sha256 hex. */
export function itinerary(hash = 'a'.repeat(64), c: CellKey = cell(), flight = 117): NormalisedItinerary {
  return {
    hash, origin: c.origin, destination: c.destination, departureDate: c.departureDate, returnDate: c.returnDate, nights: withNights(c).nights,
    outbound: [{ marketingCarrier: 'BA', flightNumber: flight, from: c.origin, to: c.destination, departLocal: `${c.departureDate}T08:25`, arriveLocal: `${c.departureDate}T11:20`, durationMinutes: 475 }],
    inbound: [{ marketingCarrier: 'BA', flightNumber: flight - 5, from: c.destination, to: c.origin, departLocal: `${c.returnDate}T18:30`, arriveLocal: `${c.returnDate}T06:35`, durationMinutes: 425 }],
    outboundMinutes: 475, inboundMinutes: 425, stopsOut: 0, stopsIn: 0, layovers: [], airportChange: false, marketingCarriers: ['BA'],
  };
}

    pricePence: 41_200, priceNativeMinor: 41_200, currencyNative: 'GBP', fx: { converted: false }, taxesIncluded: true, selfTransfer: false,

    suppliedFields: ['segments', 'segmentTimes', 'segmentCabins', 'price', 'seller', 'baggage'], partialExpansion: false,
```

```ts
import { type CellKey, type Provenance, cellKeyString, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Db } from '../src/client.js';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { CellsRepo } from '../src/repositories/cells.js';
import { ItinerariesRepo } from '../src/repositories/itineraries.js';
import type { VerifiedCellWrite } from '../src/types.js';
import { T0, at, count, freshDb, sqliteCode } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, cell, itinerary, newExecution, observationWrite, snapshotOf, withNights } from './helpers/factories.js';

const offerOf = (hash = 'a'.repeat(64), seller = 'British Airways') => ({ itineraryHash: hash, providerId: 'searchapi', seller, cabin: 'economy' }) as const;
const SEARCHAPI_PROVENANCE: Provenance = { providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-search', fetchedAt: T0, maxAgeHours: 0, fx: { converted: false }, pointOfSale: 'GB', cabin: 'economy' };

function setup(allowSynthetic = false) {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
  return { db, clock, definition, executionId: execution.id, repo: new ItinerariesRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic }), cells: new CellsRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic }) };
}
const insertObs = (repo: ItinerariesRepo, executionId: string, offerId: number, over: Parameters<typeof observationWrite>[0] = {}) => repo.insertObservation({ ...observationWrite(over), offerId, executionId });

/** Raw insert into price_observations, to prove the shared price CHECKs hold on this table too (test-017). */
function rawObservation(db: Db, executionId: string, offerId: number, over: Record<string, unknown>): void {
  const row = {
    offer_id: offerId, execution_id: executionId, source_kind: 'scraped-search', cabin: 'economy', fetched_at: T0, price_pence: 41_200, price_native_minor: 41_200, currency_native: 'GBP',
    fx_rate_nano: null, fx_rate_date: null, fx_source: null, fx_converted: 0, point_of_sale: 'GB', supplied_fields_json: '[]', partial_expansion: 0, ...over,
  };
  const cols = Object.keys(row);
  db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO price_observations (${cols.join(', ')}) VALUES (${cols.map((c) => `@${c}`).join(', ')})`).run(row);
}

describe('itineraries and offers (spec §7)', () => {
  it('itineraries and segments are insert-or-ignore on hash; get rebuilds the whole itinerary including layovers', () => {
    const { db, repo } = setup();
    const it2 = { ...itinerary(), layovers: [{ at: EWR, minutes: 95, overnight: false, airportChange: false }] };
    repo.upsertItinerary(it2);
    repo.upsertItinerary({ ...it2, nights: 99 });
    expect([count(db, 'itineraries'), count(db, 'segments')]).toEqual([1, 2]);
    expect(repo.get('a'.repeat(64))).toEqual({ ...it2, firstSeenAt: T0 });
    expect(repo.get('missing')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('UNIQUE (itinerary_hash, provider_id, seller, cabin): upsertOffer returns the same id; a raw duplicate throws', () => {
    const { db, repo } = setup();
    repo.upsertItinerary(itinerary());
    const id = repo.upsertOffer(offerOf());
    expect(repo.upsertOffer(offerOf())).toBe(id);
    expect(repo.upsertOffer(offerOf(undefined, 'Mytrip'))).not.toBe(id);
    expect(repo.upsertOffer({ ...offerOf(), cabin: 'business' })).not.toBe(id);
    expect(sqliteCode(() => db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO fare_offers (itinerary_hash, provider_id, seller, cabin, first_seen_at, last_seen_at) VALUES (?, 'searchapi', 'British Airways', 'economy', ?, ?)`).run('a'.repeat(64), T0, T0))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE');
  });

  it('latest = max fetched_at, ties broken by id; first, lowest, highest follow; previousObservation looks strictly before', () => {
    const { db, executionId, repo } = setup();
    repo.upsertItinerary(itinerary());
    const offerId = repo.upsertOffer(offerOf());
    const obs = (fetchedAt: string, pence: number) => insertObs(repo, executionId, offerId, { pricePence: pence, priceNativeMinor: pence, provenance: { ...SEARCHAPI_PROVENANCE, fetchedAt: at(fetchedAt) } });
    obs('2026-10-14T10:00:00.000Z', 50_000);
    obs('2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z', 45_000);
    obs('2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z', 40_000); // inserted later, fetched earlier
    const read = () => db.raw.prepare('SELECT first_price_pence AS f, lowest_price_pence AS l, highest_price_pence AS h, latest_price_pence AS x, first_seen_at AS fs, last_seen_at AS ls FROM fare_offers WHERE id = ?').get(offerId);
    expect(read()).toEqual({ f: 50_000, l: 40_000, h: 50_000, x: 45_000, fs: '2026-10-14T10:00:00.000Z', ls: '2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z' });
    obs('2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z', 47_000); // same fetched_at as the latest: the higher id wins
    expect(read()).toMatchObject({ x: 47_000 });
    expect(repo.previousObservation(offerId, at('2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z'))?.pricePence).toBe(40_000);
    expect(repo.previousObservation(offerId, at('2026-10-14T10:00:00.000Z'))).toBeNull();
  });

  it('observations: provider ⇔ kind both directions, the gate, offer mismatch, disagreeing fx, non-integers', () => {
    const open = setup(true);
    open.repo.upsertItinerary(itinerary());
    const offerId = open.repo.upsertOffer(offerOf());
    const fixtureOffer = open.repo.upsertOffer({ ...offerOf(), providerId: 'fixture' });
    const closed = new ItinerariesRepo(open.db, open.clock, { allowSynthetic: false });
    const write = (repo: ItinerariesRepo, id: number, over: Parameters<typeof observationWrite>[0]) => () => insertObs(repo, open.executionId, id, over);
    expect(write(closed, offerId, { provenance: { ...SEARCHAPI_PROVENANCE, sourceKind: 'synthetic' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'provider-kind' }));
    for (const repo of [open.repo, closed]) {
      expect(write(repo, fixtureOffer, { provenance: { ...SEARCHAPI_PROVENANCE, providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'scraped-search' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'provider-kind' })); // test-010
    }
    expect(write(closed, fixtureOffer, { provenance: { ...SEARCHAPI_PROVENANCE, providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'synthetic' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'synthetic-gate' }));
    expect(write(closed, offerId, { provenance: { ...SEARCHAPI_PROVENANCE, cabin: 'business' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'offer-mismatch' }));
    expect(write(closed, offerId, { provenance: { ...SEARCHAPI_PROVENANCE, providerId: 'travelpayouts', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'offer-mismatch' }));
    expect(write(closed, offerId, { provenance: { ...SEARCHAPI_PROVENANCE, fx: { converted: true, rateNano: 1, rateDate: cell().departureDate, source: 'x' } } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'fx-mismatch' }));
    expect(write(closed, offerId, { pricePence: 412.5, priceNativeMinor: 412.5 })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'integer' }));
    expect(count(open.db, 'price_observations')).toBe(0);
  });

  it('price_observations carries the shared price CHECKs (test-017)', () => {
    const { db, executionId, repo } = setup();
    repo.upsertItinerary(itinerary());
    const offerId = repo.upsertOffer(offerOf());
    rawObservation(db, executionId, offerId, {}); // positive control: the baseline row satisfies every CHECK
    rawObservation(db, executionId, offerId, { price_pence: '412', price_native_minor: '412' }); // INTEGER affinity stores 412
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT typeof(price_pence) AS t, price_pence AS p FROM price_observations ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1').get()).toEqual({ t: 'integer', p: 412 });
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawObservation(db, executionId, offerId, { fx_converted: 1, currency_native: 'GBP', fx_rate_nano: 1, fx_rate_date: '2026-10-13', fx_source: 'x' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawObservation(db, executionId, offerId, { fx_converted: 0, currency_native: 'USD' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawObservation(db, executionId, offerId, { fx_converted: 1, currency_native: 'USD', fx_rate_nano: null, fx_rate_date: '2026-10-13', fx_source: 'x' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawObservation(db, executionId, offerId, { price_pence: 412.5, price_native_minor: 412.5 }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawObservation(db, executionId, offerId, { price_pence: 'abc', price_native_minor: 'abc' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawObservation(db, executionId, offerId, { fetched_at: '2026-10-14T12:00:00Z' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(count(db, 'price_observations')).toBe(2);
  });
});

describe('CellsRepo.writeVerifiedCell (spec §7 atomic unit "each verified cell")', () => {
  function seeded() {
    const s = setup();
    const all = [cell(), cell(undefined, EWR)];
    s.cells.seed(s.executionId, all.map(withNights), []);
    s.cells.seedVerification(s.executionId, [{ cell: cell(), reason: 'top_n' }], all, ['searchapi']);
    return s;
  }
  const write = (c: CellKey, offers: Array<{ hash: string; seller: string; pence: number }>, status: 'priced' | 'empty' | 'error' = 'priced'): VerifiedCellWrite => {
    const hashes = [...new Set(offers.map((o) => o.hash))];
    return {
      cell: c,
      outcome: { ...c, status, reason: status === 'error' ? 'upstream' : null, lastError: null, fetchedAt: status === 'error' ? null : T0, minPence: offers.length > 0 ? Math.min(...offers.map((o) => o.pence)) : null, cabin: 'economy', pointOfSale: 'GB', sourceKind: 'scraped-search' },
      itineraries: hashes.map((h) => ({ itinerary: itinerary(h, c), offers: offers.filter((o) => o.hash === h).map((o) => ({ offer: offerOf(h, o.seller), observation: observationWrite({ pricePence: o.pence, priceNativeMinor: o.pence }) })) })),
    };
  };
  const bestPrice = (repo: ItinerariesRepo, executionId: string, c: CellKey) => {
    const id = repo.bestObservationForCell(executionId, c);
    return repo.writtenBy(executionId).find((o) => o.id === id)?.pricePence ?? null;
  };

  it('writes itinerary, segments, offers, observations, outcome, best_observation_id and roll-up together', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells, repo } = seeded();
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', write(cell(), [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'British Airways', pence: 60_300 }, { hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'Mytrip', pence: 57_300 }]));
    expect([count(db, 'itineraries'), count(db, 'segments'), count(db, 'fare_offers'), count(db, 'price_observations')]).toEqual([1, 2, 2, 2]);
    expect(cells.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(cell()))?.verification).toEqual({ status: 'priced', reason: null, minPence: 57_300 });
    expect(bestPrice(repo, executionId, cell())).toBe(57_300);
    expect(repo.bestObservationForCell(executionId, cell(undefined, EWR))).toBeNull();
  });

  it('merges like writeTile: an error or empty never downgrades priced; a dearer second write keeps the cheaper minimum (ruling 3)', () => {
    const { executionId, cells, repo } = seeded();
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', write(cell(), [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'British Airways', pence: 50_000 }]));
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', write(cell(), [], 'error'));
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { stage: 'verification', cells: [cell()] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'priced', minPence: 50_000, attempts: 1 });
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', write(cell(), [], 'empty'));
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { stage: 'verification', cells: [cell()] })[0]?.status).toBe('priced');
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', write(cell(), [{ hash: 'b'.repeat(64), seller: 'Mytrip', pence: 60_000 }]));
    expect(cells.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(cell()))?.verification.minPence).toBe(50_000);
    expect(bestPrice(repo, executionId, cell())).toBe(50_000);
  });

  it('a failure on the second itinerary rolls back the first itinerary, its offer and observation', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells, repo } = seeded();
    const w = write(cell(), [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'British Airways', pence: 60_300 }, { hash: 'b'.repeat(64), seller: 'Mytrip', pence: 57_300 }]);
    const second = w.itineraries[1]?.offers[0];
    if (second === undefined) throw new Error('fixture has two itineraries');
    second.observation = { ...second.observation, pricePence: 573.5, priceNativeMinor: 573.5 };
    w.outcome = { ...w.outcome, minPence: 573.5 }; // passes the pre-check, so the integer rule fires inside the transaction after the first itinerary was written
    expect(() => cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', w)).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect([count(db, 'itineraries'), count(db, 'segments'), count(db, 'fare_offers'), count(db, 'price_observations')]).toEqual([0, 0, 0, 0]);
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { stage: 'verification', cells: [cell()] })[0]?.status).toBe('not_run');
    expect(repo.bestObservationForCell(executionId, cell())).toBeNull();
    expect(cells.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(cell()))?.verification.status).toBe('not_run');
  });

  it('a failure after the outcome was written (injected at best_observation_id) rolls back the outcome, rows and roll-up (test-r2-005)', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells, repo } = seeded();
    db.raw.exec(`CREATE TRIGGER fail_best BEFORE UPDATE OF best_observation_id ON search_cells WHEN NEW.best_observation_id IS NOT NULL BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'injected'); END`);
    expect(() => cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', write(cell(), [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'British Airways', pence: 60_300 }]))).toThrow(/injected/);
    expect([count(db, 'itineraries'), count(db, 'fare_offers'), count(db, 'price_observations')]).toEqual([0, 0, 0]);
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { stage: 'verification', cells: [cell()] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'not_run', minPence: null, attempts: 0 });
    expect(repo.bestObservationForCell(executionId, cell())).toBeNull();
    expect(cells.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(cell()))?.verification).toEqual({ status: 'not_run', reason: null, minPence: null });
  });

  // test-r2-001: every field the outcome must share with its observations, one row each.
  const mismatches: Array<[string, (v: VerifiedCellWrite) => VerifiedCellWrite]> = [
    ['cabin', (v) => ({ ...v, outcome: { ...v.outcome, cabin: 'business' } })],
    ['pointOfSale', (v) => ({ ...v, outcome: { ...v.outcome, pointOfSale: 'unknown' } })],
    ['sourceKind', (v) => ({ ...v, outcome: { ...v.outcome, sourceKind: 'scraped-aggregate' } })],
    ['provider', (v) => ({ ...v, itineraries: v.itineraries.map((i) => ({ ...i, offers: i.offers.map((o) => ({ ...o, observation: { ...o.observation, provenance: { ...o.observation.provenance, providerId: 'travelpayouts', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached' } } })) })) })],
  ];
  it.each(mismatches)('refuses an outcome whose %s differs from its observations', (_field, mutate) => {
    const { db, executionId, cells } = seeded();
    expect(() => cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', mutate(write(cell(), [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'x', pence: 100 }])))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'outcome-price-mismatch' }));
    expect(count(db, 'price_observations')).toBe(0);
  });

  it("the outcome fetchedAt is the oldest observation's: accepted with the oldest, refused with the newest (spec §2)", () => {
    const { executionId, cells } = seeded();
    const later = at('2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z');
    const base = write(cell(), [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'British Airways', pence: 60_300 }, { hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'Mytrip', pence: 57_300 }]);
    const mixed: VerifiedCellWrite = {
      ...base,
      itineraries: base.itineraries.map((i) => ({ ...i, offers: i.offers.map((o, k) => (k === 1 ? { ...o, observation: { ...o.observation, provenance: { ...o.observation.provenance, fetchedAt: later } } } : o)) })),
    };
    expect(() => cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', { ...mixed, outcome: { ...mixed.outcome, fetchedAt: later } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'outcome-price-mismatch' }));
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', mixed);
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { stage: 'verification', cells: [cell()] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'priced', fetchedAt: T0, minPence: 57_300 });
  });

  it('rejects priced without offers, offers on a non-priced outcome, a foreign cell, a wrong min_pence, missing provenance and mismatched observations', () => {
    const { executionId, cells } = seeded();
    const w = (v: VerifiedCellWrite) => () => cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', v);
    const one = write(cell(), [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'x', pence: 100 }]);
    expect(w(write(cell(), []))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'priced-needs-offer' }));
    expect(w({ ...one, outcome: { ...one.outcome, status: 'empty', minPence: null } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'offers-need-priced' }));
    expect(w({ ...one, cell: cell(undefined, EWR) })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'cell-mismatch' }));
    expect(w({ ...one, outcome: { ...one.outcome, minPence: 99 } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'min-pence' }));
    expect(w({ ...one, outcome: { ...one.outcome, sourceKind: null } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'outcome-provenance' }));
    expect(w({ ...one, outcome: { ...one.outcome, pointOfSale: 'unknown' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'outcome-price-mismatch' }));
    expect(w({ ...one, outcome: { ...one.outcome, fetchedAt: at('2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z') } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'outcome-price-mismatch' }));
    expect(w({ ...write(cell(), [], 'error'), outcome: { ...write(cell(), [], 'error').outcome, reason: 'socket hang up' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'error-reason' }));
    expect(w({ ...write(cell(), [], 'empty'), outcome: { ...write(cell(), [], 'empty').outcome, reason: 'no flights https://www.google.com/x?q=1' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'outcome-reason' }));
  });
});

describe('ItinerariesRepo reads', () => {
  it('observationsForDefinition, writtenBy, offerHistory, latestVerificationOutcomeForCell (cabin-filtered, ruling 9)', () => {
    const { db, clock, definition, executionId, cells, repo } = setup();
    const w = (c: CellKey, hash: string, pence: number): VerifiedCellWrite => ({
      cell: c, outcome: { ...c, status: 'priced', reason: null, lastError: null, fetchedAt: T0, minPence: pence, cabin: 'economy', pointOfSale: 'GB', sourceKind: 'scraped-search' },
      itineraries: [{ itinerary: itinerary(hash, c), offers: [{ offer: offerOf(hash), observation: observationWrite({ pricePence: pence, priceNativeMinor: pence }) }] }],
    });
    cells.seed(executionId, [withNights(cell())], []);
    cells.seedVerification(executionId, [{ cell: cell(), reason: 'top_n' }], [cell()], ['searchapi']);
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', w(cell(), 'a'.repeat(64), 41_200));
    const child = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell(undefined, EWR) }).execution;
    cells.seed(child.id, [withNights(cell(undefined, EWR))], []);
    cells.seedVerification(child.id, [{ cell: cell(undefined, EWR), reason: 'always_include' }], [cell(undefined, EWR)], ['searchapi']);
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(child.id, 'searchapi', w(cell(undefined, EWR), 'c'.repeat(64), 39_000));
    const space = snapshotOf(definition).resolved;
    const all = repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, { executionIds: 'all', space, cabin: 'economy', includeChildren: true });
    expect(all.map((r) => [r.executionId, r.pricePence, r.cell.destination, r.cell.nights, r.seller])).toEqual([[child.id, 39_000, 'EWR', 14, 'British Airways'], [executionId, 41_200, 'JFK', 14, 'British Airways']]);
    expect(all[0]?.provenance).toEqual(SEARCHAPI_PROVENANCE);
    expect(all[0]?.itinerary).toEqual(itinerary('c'.repeat(64), cell(undefined, EWR)));
    expect(all[0]?.offer).toMatchObject({ itineraryHash: 'c'.repeat(64), providerId: 'searchapi', seller: 'British Airways', cabin: 'economy', id: expect.any(Number) });
    expect(repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, { executionIds: 'all', space, cabin: 'economy', includeChildren: false })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, { executionIds: [executionId], space: null, cabin: 'economy', includeChildren: true })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, { executionIds: 'all', space, cabin: 'business', includeChildren: true })).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, { executionIds: 'all', space: { ...space, maxNights: 13 }, cabin: 'economy', includeChildren: true })).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(repo.writtenBy(child.id).map((o) => o.pricePence)).toEqual([39_000]);
    expect(repo.offerHistory('a'.repeat(64))).toEqual([{ providerId: 'searchapi', seller: 'British Airways', cabin: 'economy', points: [{ at: T0, pricePence: 41_200, provenance: SEARCHAPI_PROVENANCE }] }]);
    expect(repo.latestVerificationOutcomeForCell(definition.id, cell(), 'economy')).toMatchObject({ executionId, status: 'priced' });
    expect(repo.latestVerificationOutcomeForCell(definition.id, cell(), 'business')).toBeNull(); // prov-007: another cabin's search never counts
    expect(repo.latestVerificationOutcomeForCell(definition.id, cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-01-01', '2027-01-15'), 'economy')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { check, integer, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { isBool, isIsoTimestamp, isInteger, isNullOrInteger } from './checks.js';

/** Spec §7 `itineraries` (with `layovers_json`): cabin-independent flights, identity by hash (spec §6.4). Insert-or-ignore. */
export const itineraries = sqliteTable(
  'itineraries',
  {
    hash: text('hash').primaryKey(),
    kind: text('kind').notNull().default('flight'),
    origin: text('origin').notNull(),
    destination: text('destination').notNull(),
    departureDate: text('departure_date').notNull(),
    returnDate: text('return_date').notNull(),
    nights: integer('nights').notNull(),
    outboundMinutes: integer('outbound_minutes'),
    inboundMinutes: integer('inbound_minutes'),
    stopsOut: integer('stops_out').notNull(),
    stopsIn: integer('stops_in').notNull(),
    airportChange: integer('airport_change'),
    carriersJson: text('carriers_json').notNull(),
    layoversJson: text('layovers_json').notNull(),
    firstSeenAt: text('first_seen_at').notNull(),
  },
  () => [
    check('itineraries_nights_ck', isInteger('nights')),
    check('itineraries_stops_out_ck', isInteger('stops_out')),
    check('itineraries_stops_in_ck', isInteger('stops_in')),
    check('itineraries_out_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('outbound_minutes')),
    check('itineraries_in_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('inbound_minutes')),
    check('itineraries_airport_change_ck', isBool('airport_change', true)),
    check('itineraries_first_seen_ck', isIsoTimestamp('first_seen_at')),
  ],
);
```
