`packages/db/src/schema/segments.ts`:

```ts
import { check, integer, primaryKey, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { isIsoTimestamp, isInteger, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { itineraries } from './itineraries.js';

/** Spec §7 `segments`: no cabin (segments belong to the cabin-independent itinerary). */
export const segments = sqliteTable(
  'segments',
  {
    itineraryHash: text('itinerary_hash').notNull().references(() => itineraries.hash),
    direction: text('direction').notNull(),
    position: integer('position').notNull(),
    mode: text('mode').notNull().default('air'),
    marketingCarrier: text('marketing_carrier').notNull(),
    operatingCarrier: text('operating_carrier'),
    flightNumber: integer('flight_number').notNull(),
    fromIata: text('from_iata').notNull(),
    toIata: text('to_iata').notNull(),
    departLocal: text('depart_local').notNull(),
    arriveLocal: text('arrive_local').notNull(),
    departUtc: text('depart_utc'),
    arriveUtc: text('arrive_utc'),
    durationMinutes: integer('duration_minutes'),
    aircraft: text('aircraft'),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.itineraryHash, t.direction, t.position] }),
    check('segments_direction_ck', isOneOf('direction', ['outbound', 'inbound'])),
    check('segments_position_ck', isInteger('position')),
    check('segments_flight_number_ck', isInteger('flight_number')),
    check('segments_duration_ck', isNullOrInteger('duration_minutes')),
    check('segments_depart_utc_ck', isIsoTimestamp('depart_utc', true)),
    check('segments_arrive_utc_ck', isIsoTimestamp('arrive_utc', true)),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/schema/fareOffers.ts`:

```ts
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text, uniqueIndex } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { CABINS, PROVIDER_IDS } from '../internal/enums.js';
import { isIsoTimestamp, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { itineraries } from './itineraries.js';

/** Spec §7 `fare_offers`: (itinerary, provider, seller, cabin). Price aggregates are recomputed from observations. */
export const fareOffers = sqliteTable(
  'fare_offers',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    itineraryHash: text('itinerary_hash').notNull().references(() => itineraries.hash),
    providerId: text('provider_id').notNull(),
    seller: text('seller').notNull(),
    cabin: text('cabin').notNull(),
    firstSeenAt: text('first_seen_at').notNull(),
    lastSeenAt: text('last_seen_at').notNull(),
    firstPricePence: integer('first_price_pence'),
    lowestPricePence: integer('lowest_price_pence'),
    highestPricePence: integer('highest_price_pence'),
    latestPricePence: integer('latest_price_pence'),
  },
  (t) => [
    uniqueIndex('fare_offers_identity_uq').on(t.itineraryHash, t.providerId, t.seller, t.cabin),
    index('fare_offers_itinerary_idx').on(t.itineraryHash),
    check('fare_offers_provider_ck', isOneOf('provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS)),
    check('fare_offers_cabin_ck', isOneOf('cabin', CABINS)),
    check('fare_offers_first_seen_ck', isIsoTimestamp('first_seen_at')),
    check('fare_offers_last_seen_ck', isIsoTimestamp('last_seen_at')),
    check('fare_offers_first_ck', isNullOrInteger('first_price_pence')),
    check('fare_offers_lowest_ck', isNullOrInteger('lowest_price_pence')),
    check('fare_offers_highest_ck', isNullOrInteger('highest_price_pence')),
    check('fare_offers_latest_ck', isNullOrInteger('latest_price_pence')),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/schema/priceObservations.ts`:

```ts
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { isBool, isIsoTimestamp, priceTableChecks } from './checks.js';
import { fareOffers } from './fareOffers.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/**
 * Spec §7 `price_observations`: append-only; verdicts are recomputed at read time. Provider ⇔ kind is a repository
 * rule here (the provider lives on `fare_offers`, which a CHECK cannot read).
 */
export const priceObservations = sqliteTable(
  'price_observations',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    offerId: integer('offer_id').notNull().references(() => fareOffers.id),
    executionId: text('execution_id').notNull().references(() => searchExecutions.id),
    sourceKind: text('source_kind').notNull(),
    cabin: text('cabin').notNull(),
    fetchedAt: text('fetched_at').notNull(),
    providerObservedAt: text('provider_observed_at'),
    pricePence: integer('price_pence').notNull(),
    priceNativeMinor: integer('price_native_minor').notNull(),
    currencyNative: text('currency_native').notNull(),
    fxRateNano: integer('fx_rate_nano'),
    fxRateDate: text('fx_rate_date'),
    fxSource: text('fx_source'),
    fxConverted: integer('fx_converted').notNull(),
    pointOfSale: text('point_of_sale').notNull(),
    maxAgeHours: integer('max_age_hours'),
    taxesIncluded: integer('taxes_included'),
    selfTransfer: integer('self_transfer'),
    segmentCabinsJson: text('segment_cabins_json'),
    baggageJson: text('baggage_json'),
    fareConditionsJson: text('fare_conditions_json'),
    bookingJson: text('booking_json'),
    bookingExpiresAt: text('booking_expires_at'),
    suppliedFieldsJson: text('supplied_fields_json').notNull(),
    partialExpansion: integer('partial_expansion').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    index('price_observations_execution_price_idx').on(t.executionId, t.pricePence),
    index('price_observations_offer_fetched_idx').on(t.offerId, t.fetchedAt),
    ...priceTableChecks('price_observations'),
    check('price_observations_taxes_ck', isBool('taxes_included', true)),
    check('price_observations_self_transfer_ck', isBool('self_transfer', true)),
    check('price_observations_partial_ck', isBool('partial_expansion')),
    check('price_observations_expires_ck', isIsoTimestamp('booking_expires_at', true)),
  ],
);
```

In `src/schema/searchCells.ts` change the column to `bestObservationId: integer('best_observation_id').references(() => priceObservations.id),` with `import { priceObservations } from './priceObservations.js';`, and add the four tables to `schema/index.ts`.

`packages/db/src/repositories/itineraries.ts`:

```ts
import type { Cabin, CellKey, CellOutcome, Clock, FxInfo, HistoryPoint, IsoDateTime, NormalisedItinerary, Observation, Offer, ProviderId, Provenance, ResolvedSpace, Segment } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { CABINS, PROVIDER_IDS, SOURCE_KINDS } from '../internal/enums.js';
import { CELL_WHERE, type OutcomeDbRow, keyParams, toOutcome } from '../internal/outcomeRow.js';
import { fxOf } from '../internal/priceRow.js';
import { oneOf, readBaggage, readBooking, readCabins, readDate, readFareConditions, readIata, readJson, readLayovers, readNullableTime, readStringArray, readSuppliedFields, readTime } from '../internal/read.js';
import { RowRejected, assertInt, assertIsoDate, assertIsoDateTime, assertNullableInt, assertNullableIsoDateTime, assertPointOfSale, assertPriceMoney, assertProviderKind } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { ObservationRow, RepoOptions } from '../types.js';

interface ObservationDbRow {
  id: number; offer_id: number; execution_id: string; source_kind: string; cabin: string; fetched_at: string; provider_observed_at: string | null; price_pence: number;
  price_native_minor: number; currency_native: string; fx_rate_nano: number | null; fx_rate_date: string | null; fx_source: string | null; fx_converted: number; point_of_sale: string;
  max_age_hours: number | null; taxes_included: number | null; self_transfer: number | null; segment_cabins_json: string | null; baggage_json: string | null;
  fare_conditions_json: string | null; booking_json: string | null; booking_expires_at: string | null; supplied_fields_json: string; partial_expansion: number;
  itinerary_hash: string; provider_id: string; seller: string;
}
interface ItineraryDbRow {
  hash: string; origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string; nights: number; outbound_minutes: number | null; inbound_minutes: number | null;
  stops_out: number; stops_in: number; airport_change: number | null; carriers_json: string; layovers_json: string; first_seen_at: string;
}
interface SegmentDbRow {
  direction: string; marketing_carrier: string; operating_carrier: string | null; flight_number: number; from_iata: string; to_iata: string; depart_local: string; arrive_local: string;
  depart_utc: string | null; arrive_utc: string | null; duration_minutes: number | null; aircraft: string | null;
}

const O = 'price_observations';
/** Field-wise FxInfo equality (arch-r2-009): independent of key order. */
const sameFx = (a: FxInfo, b: FxInfo): boolean =>
  a.converted === b.converted && (!a.converted || !b.converted || (a.rateNano === b.rateNano && a.rateDate === b.rateDate && a.source === b.source));
const nullableBool = (v: number | null): boolean | null => (v === null ? null : v === 1);
const boolOrNull = (v: boolean | null) => (v === null ? null : v ? 1 : 0);

function toObservation(r: ObservationDbRow): Observation {
  const fx = fxOf(O, r);
  const providerObservedAt = readNullableTime(`${O}.provider_observed_at`, r.provider_observed_at);
  const provenance: Provenance = {
    providerId: oneOf('fare_offers.provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS, r.provider_id), sourceKind: oneOf(`${O}.source_kind`, SOURCE_KINDS, r.source_kind),
    fetchedAt: readTime(`${O}.fetched_at`, r.fetched_at), ...(providerObservedAt === null ? {} : { providerObservedAt }), maxAgeHours: r.max_age_hours ?? 'unknown', fx,
    pointOfSale: r.point_of_sale, cabin: oneOf(`${O}.cabin`, CABINS, r.cabin),
  };
  return {
    id: r.id, offerId: r.offer_id, executionId: r.execution_id, provenance, pricePence: r.price_pence, priceNativeMinor: r.price_native_minor, currencyNative: r.currency_native, fx,
    taxesIncluded: nullableBool(r.taxes_included), selfTransfer: nullableBool(r.self_transfer),
    segmentCabins: r.segment_cabins_json === null ? null : readCabins(`${O}.segment_cabins_json`, readJson(`${O}.segment_cabins_json`, r.segment_cabins_json)),
    baggage: r.baggage_json === null ? null : readBaggage(`${O}.baggage_json`, readJson(`${O}.baggage_json`, r.baggage_json)),
    fareConditions: r.fare_conditions_json === null ? null : readFareConditions(`${O}.fare_conditions_json`, readJson(`${O}.fare_conditions_json`, r.fare_conditions_json)),
    booking: r.booking_json === null ? { kind: 'none' } : readBooking(`${O}.booking_json`, r.booking_json), bookingExpiresAt: readNullableTime(`${O}.booking_expires_at`, r.booking_expires_at),
    suppliedFields: readSuppliedFields(`${O}.supplied_fields_json`, readJson(`${O}.supplied_fields_json`, r.supplied_fields_json)), partialExpansion: r.partial_expansion === 1,
  };
}

function toSegment(s: SegmentDbRow): Segment {
  const departUtc = readNullableTime('segments.depart_utc', s.depart_utc);
  const arriveUtc = readNullableTime('segments.arrive_utc', s.arrive_utc);
  return {
    marketingCarrier: s.marketing_carrier, ...(s.operating_carrier === null ? {} : { operatingCarrier: s.operating_carrier }), flightNumber: s.flight_number,
    from: readIata('segments.from_iata', s.from_iata), to: readIata('segments.to_iata', s.to_iata), departLocal: s.depart_local, arriveLocal: s.arrive_local,
    ...(departUtc === null ? {} : { departUtc }), ...(arriveUtc === null ? {} : { arriveUtc }),
    ...(s.duration_minutes === null ? {} : { durationMinutes: s.duration_minutes }), ...(s.aircraft === null ? {} : { aircraft: s.aircraft }),
  };
}

const OBSERVATION_JOIN = `FROM price_observations o JOIN fare_offers f ON f.id = o.offer_id JOIN itineraries i ON i.hash = f.itinerary_hash JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = o.execution_id`;
const OBSERVATION_COLUMNS = 'o.*, f.itinerary_hash, f.provider_id, f.seller';
const RECOMPUTE_OFFER_SQL = `
UPDATE fare_offers SET
  first_seen_at = (SELECT MIN(fetched_at) FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id),
  last_seen_at = (SELECT MAX(fetched_at) FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id),
  first_price_pence = (SELECT price_pence FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id ORDER BY fetched_at ASC, id ASC LIMIT 1),
  lowest_price_pence = (SELECT MIN(price_pence) FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id),
  highest_price_pence = (SELECT MAX(price_pence) FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id),
  latest_price_pence = (SELECT price_pence FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id ORDER BY fetched_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1)
WHERE id = @id`;

/** Spec §7 itineraries, segments, fare offers and price observations. */
export class ItinerariesRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
    private readonly opts: RepoOptions,
  ) {}

  /** Insert-or-ignore on hash, with its segments and layovers, in one transaction (spec §7). */
  upsertItinerary(it: NormalisedItinerary): void {
    if (it.hash.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('hash', 'itinerary hash is empty');
    assertInt('nights', it.nights);
    assertInt('stops_out', it.stopsOut);
    assertInt('stops_in', it.stopsIn);
    assertNullableInt('outbound_minutes', it.outboundMinutes ?? null);
    assertNullableInt('inbound_minutes', it.inboundMinutes ?? null);
    assertIsoDate('departure_date', it.departureDate);
    assertIsoDate('return_date', it.returnDate);
    const segs = [...it.outbound.map((s, position) => ({ s, direction: 'outbound', position })), ...it.inbound.map((s, position) => ({ s, direction: 'inbound', position }))];
    for (const { s } of segs) {
      assertInt('flight_number', s.flightNumber);
      assertNullableInt('duration_minutes', s.durationMinutes ?? null);
    }
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      this.db.raw
        .prepare(
          `INSERT INTO itineraries (hash, kind, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, nights, outbound_minutes, inbound_minutes, stops_out, stops_in, airport_change, carriers_json, layovers_json, first_seen_at)
           VALUES (@hash, 'flight', @origin, @destination, @departureDate, @returnDate, @nights, @outboundMinutes, @inboundMinutes, @stopsOut, @stopsIn, @airportChange, @carriers, @layovers, @now)
           ON CONFLICT(hash) DO NOTHING`,
        )
        .run({
          hash: it.hash, origin: it.origin, destination: it.destination, departureDate: it.departureDate, returnDate: it.returnDate, nights: it.nights,
          outboundMinutes: it.outboundMinutes ?? null, inboundMinutes: it.inboundMinutes ?? null, stopsOut: it.stopsOut, stopsIn: it.stopsIn,
          airportChange: it.airportChange === undefined ? null : it.airportChange ? 1 : 0, carriers: JSON.stringify(it.marketingCarriers), layovers: JSON.stringify(it.layovers), now: this.clock.now(),
        });
      const insertSegment = this.db.raw.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO segments (itinerary_hash, direction, position, mode, marketing_carrier, operating_carrier, flight_number, from_iata, to_iata, depart_local, arrive_local, depart_utc, arrive_utc, duration_minutes, aircraft)
         VALUES (@hash, @direction, @position, 'air', @marketingCarrier, @operatingCarrier, @flightNumber, @from, @to, @departLocal, @arriveLocal, @departUtc, @arriveUtc, @durationMinutes, @aircraft)
         ON CONFLICT(itinerary_hash, direction, position) DO NOTHING`,
      );
      for (const { s, direction, position } of segs) {
        insertSegment.run({
          hash: it.hash, direction, position, marketingCarrier: s.marketingCarrier, operatingCarrier: s.operatingCarrier ?? null, flightNumber: s.flightNumber, from: s.from, to: s.to,
          departLocal: s.departLocal, arriveLocal: s.arriveLocal, departUtc: s.departUtc ?? null, arriveUtc: s.arriveUtc ?? null, durationMinutes: s.durationMinutes ?? null, aircraft: s.aircraft ?? null,
        });
      }
    });
  }

  get(hash: string): (NormalisedItinerary & { firstSeenAt: IsoDateTime }) | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], ItineraryDbRow>('SELECT * FROM itineraries WHERE hash = ?').get(hash);
    if (r === undefined) return null;
    const segs = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], SegmentDbRow>('SELECT * FROM segments WHERE itinerary_hash = ? ORDER BY position').all(hash);
    const I = 'itineraries';
    return {
      hash: r.hash, origin: readIata(`${I}.origin`, r.origin), destination: readIata(`${I}.destination`, r.destination),
      departureDate: readDate(`${I}.departure_date`, r.departure_date), returnDate: readDate(`${I}.return_date`, r.return_date), nights: r.nights,
      outbound: segs.filter((s) => s.direction === 'outbound').map(toSegment), inbound: segs.filter((s) => s.direction === 'inbound').map(toSegment),
      ...(r.outbound_minutes === null ? {} : { outboundMinutes: r.outbound_minutes }), ...(r.inbound_minutes === null ? {} : { inboundMinutes: r.inbound_minutes }),
      stopsOut: r.stops_out, stopsIn: r.stops_in, layovers: readLayovers(`${I}.layovers_json`, readJson(`${I}.layovers_json`, r.layovers_json)),
      ...(r.airport_change === null ? {} : { airportChange: r.airport_change === 1 }),
      marketingCarriers: readStringArray(`${I}.carriers_json`, readJson(`${I}.carriers_json`, r.carriers_json)), firstSeenAt: readTime(`${I}.first_seen_at`, r.first_seen_at),
    };
  }

  /** Returns the offer id for (itinerary, provider, seller, cabin), creating it if absent. */
  upsertOffer(offer: Offer): number {
    if (offer.seller.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('seller', 'seller is required (spec §7 NOT NULL)');
    const now = this.clock.now();
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(`INSERT INTO fare_offers (itinerary_hash, provider_id, seller, cabin, first_seen_at, last_seen_at) VALUES (@itineraryHash, @providerId, @seller, @cabin, @now, @now) ON CONFLICT(itinerary_hash, provider_id, seller, cabin) DO NOTHING`)
      .run({ ...offer, now });
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, { id: number }>('SELECT id FROM fare_offers WHERE itinerary_hash = @itineraryHash AND provider_id = @providerId AND seller = @seller AND cabin = @cabin').get({ ...offer });
    if (r === undefined) throw new Error('fare offer vanished after upsert');
    return r.id;
  }

  /** Appends one observation and recomputes the offer's first/lowest/highest/latest (latest = max fetched_at, tie id). */
  insertObservation(obs: Omit<Observation, 'id'>): number {
    const offer = this.db.raw.prepare<[number], { provider_id: string; cabin: string }>('SELECT provider_id, cabin FROM fare_offers WHERE id = ?').get(obs.offerId);
    if (offer === undefined) throw new RowRejected('missing', `fare offer ${obs.offerId} does not exist`);
    const p = obs.provenance;
    assertProviderKind(p.providerId, p.sourceKind, this.opts.allowSynthetic);
    if (p.providerId !== offer.provider_id || p.cabin !== offer.cabin) throw new RowRejected('offer-mismatch', `observation ${p.providerId}/${p.cabin} does not match offer ${offer.provider_id}/${offer.cabin}`);
    if (!sameFx(p.fx, obs.fx)) throw new RowRejected('fx-mismatch', 'observation fx and provenance fx disagree');
    assertPriceMoney(obs);
    assertIsoDateTime('fetched_at', p.fetchedAt);
    if (p.providerObservedAt !== undefined) assertIsoDateTime('provider_observed_at', p.providerObservedAt);
    assertNullableIsoDateTime('booking_expires_at', obs.bookingExpiresAt);
    if (p.maxAgeHours !== 'unknown') assertInt('max_age_hours', p.maxAgeHours);
    assertPointOfSale(p.pointOfSale);
    const fx = obs.fx;
    return this.db.transaction(() => {
      const id = Number(
        this.db.raw
          .prepare(
            `INSERT INTO price_observations (offer_id, execution_id, source_kind, cabin, fetched_at, provider_observed_at, price_pence, price_native_minor, currency_native,
              fx_rate_nano, fx_rate_date, fx_source, fx_converted, point_of_sale, max_age_hours, taxes_included, self_transfer, segment_cabins_json, baggage_json,
              fare_conditions_json, booking_json, booking_expires_at, supplied_fields_json, partial_expansion)
             VALUES (@offerId, @executionId, @sourceKind, @cabin, @fetchedAt, @providerObservedAt, @pricePence, @priceNativeMinor, @currencyNative,
              @fxRateNano, @fxRateDate, @fxSource, @fxConverted, @pointOfSale, @maxAgeHours, @taxesIncluded, @selfTransfer, @segmentCabins, @baggage,
              @fareConditions, @booking, @bookingExpiresAt, @suppliedFields, @partialExpansion)`,
          )
          .run({
            offerId: obs.offerId, executionId: obs.executionId, sourceKind: p.sourceKind, cabin: p.cabin, fetchedAt: p.fetchedAt, providerObservedAt: p.providerObservedAt ?? null,
            pricePence: obs.pricePence, priceNativeMinor: obs.priceNativeMinor, currencyNative: obs.currencyNative,
            fxRateNano: fx.converted ? fx.rateNano : null, fxRateDate: fx.converted ? fx.rateDate : null, fxSource: fx.converted ? fx.source : null, fxConverted: fx.converted ? 1 : 0,
            pointOfSale: p.pointOfSale, maxAgeHours: p.maxAgeHours === 'unknown' ? null : p.maxAgeHours,
            taxesIncluded: boolOrNull(obs.taxesIncluded), selfTransfer: boolOrNull(obs.selfTransfer),
            segmentCabins: obs.segmentCabins === null ? null : JSON.stringify(obs.segmentCabins), baggage: obs.baggage === null ? null : JSON.stringify(obs.baggage),
            fareConditions: obs.fareConditions === null ? null : JSON.stringify(obs.fareConditions), booking: JSON.stringify(obs.booking),
            bookingExpiresAt: obs.bookingExpiresAt, suppliedFields: JSON.stringify(obs.suppliedFields), partialExpansion: obs.partialExpansion ? 1 : 0,
          }).lastInsertRowid,
      );
      this.db.raw.prepare(RECOMPUTE_OFFER_SQL).run({ id: obs.offerId });
      return id;
    });
  }

  /** Observations from this definition's executions (children when asked), within the current space (null = all) and cabin. Cheapest first. */
  observationsForDefinition(definitionId: string, opts: { executionIds: string[] | 'all'; space: ResolvedSpace | null; cabin: Cabin; includeChildren: boolean }): ObservationRow[] {
    const s = opts.space;
    return this.rows(
      `SELECT ${OBSERVATION_COLUMNS} ${OBSERVATION_JOIN}
       WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId AND (@children = 1 OR e.is_child = 0) AND (@ids IS NULL OR o.execution_id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@ids))) AND f.cabin = @cabin
         AND (@spaced = 0 OR (i.origin IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@origins)) AND i.destination IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@destinations))
              AND i.departure_date BETWEEN @from AND @to AND i.nights BETWEEN @minNights AND @maxNights))
       ORDER BY o.price_pence, o.id`,
      {
        definitionId, children: opts.includeChildren ? 1 : 0, ids: opts.executionIds === 'all' ? null : JSON.stringify(opts.executionIds), cabin: opts.cabin, spaced: s === null ? 0 : 1,
        origins: JSON.stringify(s?.origins ?? []), destinations: JSON.stringify(s?.destinations ?? []), from: s?.departureFrom ?? '', to: s?.departureTo ?? '', minNights: s?.minNights ?? 0, maxNights: s?.maxNights ?? 0,
      },
    );
  }

  /** Observations this execution wrote: the verified-basis alert candidates (spec §6.8). */
  writtenBy(executionId: string): ObservationRow[] {
    return this.rows(`SELECT ${OBSERVATION_COLUMNS} ${OBSERVATION_JOIN} WHERE o.execution_id = @executionId ORDER BY o.id`, { executionId });
  }

  /** The newest observation of the offer fetched strictly before `before` (spec §6.8 drop rules). */
  previousObservation(offerId: number, before: IsoDateTime): Observation | null {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, ObservationDbRow>(`SELECT ${OBSERVATION_COLUMNS} FROM price_observations o JOIN fare_offers f ON f.id = o.offer_id WHERE o.offer_id = @offerId AND o.fetched_at < @before ORDER BY o.fetched_at DESC, o.id DESC LIMIT 1`)
      .get({ offerId, before });
    return r === undefined ? null : toObservation(r);
  }

  /** Every observation of every offer of one itinerary, one series per offer, oldest first (History view). */
  offerHistory(hash: string): Array<{ providerId: ProviderId; seller: string; cabin: Cabin; points: HistoryPoint[] }> {
    const out = new Map<number, { providerId: ProviderId; seller: string; cabin: Cabin; points: HistoryPoint[] }>();
    for (const r of this.db.raw.prepare<[string], ObservationDbRow>(`SELECT ${OBSERVATION_COLUMNS} FROM price_observations o JOIN fare_offers f ON f.id = o.offer_id WHERE f.itinerary_hash = ? ORDER BY f.id, o.fetched_at, o.id`).all(hash)) {
      const o = toObservation(r);
      const series = out.get(r.offer_id) ?? { providerId: o.provenance.providerId, seller: r.seller, cabin: o.provenance.cabin, points: [] };
      series.points.push({ at: o.provenance.fetchedAt, pricePence: o.pricePence, provenance: o.provenance });
      out.set(r.offer_id, series);
    }
    return [...out.values()];
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 "not seen in latest search": the newest priced|empty verification outcome for the cell in the definition's cabin (ruling 9). */
  latestVerificationOutcomeForCell(definitionId: string, cell: CellKey, cabin: Cabin): CellOutcome | null {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(
        `SELECT o.* FROM cell_outcomes o JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = o.execution_id
         WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId AND o.stage = 'verification' AND o.status IN ('priced', 'empty') AND o.cabin = @cabin
           AND o.origin = @origin AND o.destination = @destination AND o.departure_date = @departureDate AND o.return_date = @returnDate
         ORDER BY o.fetched_at DESC, e.created_at DESC, e.rowid DESC LIMIT 1`,
      )
      .get({ definitionId, cabin, ...cell });
    return r === undefined ? null : toOutcome(r);
  }

  /** The cell's `best_observation_id` in this execution: the cheapest observation written (ruling 13). */
  bestObservationForCell(executionId: string, cell: CellKey): number | null {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, { id: number | null }>(`SELECT best_observation_id AS id FROM search_cells WHERE ${CELL_WHERE}`).get(keyParams(executionId, cell))?.id ?? null;
  }

  private rows(sql: string, params: Record<string, unknown>): ObservationRow[] {
    const cache = new Map<string, NormalisedItinerary>();
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, ObservationDbRow>(sql)
      .all(params)
      .map((r) => {
        let it = cache.get(r.itinerary_hash);
        if (it === undefined) {
          const full = this.get(r.itinerary_hash);
          if (full === null) throw new Error(`observation ${r.id} points at missing itinerary ${r.itinerary_hash}`);
          const { firstSeenAt: _firstSeenAt, ...itinerary } = full;
          it = itinerary;
          cache.set(r.itinerary_hash, it);
        }
        const o = toObservation(r);
        return {
          ...o, offer: { id: r.offer_id, itineraryHash: r.itinerary_hash, providerId: o.provenance.providerId, seller: r.seller, cabin: o.provenance.cabin }, itinerary: it, seller: r.seller,
          cell: { origin: it.origin, destination: it.destination, departureDate: it.departureDate, returnDate: it.returnDate, nights: it.nights },
        };
      });
  }
}
```

Add to `packages/db/src/repositories/cells.ts` (imports: `ItinerariesRepo` from `./itineraries.js`; `VerifiedCellWrite` from `../types.js`):

```ts
  /**
   * Spec §7 atomic unit: one verified cell's itineraries, segments, offers, observations, outcome, best_observation_id
   * and roll-up. Merged like writeTile (ruling 3); best_observation_id is the cheapest observation written (ruling 13).
   */
  writeVerifiedCell(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, w: VerifiedCellWrite): void {
    const key = cellKeyString(w.cell);
    const o = w.outcome;
    const offers = w.itineraries.flatMap((i) => i.offers.map((x) => ({ ...x, itinerary: i.itinerary })));
    if (cellKeyString(o) !== key) throw new RowRejected('cell-mismatch', `outcome is for ${cellKeyString(o)}, not ${key}`);
    if (o.status !== 'priced' && o.status !== 'empty' && o.status !== 'error') throw new RowRejected('verified-status', `a verified cell is priced, empty or error; got ${o.status}`);
    if (o.status === 'priced' && offers.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('priced-needs-offer', `${key} is priced with no offer (spec §5.4)`);
    if (o.status !== 'priced' && offers.length > 0) throw new RowRejected('offers-need-priced', `${key} has offers but status ${o.status}`);
    if (o.status === 'error') assertErrorReason(o.reason);
    else {
      assertOutcomeProvenance(o);
      assertOutcomeReason(o);
    }
    for (const x of offers) {
      if (cellKeyString(x.itinerary) !== key) throw new RowRejected('cell-mismatch', `itinerary ${x.itinerary.hash} is for ${cellKeyString(x.itinerary)}, not ${key}`);
      if (x.offer.itineraryHash !== x.itinerary.hash || x.offer.providerId !== providerId) throw new RowRejected('offer-mismatch', `offer does not belong to itinerary ${x.itinerary.hash} from ${providerId}`);
      const p = x.observation.provenance;
      if (p.providerId !== providerId || p.cabin !== o.cabin || p.pointOfSale !== o.pointOfSale || p.sourceKind !== o.sourceKind) throw new RowRejected('outcome-price-mismatch', `${key} observation provenance differs from its outcome (ruling 5)`);
    }
    if (offers.length > 0) {
      const cheapest = Math.min(...offers.map((x) => x.observation.pricePence));
      const oldest = offers.map((x) => x.observation.provenance.fetchedAt).sort()[0];
      if (o.minPence !== cheapest) throw new RowRejected('min-pence', `${key} min_pence ${String(o.minPence)} differs from the cheapest offer ${cheapest}`);
      if (o.fetchedAt !== oldest) throw new RowRejected('outcome-price-mismatch', `${key} outcome fetchedAt must be its oldest observation's (spec §2)`);
    }
    const itineraries = new ItinerariesRepo(this.db, this.clock, this.opts);
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      let best: { id: number; pence: number } | null = null;
      for (const x of offers) {
        itineraries.upsertItinerary(x.itinerary);
        const offerId = itineraries.upsertOffer(x.offer);
        const id = itineraries.insertObservation({ ...x.observation, offerId, executionId });
        if (best === null || x.observation.pricePence < best.pence) best = { id, pence: x.observation.pricePence };
      }
      const existing = this.current(executionId, w.cell, 'verification', providerId);
      const incoming: CellOutcome = { ...existing, ...o, minPence: best?.pence ?? null, attempts: existing.attempts, reusedFromExecutionId: null };
      storeOutcome(this.db, nextOutcome(existing, incoming, o.status === 'error'));
      if (best !== null) {
        this.db.raw
          .prepare(`UPDATE search_cells SET best_observation_id = @bestId WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND (best_observation_id IS NULL OR (SELECT price_pence FROM price_observations WHERE id = search_cells.best_observation_id) > @bestPence)`)
          .run({ ...keyParams(executionId, w.cell), bestId: best.id, bestPence: best.pence });
      }
      storeRollUps(this.db, executionId, 'verification', [w.cell]);
      if (o.status !== 'error') this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_executions SET auto_resume_count = 0 WHERE id = ?').run(executionId);
    });
  }
```

### Task 3.14: Reuse, supersede, calendar-basis reads and route history

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/repositories/calendarPrices.ts`, `src/repositories/cells.ts` (add `copyReused`)
- Create: `test/reuse.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `resolveWindow` (WP1), `readDefinition` (Task 3.7).
- Produces: `CalendarPricesRepo.reuseCandidates`, `supersedeReused`, `latestPerCell`, `previousFor`, `history`, `belowVerified`; `CellsRepo.copyReused`.

Rules: a reuse source is the newest `priced | empty | no_data` discovery outcome per cell from a non-child execution of the same definition, provider, cabin, point of sale and source kind, fetched after the cutoff. `copyReused` checks every copy before writing (prov-005): the copy's cell, the outcome's cell and the price's cell agree; the outcome is the same provider's discovery outcome and carries its provenance; a priced outcome has exactly one price, whose provider, `fetchedAt`, `pricePence` (= `minPence`), cabin, point of sale and source kind equal the outcome's (rule `reuse-source`). Route history (ruling 8) is one point per (provider, execution fetch): the cheapest fetched price across the pair's cells in that execution, carrying that cell's key and provenance; reused copies and child executions are excluded (a child fetches one cell, which is not a route minimum); with `definitionId`, only that definition's executions and only cells inside its space as resolved today.

```ts
import { type CellKey, type IsoDateTime, cellKeyString, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { CalendarPricesRepo } from '../src/repositories/calendarPrices.js';
import { CellsRepo } from '../src/repositories/cells.js';
import { DefinitionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/definitions.js';
import { ExecutionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/executions.js';
import type { ReusedCopy, TileWrite } from '../src/types.js';
import { T0, at, count, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, calendarPrice, cell, exampleDefinitionInput, newExecution, snapshotOf, withNights } from './helpers/factories.js';

const P = cell(); // priced
const E = cell(undefined, EWR); // empty
const N = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-15', '2027-03-29'); // no_data
const X = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-16', '2027-03-30'); // error
const R = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-17', '2027-03-31'); // left not_run
const ALL = [P, E, N, X, R];
const opts = { allowSynthetic: false };
type TileOutcome = TileWrite['outcomes'][number];
const out = (c: CellKey, status: TileOutcome['status'], fetchedAt: IsoDateTime, minPence: number | null = null): TileOutcome => ({
  ...c, status, reason: null, lastError: null, fetchedAt, minPence, cabin: 'economy', pointOfSale: 'unknown', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached',
});
const pricedTile = (cells: Array<[CellKey, number]>, fetchedAt: IsoDateTime): TileWrite => ({
  tileIndex: 0, cells: cells.map(([c]) => c), discards: 0,
  prices: cells.map(([c, pence]) => calendarPrice(c, { pricePence: pence, priceNativeMinor: pence, fetchedAt })), outcomes: cells.map(([c, pence]) => out(c, 'priced', fetchedAt, pence)),
});
const ask = (prices: CalendarPricesRepo, definitionId: string, cells: CellKey[], newerThan = '2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z') =>
  prices.reuseCandidates(definitionId, 'travelpayouts', 'economy', 'unknown', 'authorised-cached', cells, at(newerThan));

/** Execution a (completed) fetched P priced 40,000, E empty, N no_data at T0, and X failed. */
function world() {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const cells = new CellsRepo(db, clock, opts);
  const prices = new CalendarPricesRepo(db, clock, opts);
  const { definition, execution: a } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'completed' });
  cells.seed(a.id, ALL.map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
  cells.writeTile(a.id, 'travelpayouts', {
    tileIndex: 0, cells: [P, E, N], prices: [calendarPrice(P, { pricePence: 40_000, priceNativeMinor: 40_000 })], outcomes: [out(P, 'priced', T0, 40_000), out(E, 'empty', T0), out(N, 'no_data', T0)], discards: 0,
  });
  cells.writeTileError(a.id, 'travelpayouts', [X], 'upstream', null);
  return { db, clock, cells, prices, definition, a: a.id };
}
const find = (copies: ReusedCopy[], c: CellKey) => {
  const hit = copies.find((x) => cellKeyString(x.cell) === cellKeyString(c));
  if (hit === undefined) throw new Error(`no copy for ${cellKeyString(c)}`);
  return hit;
};

describe('reuse (spec §8.1 "reuse, per cell", §7 atomic unit "each reused cell")', () => {
  it('candidates: newest priced|empty|no_data per cell for the same provider, cabin, point of sale and source kind; never error or not_run', () => {
    const { prices, definition, a } = world();
    const copies = ask(prices, definition.id, ALL);
    expect(copies.map((c) => cellKeyString(c.cell))).toEqual([E, P, N].map(cellKeyString));
    expect(find(copies, P)).toMatchObject({ outcome: { executionId: a, status: 'priced', minPence: 40_000 }, price: { executionId: a, pricePence: 40_000 } });
    expect(find(copies, E).price).toBeNull();
    expect(ask(prices, definition.id, [P])).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('refuses on a cabin, point-of-sale or source-kind change, and on age (fetched_at must be newer than the cutoff)', () => {
    const { prices, definition } = world();
    const n = (cabin: 'economy' | 'business', pos: string, kind: 'authorised-cached' | 'scraped-aggregate', newerThan: string) => prices.reuseCandidates(definition.id, 'travelpayouts', cabin, pos, kind, ALL, at(newerThan)).length;
    expect(n('business', 'unknown', 'authorised-cached', '2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z')).toBe(0);
    expect(n('economy', 'GB', 'authorised-cached', '2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z')).toBe(0);
    expect(n('economy', 'unknown', 'scraped-aggregate', '2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z')).toBe(0);
    expect(n('economy', 'unknown', 'authorised-cached', T0)).toBe(0);
  });

  it('a newer execution wins; child executions are never reuse sources', () => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition } = world();
    clock.advance(3_600_000);
    const b = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'completed' }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(b, [withNights(P)], ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.writeTile(b, 'travelpayouts', pricedTile([[P, 39_000]], clock.now()));
    clock.advance(3_600_000);
    const child = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: P, status: 'completed' }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(child, [withNights(P)], ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.writeTile(child, 'travelpayouts', pricedTile([[P, 1]], clock.now()));
    expect(ask(prices, definition.id, [P])[0]?.outcome.executionId).toBe(b);
  });

  it('copyReused copies outcome and price with the original fetched_at and reused_from = the fetching execution, even from a reused copy', () => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition, a } = world();
    clock.advance(60_000);
    const c = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(c, ALL.map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.copyReused(c, 'travelpayouts', ask(prices, definition.id, ALL));
    expect(cells.outcomes(c, { cells: [P] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'priced', fetchedAt: T0, minPence: 40_000, cabin: 'economy', pointOfSale: 'unknown', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached', reusedFromExecutionId: a });
    expect(cells.outcomes(c, { cells: [X] })[0]?.status).toBe('not_run');
    expect(cells.rollUps(c).get(cellKeyString(P))?.discovery).toEqual({ status: 'priced', reason: null, minPence: 40_000 });
    expect(cells.buckets(c, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts')).toEqual({ priced: 1, empty: 1, no_data: 1, error: 0, skipped: 0, not_run: 2, reused: 3 });
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT fetched_at AS f, reused_from_execution_id AS r FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = ?').all(c)).toEqual([{ f: T0, r: a }]);
    expect(prices.fetchedBy(c)).toEqual([]); // a reused copy is not "fetched by" c
    new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock).setStatus(c, 'completed');
    clock.advance(60_000);
    const d = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(d, [withNights(P)], ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.copyReused(d, 'travelpayouts', ask(prices, definition.id, [P]));
    expect(cells.outcomes(d, { cells: [P] })[0]?.reusedFromExecutionId).toBe(a);
  });

  it('copyReused refuses error sources and another provider before writing', () => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition, a } = world();
    const c = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(c, ALL.map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
    const errorOutcome = cells.outcomes(a, { cells: [X] })[0];
    if (errorOutcome === undefined) throw new Error('fixture');
    expect(() => cells.copyReused(c, 'travelpayouts', [{ cell: X, outcome: errorOutcome, price: null }])).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'reuse-source' }));
    expect(() => cells.copyReused(c, 'searchapi', ask(prices, definition.id, ALL))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'reuse-source' }));
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(1);
  });

  it('copyReused refuses a copy the database does not hold: an amount the source never fetched, or a source from another definition (prov-r2-001)', () => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition } = world();
    const c = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(c, ALL.map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
    const real = find(ask(prices, definition.id, [P]), P);
    if (real.price === null) throw new Error('fixture: P is priced');
    const forged: ReusedCopy = { ...real, outcome: { ...real.outcome, minPence: 1 }, price: { ...real.price, pricePence: 1, priceNativeMinor: 1 } }; // internally consistent
    expect(() => cells.copyReused(c, 'travelpayouts', [forged])).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'reuse-source' }));
    const other = new DefinitionsRepo(db, clock).create(exampleDefinitionInput({ name: 'Other' }));
    const o = newExecution(db, clock, { definition: other, status: 'completed' }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(o, [withNights(P)], ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.writeTile(o, 'travelpayouts', pricedTile([[P, 30_000]], T0));
    const foreign = find(prices.reuseCandidates(other.id, 'travelpayouts', 'economy', 'unknown', 'authorised-cached', [P], at('2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z')), P);
    expect(() => cells.copyReused(c, 'travelpayouts', [foreign])).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'reuse-source' }));
    expect(cells.outcomes(c, { cells: [P] })[0]?.status).toBe('not_run');
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = ?').get(c)).toEqual({ n: 0 });
  });

  // prov-005: each mismatch is refused before any write.
  const mismatches: Array<[string, (p: ReusedCopy) => ReusedCopy]> = [
    ['copy cell ≠ outcome cell', (p) => ({ ...p, cell: E })],
    ['price cell ≠ outcome cell', (p) => ({ ...p, price: p.price === null ? null : { ...p.price, cell: E } })],
    ['price provider', (p) => ({ ...p, price: p.price === null ? null : { ...p.price, providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-aggregate' } })],
    ['price fetchedAt', (p) => ({ ...p, price: p.price === null ? null : { ...p.price, fetchedAt: at('2026-10-14T11:30:00.000Z') } })],
    ['price ≠ minPence', (p) => ({ ...p, price: p.price === null ? null : { ...p.price, pricePence: 39_999, priceNativeMinor: 39_999 } })],
    ['price cabin', (p) => ({ ...p, price: p.price === null ? null : { ...p.price, cabin: 'business' } })],
    ['price point of sale', (p) => ({ ...p, price: p.price === null ? null : { ...p.price, pointOfSale: 'GB' } })],
    ['outcome without provenance', (p) => ({ ...p, outcome: { ...p.outcome, sourceKind: null } })],
    ['priced without a price', (p) => ({ ...p, price: null })],
  ];
  it.each(mismatches)('copyReused refuses %s and writes nothing', (_name, mutate) => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition } = world();
    const c = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(c, ALL.map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
    expect(() => cells.copyReused(c, 'travelpayouts', [mutate(find(ask(prices, definition.id, [P]), P))])).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'reuse-source' }));
    expect(cells.outcomes(c, { cells: [P, E] }).map((o) => o.status)).toEqual(['not_run', 'not_run']);
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(1);
  });

  it('a later target that is no longer not_run rolls back the copies already written (test-011)', () => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition } = world();
    const c = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(c, ALL.map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.setOutcome(c, N, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts', { status: 'error', reason: 'network' }); // N sorts after E and P
    expect(() => cells.copyReused(c, 'travelpayouts', ask(prices, definition.id, ALL))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'reuse-target' }));
    expect(cells.outcomes(c, { cells: [E, P] }).map((o) => [o.status, o.reusedFromExecutionId])).toEqual([['not_run', null], ['not_run', null]]);
    expect(cells.rollUps(c).get(cellKeyString(P))?.discovery.status).toBe('not_run');
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(1);
  });

  it('supersedeReused deletes only the reused row', () => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition } = world();
    const c = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(c, ALL.map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.copyReused(c, 'travelpayouts', ask(prices, definition.id, [P]));
    prices.supersedeReused(c, 'travelpayouts', P);
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = ?').get(c)).toEqual({ n: 0 });
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe('calendar-basis reads (spec §6.9, §6.8) ignore reused copies (test-001)', () => {
  it('with P reused into b (not fetched), latestPerCell, belowVerified and previousFor still return a\'s row', () => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition, a } = world();
    clock.advance(3_600_000);
    const b = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(b, [withNights(P)], ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.copyReused(b, 'travelpayouts', ask(prices, definition.id, [P]));
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = ?').get(b)).toEqual({ n: 1 }); // the reused row exists
    const space = snapshotOf(definition).resolved;
    expect(prices.latestPerCell(definition.id, 'economy', space).map((r) => [r.executionId, r.reusedFromExecutionId])).toEqual([[a, null]]);
    expect(prices.belowVerified(definition.id, 'economy', space, 40_001).map((r) => r.executionId)).toEqual([a]);
    expect(prices.previousFor(definition.id, 'travelpayouts', 'economy', P, at('2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z'), at('2026-10-07T13:00:00.000Z'))?.executionId).toBe(a);
    expect(prices.history(P.origin, P.destination, 'economy', {})).toEqual([{ providerId: 'travelpayouts', points: [expect.objectContaining({ at: T0, pricePence: 40_000 })] }]);
  });

  it('with P fetched fresh in b, the newer row wins and cabin and space apply', () => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition, a } = world();
    clock.advance(3_600_000);
    const b = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(b, [withNights(P)], ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.writeTile(b, 'travelpayouts', pricedTile([[P, 41_000]], clock.now()));
    const space = snapshotOf(definition).resolved;
    const rows = prices.latestPerCell(definition.id, 'economy', space);
    expect(rows.map((r) => [r.executionId, r.pricePence, r.reusedFromExecutionId])).toEqual([[b, 41_000, null]]);
    expect(rows[0]?.provenance).toMatchObject({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', fetchedAt: '2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z', maxAgeHours: 48, fx: { converted: false } });
    expect(prices.latestPerCell(definition.id, 'economy', null)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(prices.latestPerCell(definition.id, 'business', space)).toEqual([]);
    expect(prices.latestPerCell(definition.id, 'economy', { ...space, minNights: 15 })).toEqual([]);
    expect(prices.belowVerified(definition.id, 'economy', space, 41_001).map((r) => r.pricePence)).toEqual([41_000]);
    expect(prices.belowVerified(definition.id, 'economy', space, 41_000)).toEqual([]);
    expect(prices.previousFor(definition.id, 'travelpayouts', 'economy', P, at('2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z'), at('2026-10-07T13:00:00.000Z'))).toMatchObject({ executionId: a, pricePence: 40_000 });
    expect(prices.previousFor(definition.id, 'travelpayouts', 'economy', P, at('2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z'), at('2026-10-14T12:00:00.001Z'))).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('route history (ruling 8, po-001): one point per provider and execution fetch', () => {
  it('two cells, two fetches: each point is that fetch\'s cheapest cell, with its full cell, execution and provenance; reused copies and children add nothing', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const cells = new CellsRepo(db, clock, opts);
    const prices = new CalendarPricesRepo(db, clock, opts);
    const { definition, execution: a } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'completed' });
    const Q = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-21', '2027-04-04');
    const late = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-05-01', '2027-05-15'); // outside the definition's window (ends 2027-04-20)
    cells.seed(a.id, [P, Q, late].map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.writeTile(a.id, 'travelpayouts', pricedTile([[P, 40_000], [Q, 38_000], [late, 20_000]], T0));
    clock.advance(3_600_000);
    const b = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'completed' }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(b, [P, Q].map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.writeTile(b, 'travelpayouts', pricedTile([[P, 41_000], [Q, 42_000]], clock.now()));
    clock.advance(3_600_000);
    const c = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id; // reuses only
    cells.seed(c, [withNights(P)], ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.copyReused(c, 'travelpayouts', ask(prices, definition.id, [P], '2026-10-14T00:00:00.000Z'));
    const child = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: Q }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(child, [withNights(Q)], ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.writeTile(child, 'travelpayouts', pricedTile([[Q, 30_000]], clock.now()));
    const other = new DefinitionsRepo(db, clock).create(exampleDefinitionInput({ name: 'Other' }));
    const o = newExecution(db, clock, { definition: other, status: 'completed' }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(o, [withNights(P)], ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.writeTile(o, 'travelpayouts', pricedTile([[P, 35_000]], clock.now()));

    const all = prices.history(P.origin, P.destination, 'economy');
    expect(all.map((s) => [s.providerId, s.points.map((p) => [p.at, p.pricePence, p.cell.departureDate, p.cell.nights, p.executionId])])).toEqual([
      ['travelpayouts', [[T0, 20_000, '2027-05-01', 14, a.id], ['2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z', 41_000, '2027-03-14', 14, b], ['2026-10-14T14:00:00.000Z', 35_000, '2027-03-14', 14, o]]],
    ]);
    const scoped = prices.history(P.origin, P.destination, 'economy', { definitionId: definition.id });
    expect(scoped[0]?.points.map((p) => [p.at, p.pricePence, cellKeyString(p.cell), p.executionId])).toEqual([[T0, 38_000, cellKeyString(Q), a.id], ['2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z', 41_000, cellKeyString(P), b]]);
    expect(scoped[0]?.points[0]?.provenance).toMatchObject({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', fetchedAt: T0, cabin: 'economy' });
    expect(prices.history(P.origin, P.destination, 'business', {})).toEqual([]);
    expect(() => prices.history(P.origin, P.destination, 'economy', { definitionId: 'missing' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'missing' }));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Add to `packages/db/src/repositories/calendarPrices.ts` (imports: `Cabin`, `Cell`, `CellKey`, `HistoryPoint`, `Iata`, `IsoDateTime`, `PointOfSale`, `ProviderId`, `ResolvedSpace`, `SourceKind`, `cellKeyString`, `resolveWindow` from `@gmt/core`; `type OutcomeDbRow, toOutcome` from `../internal/outcomeRow.js`; `type DefinitionDbRow, readDefinition` from `../internal/definitionRow.js`; `RowRejected, assertIsoDateTime` from `../internal/validate.js`; `RowCorrupt` from `../internal/read.js`; `ReusedCopy` from `../types.js`):

```ts
/**
 * Spec §8.1 reuse source: per cell, the newest priced|empty|no_data discovery outcome for the same definition,
 * provider, cabin, point of sale and source kind, from a non-child execution, whose original `fetched_at` is newer
 * than the cutoff (the engine passes now − 0.9 × discoveryRefreshHours).
 */
const REUSE_CANDIDATES_SQL = `
WITH ranked AS (
  SELECT o.*, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY o.origin, o.destination, o.departure_date, o.return_date ORDER BY o.fetched_at DESC, e.created_at DESC, e.rowid DESC) AS rn
  FROM cell_outcomes o JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = o.execution_id
  WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId AND e.is_child = 0 AND o.stage = 'discovery' AND o.provider_id = @providerId
    AND o.status IN ('priced', 'empty', 'no_data') AND o.cabin = @cabin AND o.point_of_sale = @pointOfSale AND o.source_kind = @sourceKind AND o.fetched_at > @newerThan
)
SELECT * FROM ranked WHERE rn = 1 ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date`;

const PRICE_FOR_OUTCOME_SQL = `SELECT * FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND provider_id = @providerId
  AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate`;

/** Nights of a stored cell, computed in SQL the same way `withNights` does (UTC calendar days). */
const NIGHTS = 'CAST(julianday(p.return_date) - julianday(p.departure_date) AS INTEGER)';

/** Spec §6.9 calendar basis: latest fetched per (provider, cell) in the cabin, reused copies ignored, inside the space when given. */
const LATEST_PER_CELL_SQL = `
WITH ranked AS (
  SELECT p.*, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.provider_id, p.origin, p.destination, p.departure_date, p.return_date ORDER BY p.fetched_at DESC, p.id DESC) AS rn
  FROM calendar_prices p JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = p.execution_id
  WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId AND p.cabin = @cabin AND p.reused_from_execution_id IS NULL
    AND (@spaced = 0 OR (p.origin IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@origins)) AND p.destination IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@destinations))
         AND p.departure_date BETWEEN @from AND @to AND ${NIGHTS} BETWEEN @minNights AND @maxNights))
)
SELECT * FROM ranked WHERE rn = 1 ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, provider_id`;

/**
 * Ruling 8: per (provider, execution) the cheapest fetched price on the pair (ties: earliest fetched, lowest id).
 * Children are excluded: a child fetches one cell, which is not a route minimum.
 */
const HISTORY_SQL = `
WITH ranked AS (
  SELECT p.*, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.provider_id, p.execution_id ORDER BY p.price_pence, p.fetched_at, p.id) AS rn
  FROM calendar_prices p JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = p.execution_id
  WHERE p.origin = @origin AND p.destination = @destination AND p.cabin = @cabin AND p.reused_from_execution_id IS NULL AND e.is_child = 0
    AND (@definitionId IS NULL OR (e.definition_id = @definitionId AND p.departure_date BETWEEN @from AND @to AND ${NIGHTS} BETWEEN @minNights AND @maxNights))
)
SELECT ranked.*, CAST(julianday(return_date) - julianday(departure_date) AS INTEGER) AS nights FROM ranked WHERE rn = 1 ORDER BY provider_id, fetched_at, id`;

const spaceParams = (space: ResolvedSpace | null) => ({
  spaced: space === null ? 0 : 1, origins: JSON.stringify(space?.origins ?? []), destinations: JSON.stringify(space?.destinations ?? []),
  from: space?.departureFrom ?? '', to: space?.departureTo ?? '', minNights: space?.minNights ?? 0, maxNights: space?.maxNights ?? 0,
});
```

```ts
  reuseCandidates(definitionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, cabin: Cabin, pointOfSale: PointOfSale, sourceKind: SourceKind, cells: CellKey[], newerThan: IsoDateTime): ReusedCopy[] {
    assertIsoDateTime('newerThan', newerThan);
    const wanted = new Set(cells.map(cellKeyString));
    const priceStmt = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CalendarPriceDbRow>(PRICE_FOR_OUTCOME_SQL);
    const out: ReusedCopy[] = [];
    for (const r of this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(REUSE_CANDIDATES_SQL).all({ definitionId, providerId, cabin, pointOfSale, sourceKind, newerThan })) {
      const outcome = toOutcome(r);
      const cell: CellKey = { origin: outcome.origin, destination: outcome.destination, departureDate: outcome.departureDate, returnDate: outcome.returnDate };
      if (!wanted.has(cellKeyString(cell))) continue;
      let price: CalendarPriceRow | null = null;
      if (outcome.status === 'priced') {
        const p = priceStmt.get({ executionId: outcome.executionId, providerId, ...cell });
        // why: writeTile enforces a price for every priced outcome; a missing one is corruption, not a reusable cell.
        if (p === undefined) throw new RowCorrupt('calendar_prices', `priced outcome ${cellKeyString(cell)} in ${outcome.executionId} has no calendar price`);
        price = toCalendarPriceRow(p);
      }
      out.push({ cell, outcome, price });
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** Spec §7 "a fresh price superseding a reused row deletes that row" (writeTile does it inline). Fresh rows are never deleted. */
  supersedeReused(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, cell: CellKey): void {
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(`DELETE FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND provider_id = @providerId AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate AND reused_from_execution_id IS NOT NULL`)
      .run({ executionId, providerId, ...cell });
  }

  latestPerCell(definitionId: string, cabin: Cabin, space: ResolvedSpace | null): CalendarPriceRow[] {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CalendarPriceDbRow>(LATEST_PER_CELL_SQL).all({ definitionId, cabin, ...spaceParams(space) }).map(toCalendarPriceRow);
  }

  /** Calendar-gap source (spec §10): latest calendar prices strictly below the cheapest verified price. */
  belowVerified(definitionId: string, cabin: Cabin, space: ResolvedSpace, thresholdPence: number): CalendarPriceRow[] {
    return this.latestPerCell(definitionId, cabin, space).filter((r) => r.pricePence < thresholdPence);
  }

  /** Spec §6.8 calendar drops: the newest fetched (not reused) price for the same (provider, cabin, cell) with fetched_at in [notOlderThan, before). */
  previousFor(definitionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, cabin: Cabin, cell: CellKey, before: IsoDateTime, notOlderThan: IsoDateTime): CalendarPriceRow | null {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CalendarPriceDbRow>(
        `SELECT p.* FROM calendar_prices p JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = p.execution_id
         WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId AND p.provider_id = @providerId AND p.cabin = @cabin AND p.reused_from_execution_id IS NULL
           AND p.origin = @origin AND p.destination = @destination AND p.departure_date = @departureDate AND p.return_date = @returnDate
           AND p.fetched_at < @before AND p.fetched_at >= @notOlderThan
         ORDER BY p.fetched_at DESC, p.id DESC LIMIT 1`,
      )
      .get({ definitionId, providerId, cabin, ...cell, before, notOlderThan });
    return r === undefined ? null : toCalendarPriceRow(r);
  }

  /**
   * Route calendar history (ruling 8): per provider, one point per execution fetch, the cheapest cell's price, key and
   * provenance; oldest first. With `definitionId`: that definition's executions, cells inside its space resolved today.
   */
  history(origin: Iata, destination: Iata, cabin: Cabin, opts: { definitionId?: string } = {}): Array<{ providerId: ProviderId; points: Array<HistoryPoint & { cell: Cell; executionId: string }> }> {
    let scope: { definitionId: string | null; from: string; to: string; minNights: number; maxNights: number } = { definitionId: null, from: '', to: '', minNights: 0, maxNights: 0 };
    if (opts.definitionId !== undefined) {
      const d = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], DefinitionDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_definitions WHERE id = ?').get(opts.definitionId);
      if (d === undefined) throw new RowRejected('missing', `definition ${opts.definitionId} does not exist`);
      const def = readDefinition(d);
      const w = resolveWindow(def.window, this.clock.todayLondon());
      scope = { definitionId: def.id, from: w.departureFrom, to: w.departureTo, minNights: def.minNights, maxNights: def.maxNights };
    }
    const out = new Map<ProviderId, Array<HistoryPoint & { cell: Cell; executionId: string }>>();
    for (const r of this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CalendarPriceDbRow & { nights: number }>(HISTORY_SQL).all({ origin, destination, cabin, ...scope })) {
      const row = toCalendarPriceRow(r);
      const points = out.get(row.providerId) ?? [];
      // why: the plan contract and core's routeHistoryResponseSchema carry the full Cell and the fetching execution (arch-r2-004).
      points.push({ at: row.fetchedAt, pricePence: row.pricePence, provenance: row.provenance, cell: { ...row.cell, nights: r.nights }, executionId: row.executionId });
      out.set(row.providerId, points);
    }
    return [...out.entries()].map(([providerId, points]) => ({ providerId, points }));
  }
```

Add to `packages/db/src/repositories/cells.ts` (imports: `CalendarPriceRow`, `ReusedCopy` from `../types.js`; `type CalendarPriceDbRow, toCalendarPriceRow` added to the `../internal/priceRow.js` import):

```ts
/** prov-005: every field a reused copy carries must agree before anything is written (rule reuse-source). */
function assertReusable(providerId: ProviderId, { cell, outcome, price }: ReusedCopy): void {
  const key = cellKeyString(cell);
  const refuse = (why: string) => new RowRejected('reuse-source', `${key}: ${why} (spec §8.1)`);
  if (outcome.status !== 'priced' && outcome.status !== 'empty' && outcome.status !== 'no_data') throw refuse(`only priced, empty or no_data outcomes are reused, got ${outcome.status}`);
  if (outcome.providerId !== providerId || outcome.stage !== 'discovery') throw refuse(`a ${outcome.providerId} ${outcome.stage} outcome cannot be reused for ${providerId} discovery`);
  if (cellKeyString(outcome) !== key) throw refuse(`the outcome is for ${cellKeyString(outcome)}`);
  if (outcome.fetchedAt === null || outcome.cabin === null || outcome.pointOfSale === null || outcome.sourceKind === null) throw refuse('the outcome carries no provenance');
  if ((outcome.status === 'priced') !== (price !== null)) throw refuse(`a ${outcome.status} outcome with${price === null ? 'out' : ''} a price`);
  if (price === null) return;
  if (cellKeyString(price.cell) !== key) throw refuse(`the price is for ${cellKeyString(price.cell)}`);
  if (price.providerId !== providerId) throw refuse(`the price is from ${price.providerId}`);
  if (price.fetchedAt !== outcome.fetchedAt || price.pricePence !== outcome.minPence) throw refuse('the price and the outcome disagree on fetched_at or amount');
  if (price.cabin !== outcome.cabin || price.pointOfSale !== outcome.pointOfSale || price.sourceKind !== outcome.sourceKind) throw refuse('the price and the outcome disagree on cabin, point of sale or source kind');
}
```

```ts
  /**
   * Spec §7 atomic unit "each reused cell's outcome + copied prices + roll-up" (one transaction for the batch).
   * Copies keep the original fetched_at and provenance; reused_from_execution_id is always the execution that
   * fetched the price, even when the source is itself a reused copy.
   */
  copyReused(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, copies: ReusedCopy[]): void {
    for (const copy of copies) {
      assertReusable(providerId, copy);
      if (copy.price !== null) validateCalendarPrice(copy.price, this.opts.allowSynthetic, false);
    }
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const { cell, outcome, price } of copies) {
        const key = { ...keyParams(executionId, cell), stage: 'discovery', providerId };
        const target = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(SELECT_OUTCOME_SQL).get(key);
        if (target?.status !== 'not_run') throw new RowRejected('reuse-target', `${cellKeyString(cell)} in ${executionId} is ${target?.status ?? 'missing'}, not not_run`);
        this.assertSourceStored(executionId, providerId, { cell, outcome, price });
        const reusedFrom = outcome.reusedFromExecutionId ?? outcome.executionId;
        storeOutcome(this.db, { ...outcome, ...keyParams(executionId, cell), executionId, stage: 'discovery', providerId, attempts: 0, lastError: null, reusedFromExecutionId: reusedFrom });
        if (price !== null) insertCalendarPrice(this.db, executionId, { ...price, reusedFromExecutionId: reusedFrom });
      }
      storeRollUps(this.db, executionId, 'discovery', copies.map((c) => c.cell));
    });
  }

  /**
   * prov-r2-001: the copy must be what the database holds. The source outcome is re-read by its keys and the price by
   * its id; both must equal the values supplied, and the source must be a non-child execution of the target's
   * definition (spec §8.1). This is the one path that writes a price without a provider call, so it trusts no caller.
   */
  private assertSourceStored(targetExecutionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, { cell, outcome, price }: ReusedCopy): void {
    const refuse = (why: string) => new RowRejected('reuse-source', `${cellKeyString(cell)}: ${why} (spec §8.1)`);
    const src = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow & { definition_id: string; is_child: number }>(
        `SELECT o.*, e.definition_id, e.is_child FROM cell_outcomes o JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = o.execution_id
         WHERE o.execution_id = @executionId AND o.origin = @origin AND o.destination = @destination AND o.departure_date = @departureDate AND o.return_date = @returnDate
           AND o.stage = 'discovery' AND o.provider_id = @providerId`,
      )
      .get({ ...keyParams(outcome.executionId, cell), providerId });
    if (src === undefined) throw refuse(`no stored outcome in ${outcome.executionId}`);
    const target = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { definition_id: string }>('SELECT definition_id FROM search_executions WHERE id = ?').get(targetExecutionId);
    if (src.is_child !== 0 || target === undefined || src.definition_id !== target.definition_id) throw refuse('the source is not a parent execution of the same definition');
    const stored = toOutcome(src);
    const same = (a: CellOutcome) => [a.status, a.reason, a.fetchedAt, a.minPence, a.cabin, a.pointOfSale, a.sourceKind, a.reusedFromExecutionId].join('|');
    if (same(stored) !== same(outcome)) throw refuse('the supplied outcome differs from the stored one');
    if (price === null) return;
    const p = this.db.raw.prepare<[number], CalendarPriceDbRow>('SELECT * FROM calendar_prices WHERE id = ?').get(price.id);
    if (p === undefined) throw refuse(`calendar price ${price.id} does not exist`);
    const held = toCalendarPriceRow(p);
    const priceKey = (x: CalendarPriceRow) => [x.executionId, x.providerId, cellKeyString(x.cell), x.pricePence, x.priceNativeMinor, x.currencyNative, x.fetchedAt, x.cabin, x.pointOfSale, x.sourceKind, x.reusedFromExecutionId].join('|');
    if (held.executionId !== outcome.executionId || priceKey(held) !== priceKey(price)) throw refuse('the supplied price differs from the stored one');
  }
```

### Task 3.15: The synthetic gate and fixture ⇔ synthetic on every write path

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/schema/cellOutcomes.ts` (CHECK `cell_outcomes_provider_kind_ck`), `src/repositories/cells.ts`, `src/repositories/itineraries.ts`; regenerate the migration.
- Create: `test/syntheticGate.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: no new methods; every write that carries a provider refuses `fixture` unless `allowSynthetic` (rule `synthetic-gate`), and every write that carries a source kind enforces `provider_id = 'fixture'` ⇔ `source_kind = 'synthetic'` (rule `provider-kind`) (ruling 1, spec §5.5).

The paths: `CellsRepo.seed`, `seedVerification`, `setOutcome`, `setOutcomesForProvider`, `markNoData`, `writeTileError` and `ItinerariesRepo.upsertOffer` carry a provider and no kind (gate only); `writeTile`, `writeVerifiedCell` and `copyReused` carry both; `CalendarPricesRepo.insert` and `ItinerariesRepo.insertObservation` were gated in Tasks 3.10 and 3.13. `upsertItinerary` carries neither (an itinerary is provider-independent, spec §6.4); the itineraries a fixture run writes go through `writeVerifiedCell`, which is gated. In SQL, `cell_outcomes` gains `source_kind IS NULL OR (provider_id = 'fixture') = (source_kind = 'synthetic')`.

```ts
import { type CellKey, type SourceKind, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { CellsRepo } from '../src/repositories/cells.js';
import { ExecutionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/executions.js';
import { ItinerariesRepo } from '../src/repositories/itineraries.js';
import type { ReusedCopy, TileWrite, VerifiedCellWrite } from '../src/types.js';
import { T0, count, freshDb, sqliteCode } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, cell, itinerary, newExecution, observationWrite, withNights } from './helpers/factories.js';

const A = cell();
const B = cell(undefined, EWR);

/** One database, two views of it: the gate open (tests, smoke) and closed (production). */
function world() {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
  const open = new CellsRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic: true });
  const closed = new CellsRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic: false });
  open.seed(execution.id, [A, B].map(withNights), ['fixture', 'travelpayouts']);

}
type World = ReturnType<typeof world>;

  tileIndex: 0, cells: [c], prices: [], discards: 0,

});
const verified = (c: CellKey, sourceKind: SourceKind): VerifiedCellWrite => ({

    ['seedVerification', (w, r) => w[r].seedVerification(w.executionId, [{ cell: A, reason: 'top_n' }], [A], ['fixture']), (w) => count(w.db, 'cell_outcomes')],
    ['setOutcome', (w, r) => w[r].setOutcome(w.executionId, A, 'discovery', 'fixture', { status: 'error', reason: 'upstream' }), (w) => w.open.outcomes(w.executionId, { providerId: 'fixture' }).filter((o) => o.status === 'error').length],
    ['setOutcomesForProvider', (w, r) => w[r].setOutcomesForProvider(w.executionId, 'discovery', 'fixture', ['not_run'], { status: 'skipped', reason: 'provider_disabled' }), (w) => w.open.outcomes(w.executionId, { providerId: 'fixture' }).filter((o) => o.status === 'skipped').length],
    ['markNoData', (w, r) => w[r].markNoData(w.executionId, 'discovery', 'fixture', 'cabin-not-offered'), (w) => w.open.outcomes(w.executionId, { providerId: 'fixture' }).filter((o) => o.status === 'no_data').length],
    ['writeTileError', (w, r) => w[r].writeTileError(w.executionId, 'fixture', [A], 'upstream', null), (w) => w.open.outcomes(w.executionId, { providerId: 'fixture' }).filter((o) => o.status === 'error').length],
    ['upsertOffer', (w, r) => { w.itOpen.upsertItinerary(itinerary()); return (r === 'open' ? w.itOpen : w.itClosed).upsertOffer({ itineraryHash: 'a'.repeat(64), providerId: 'fixture', seller: 'Fixture', cabin: 'economy' }); }, (w) => count(w.db, 'fare_offers')],

    const w = world();
    w.open.seedVerification(w.executionId, [{ cell: A, reason: 'top_n' }], [A], ['fixture']);
    const fixtureObservation = observationWrite({ provenance: { providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'synthetic', fetchedAt: T0, maxAgeHours: 0, fx: { converted: false }, pointOfSale: 'unknown', cabin: 'economy' } });
    const write: VerifiedCellWrite = {
      cell: A, outcome: { ...verified(A, 'synthetic').outcome, status: 'priced', minPence: 41_200 },
      itineraries: [{ itinerary: itinerary(), offers: [{ offer: { itineraryHash: 'a'.repeat(64), providerId: 'fixture', seller: 'Fixture', cabin: 'economy' }, observation: fixtureObservation }] }],
    };
    expect(() => w.closed.writeVerifiedCell(w.executionId, 'fixture', write)).toThrow(gate);
    expect([count(w.db, 'itineraries'), count(w.db, 'fare_offers'), count(w.db, 'price_observations')]).toEqual([0, 0, 0]);
    w.open.writeVerifiedCell(w.executionId, 'fixture', write);
    expect(count(w.db, 'price_observations')).toBe(1);
  });

  it('copyReused', () => {
    const w = world();
    w.open.writeTile(w.executionId, 'fixture', tile(A, 'synthetic'));
    const source = w.open.outcomes(w.executionId, { cells: [A], providerId: 'fixture' })[0];
    if (source === undefined) throw new Error('fixture outcome missing');
    new ExecutionsRepo(w.db, w.clock).setStatus(w.executionId, 'completed'); // the source is a finished parent of the same definition (prov-r2-001)
    const target = newExecution(w.db, w.clock, { definition: w.definition }).execution.id;
    w.open.seed(target, [withNights(A)], ['fixture', 'travelpayouts']);
    const copy: ReusedCopy = { cell: A, outcome: source, price: null };
    expect(() => w.closed.copyReused(target, 'fixture', [copy])).toThrow(gate);
    expect(() => w.open.copyReused(target, 'fixture', [{ ...copy, outcome: { ...source, sourceKind: 'authorised-cached' } }])).toThrow(kind);
    expect(() => w.open.copyReused(target, 'travelpayouts', [{ ...copy, outcome: { ...source, providerId: 'travelpayouts' } }])).toThrow(kind);
    w.open.copyReused(target, 'fixture', [copy]);
    expect(w.open.outcomes(target, { providerId: 'fixture' })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'empty', sourceKind: 'synthetic', reusedFromExecutionId: w.executionId });
  });

  it('cell_outcomes enforces fixture ⇔ synthetic in SQL, both directions', () => {
    const w = world();
    const raw = (providerId: string, sourceKind: string) => () =>
      w.db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = 'empty', fetched_at = ?, cabin = 'economy', point_of_sale = 'unknown', source_kind = ? WHERE provider_id = ? AND departure_date = '2027-03-14' AND destination = 'JFK'`).run(T0, sourceKind, providerId);
    expect(sqliteCode(raw('fixture', 'authorised-cached'))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(raw('travelpayouts', 'synthetic'))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(raw('fixture', 'synthetic')).not.toThrow();
  });
});
```

### Task 3.16: Alerts (soft-deleted rules), operator notifications, route price history

**Files:**
- Create: `src/schema/alertRules.ts`, `src/schema/alertEvents.ts`, `src/schema/operatorNotifications.ts`, `src/schema/routePriceHistory.ts`, `src/repositories/alerts.ts`, `src/repositories/notifications.ts`, `src/repositories/routePriceHistory.ts`, `test/alerts.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/schema/index.ts`; regenerate the migration.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `AlertsRepo(db, clock)` (with `get`, `softDelete`, `priorFirings`), `NotificationsRepo(db, clock)` (keyed by definition), `RoutePriceHistoryRepo(db, opts)` (`series` filtered by cabin, ruling 9).

Rules: a rule is soft-deleted (`deleted_at`, ruling 12): it leaves `rules()` and `get()`, cannot be updated, and its events stay readable; there is no cascade. The `providers` table is not created (ruling 11): provider metadata comes from config. `RouteSeriesPoint.sourceKind` has no column: route history comes only from a provider's itinerary search, so it follows the provider (`searchapi` → `scraped-search`, `fixture` → `synthetic`, `travelpayouts` → `authorised-cached`), and the provider ⇔ kind rule and the gate are enforced on write.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';

import { RoutePriceHistoryRepo } from '../src/repositories/routePriceHistory.js';
```
